Abstract: We prove that for every integer $n\geq 2$ and $8π(n-1)<ρ<8πn$, the singular Liouville equation $Δu+\e^u=ρδ_0$ on a rectangular torus $E_{\mathrm{i}b}=\mathbb{C}/(\mathbb Z+\mathrm{i} b\mathbb Z)$ has exactly $n$ solutions, which are all axisymmetric. Together with previous results by Chen-Lin and Lin-Wang, this yields that \begin{itemize} \item $E_{\mathrm{i} b}$ admits no spherical metrics with a conical singularity of angle $2π\vartheta$ as long as $\vartheta$ is a positive odd integer. \item For every integer $n\geq 1$, $E_{\mathrm{i} b}$ admits exactly $n$ spherical metrics with a conical singularity of angle $2π\vartheta$ for each $\vartheta\in (2n-1, 2n+1)$. \end{itemize} The basic idea is to prove that the linearized equation has only trivial solutions in the space of axisymmetric functions. The previous method of analysing nodal domains via Bol's isoperimetric inequality only works for $ρ\leq 8π$. We develop a unified approach for all $ρ\in (0,+\infty)\setminus 8π\mathbb{N}_{\geq 1}$ by exploring the deep connection with the monodromy of the classical Lamé equation.

</details>


### [169] [Sharp Dispersive and Strichartz Estimates for the Neumann Cylindrical Wave Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30208)
*Len Meas*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对三维圆柱凸域内带Neumann边界条件的线性波动方程，建立了局部时间色散估计和锐利Strichartz估计。利用基于Airy函数导数的零点及定制的Airy–Poisson求和公式进行显式谱分析，刻画了多重反射波和焦散的微局域行为。最终将这些无损导数的锐利Strichartz估计应用于能量临界五次非线性波动方程（NLW），证明了其在Neumann边界条件下的局部适定性和小数据全局适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆柱凸域的几何特性要求非负曲率半径沿轴向方向消失，这使得传统的Strichartz估计方法失效或产生导数损失。需要针对这类特殊几何（曲率退化域）发展新的分析工具，以获取无导数损失的锐利Strichartz估计，从而为解决能量临界非线性波动方程的适定性问题奠定基础。

Method: 首先利用基于Airy函数导数零点的显式谱分析来处理圆柱域中Laplace算子的谱结构；然后构造定制的Airy–Poisson求和公式，捕捉多重反射波与焦散的微局域行为；在此基础上推导局部时间色散估计和锐利Strichartz估计；最后通过无导数损失的Strichartz估计，结合标准的非线性波动方程局部理论，证明能量临界五次NLW的局部和全局适定性。

Result: 成功建立了圆柱凸域内带Neumann边界条件的波动方程的局部时间色散估计和锐利Strichartz估计（无导数损失），并以此证明了能量临界五次非线性波动方程在Neumann边界条件下的局部适定性和小数据全局适定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为曲率退化域（圆柱形凸域）上的波动方程Strichartz估计提供了新方法，填补了此类几何下Neumann边界条件问题的空白，并将锐利估计成功应用于能量临界非线性问题的适定性分析，具有重要的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: We establish local-in-time dispersive and sharp Strichartz estimates for the linear wave equation with Neumann boundary conditions inside a cylindrical convex domain $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^3$ featuring a non-empty smooth boundary $\partialΩ$. The cylindrical geometry dictates that the nonnegative radius of curvature vanishes along the axial direction. By utilizing an explicit spectral analysis based on the zeros of the Airy function derivative, alongside a tailored Airy--Poisson summation formula, we capture the microlocal behavior of multi-reflected waves and caustics. Finally, we apply these sharp Strichartz estimates without loss of derivatives to prove local well-posedness and small-data global well-posedness for the energy-critical quintic nonlinear wave equation (NLW) subject to Neumann boundary conditions.

</details>


### [170] [Parameter-uniform Robin uniqueness on large dilations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30211)
*Sophie Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了大膨胀（large-dilation）唯一性在Robin边界条件下对可能无界的均匀C^{2,γ}区域成立，且膨胀阈值不依赖于Robin系数α（包括Dirichlet和Neumann端点情形），并对有界C^{4,γ}区域建立了谱底收敛率O(κ^{-1/2})及边界层的平均曲率一阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berestycki和Graham在固定α时证明了大膨胀唯一性，并推测膨胀阈值不应依赖α。本文旨在严格验证这一猜想，将结果推广到包含Dirichlet和Neumann端点的所有情形，并适用于可能无界的均匀C^{2,γ}区域。

Method: 方法包含三个关键部分：(1) 通过紧致化边界参数证明半空间线性化问题在边界参数上保持共同的正谱间隙；(2) 在Dirichlet端点处处理非线性紧致性困难——由于Robin系数发散，在倒数尺度上做重缩放使极限方程变为调和方程，并利用Liouville引理排除任何可能残留的迹；(3) 将端点紧致性、半空间谱间隙与局部化技巧结合得到均匀的大膨胀唯一性。对有界区域还通过谱底收敛分析和边界层展开（含平均曲率修正）获得定量误差估计。

Result: 证实了膨胀阈值不依赖于Robin系数α，包括Dirichlet和Neumann端点；对∂Ω≠∅的情况得到谱底到半空间值的收敛率O(κ^{-1/2})；对有界C^{4,γ}区域，边界层具有一阶平均曲率修正，余项为O(κ^{-1-γ}+κ^{-2})。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Berestycki-Graham提出的问题，将大膨胀唯一性从固定α推广到α无依赖的全谱情形（含端点），并获得了谱收敛率与边界层展开的定量结果，为理解Robin问题在区域膨胀下的渐近行为提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: Berestycki and Graham proved large-dilation uniqueness for bounded positive solutions of $-Δu=f(u)$ in $κΩ$, with $u+α\partial_νu=0$ on $\partial(κΩ)$, when $α$ is fixed, and remarked that the dilation threshold should not depend on $α$. We show that it does not, including at the Dirichlet and Neumann endpoints, for possibly unbounded uniformly $C^{2,γ}$ domains. The half-space linearizations retain a common positive spectral gap across the compactified boundary parameter. The remaining endpoint difficulty is nonlinear compactness at the Dirichlet end, where the Robin coefficient diverges. Rescaling at its reciprocal scale makes the limiting equation harmonic. Any trace that persisted in this limit would give a bounded harmonic half-space solution of $\partial_νv+v=0$, which a Liouville lemma rules out. Combining this endpoint compactness with the half-space gap and localization gives uniform large-dilation uniqueness. When $\partialΩ\neq\varnothing$, we further obtain convergence of the spectral bottom to its half-space value with error $O(κ^{-1/2})$. For bounded $C^{4,γ}$ domains the boundary layer also has a first mean-curvature correction, with remainder $O(κ^{-1-γ}+κ^{-2})$ on each fixed boundary strip.

</details>


### [171] [Strong Unique Continuation for Fractional Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30232)
*Harsh Prasad*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了分数阶Schrödinger方程(-Δ)^s u=Vu在临界尺度势V∈L^{n/2s}_loc下对所有0<s<1成立的强唯一延拓性质，这是经典Jerison-Kenig结果的非局部对应版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Jerison-Kenig定理给出了局部Schrödinger算子(-Δ+V)在临界势下满足强唯一延拓性质，但该结果在非局部（分数阶）算子情形下的推广长期是一个开放问题。分数阶Schrödinger方程近年来在数学物理中具有广泛的应用，因此建立其强唯一延拓性质具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 本文采用非局部分析的技术手段（如Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓、Hardy不等式、Carleman估计等）来证明分数阶Laplacian情形下的强唯一延拓性质，处理了所有0<s<1的分数阶参数以及临界尺度势V∈L^{n/2s}_loc。

Result: 成功证明了分数阶Schrödinger方程(-Δ)^s u=Vu在所有0<s<1区间内、容许临界可积性势V∈L^{n/2s}_loc的强唯一延拓性质，推广了经典Jerison-Kenig定理至非局部框架。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了非局部算子强唯一延拓理论的重要空白，其意义在于：它证实了分数阶Schrödinger算子在临界势尺度下仍然保持乌尼延拓性质，与局部情形在最优性上保持一致，为非局部椭圆方程解的定性理论奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We prove the strong unique continuation property for the fractional Schrödinger equation \[
  (-Δ)^s u=Vu \] with scaling-critical potentials
  $ V\in L^{\frac{n}{2s}}_{\mathrm{loc}}$ for all $0<s<1$. Our result constitutes the nonlocal counterpart to the classical Jerison--Kenig result for Schrödinger operators.

</details>


### [172] [On smooth self similar solution to compressible Euler equation with physical vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30443)
*Jihoi Kim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究维度 d>1 中具有空间无穷远处密度衰减的等熵欧拉方程，在不同于 Merle 等人（2022）工作的参数范围内，证明存在一类"全球壳类"（holosphere-like）自相似解——这些解在越过声速线时保持光滑，但在某个球面上密度趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guderley 的开创性工作引入了球对称自相似解的相图，而 Merle 等人（2022）在特定参数范围内证明了通过声速线的光滑衰减解的存在性。本文的动机是在不同的参数范围内探索新的解类型，特别是具有球面密度消失现象的 holosphere 解，以丰富对等熵欧拉方程自相似解结构的理解。

Method: 对球对称自相似解的控制非线性 ODE 进行相图分析。研究在不同于 Merle 等人所用参数的另一参数范围内，构造在声速线处光滑但密度在某个有限球面上趋于零的全球壳类解，并通过合适的数学分析手段证明其存在性。

Result: 在选定的参数范围内，证明了存在 holosphere 类自相似解：这些解在穿越声速线时保持光滑性，但在某个球面位置上密度衰减至零，形成球壳结构。

Conclusion: 该结果扩展了等熵欧拉方程自相似解族的已知范围，揭示了在特定参数条件下可存在具有球面密度消失特征的新型光滑解，为理解气体动力学中自相似解的全局结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We consider the barotropic Euler equations in dimension $d > 1$ with decaying density at spatial infinity. The phase portrait of the nonlinear ODE governing the equation for spherically symmetric self-similar solutions has been introduced in the pioneering work of Guderley. The existence of smooth decaying solutions through the sonic line has been obtained in the work (F. Merle et al., 2022) in a suitable range of parameters. We work in this paper in a different range of parameters and show the existence of holosphere like solutions which are smooth through the sonic line, but display vanishing density at a sphere.

</details>


### [173] [Derivation of a linearly elastic elliptic membrane shell theory in the framework of magnetoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30445)
*Trung Hieu Giang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文从三维线性磁弹性模型出发，研究薄壳厚度趋于零时的渐近行为，利用Γ-收敛严格推导出二维线性磁弹性壳模型，并建立了与原三维模型在极小元收敛方面的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究薄壳结构在磁弹性效应下的渐近行为，为从三维模型降维到二维壳模型提供严格的数学基础，弥补磁弹性薄壳理论中缺乏严格推导的空白。

Method: 从三维线性化磁弹性模型出发，针对一族线性弹性椭圆膜壳，采用Γ-收敛方法进行渐近分析，严格推导二维线性磁弹性壳模型。

Result: 成功推导出二维线性磁弹性壳模型，并证明其与原三维模型之间极小元收敛的关系。

Conclusion: 通过Γ-收敛严格建立了三维到二维磁弹性壳模型的降维理论，为后续磁弹性薄壳理论研究提供了数学框架。

Abstract: Starting from a three-dimensional model of linearized magnetoelasticity, we investigate the asymptotic behavior of a thin shell as its thickness approaches zero. Our analysis focuses on a family of linearly elastic elliptic membrane shells. Using $Γ$-convergence, we rigorously derive a two-dimensional linear magnetoelastic shell model and establish its relationship to the original three-dimensional model in terms of the convergence of minimizers.

</details>


### [174] [Grow-up rates of inhomogeneous semilinear heat equations in the critical dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30518)
*Kenta Kumagai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究单位球内带指数非线性和非齐次项的半线性热方程解的增长率。在临界维数N=10下，给出grow-up现象消失的定量刻画，揭示了与N≥11情形本质不同的增长行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知当f=0时，大时间行为在临界维数N=10处发生变化，N≥10时出现grow-up现象。对于N≥10的非齐次情形，当f超过阈值时grow-up现象消失。本文旨在定量刻画临界维数下这一消失过程，并严格证明Galaktionov和King的正式计算结果。

Method: 通过获得临界维数下grow-up速率来定量描述grow-up现象的消失，分析解的外层行为变化，将其与N≥11情形进行对比。

Result: 临界维数下表现出与N≥11情形定性不同的grow-up行为，这种差异源于解的外层行为的变化。即使在f=0的情形下，本文结果也为Galaktionov和King的正式计算提供了严格的数学证明。

Conclusion: 临界维数N=10下grow-up现象的消失具有独特的定量特征，其行为模式与N≥11时存在本质区别，为理解半线性热方程的增长行为提供了更完整的理论。

Abstract: This paper concerns the grow-up rate of solutions to a semilinear heat equation in the unit ball with the exponential nonlinearity and an inhomogeneous term $f$. When $f=0$, it is known that the large-time behavior of a solution changes at the critical dimension $N=10$, and the grow-up phenomenon occurs for the case $N\ge 10$. For the inhomogeneous case with $N\ge 10$, the present author and a coauthor showed in [12] that the grow-up phenomenon disappears once $f$ exceeds a threshold.
  In this paper, we provide a quantitative characterization of the disappearance of the grow-up phenomenon by obtaining the grow-up rate in the critical dimension. Our result shows that a qualitatively different type of grow-up behavior occurs in the critical dimension compared with the case $N\ge 11$ studied in [12]. The difference is caused by a change in the outer behavior of the solution. Even in the case $f=0$, our result is new in that it provides a rigorous justification of the formal computation by Galaktionov and King [10].

</details>


### [175] [Finite speed of propagation and waiting time phenomena for stochastic porous media equations with nonlinear conservative noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30548)
*Günther Grün,Max Sauerbrey,Joshua Utley*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有非线性保守噪声的随机多孔介质方程的动力学解的有限传播速度问题，提出了一种新的迭代技术，获得涉及单一积分量的Stampacchia型不等式，并证明了等待时间现象的发生条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近已建立了带非线性保守噪声的随机多孔介质方程动力学解的存在唯一性，但解的有限传播速度性质尚不明确。由于多孔介质项和噪声项的标度行为可能不同，传统的证明方法难以直接应用，需要发展新的技术手段。

Method: 从局部能量估计出发，提出一种新颖的迭代技术，克服了多孔介质项与噪声项标度非一致的困难，从而获得涉及单一积分量的Stampacchia型不等式；在此基础上应用为线性源型噪声情形发展的随机滤波（stochastic filtering）论证。

Result: 证明了带非线性保守噪声的随机多孔介质方程动力学解具有有限传播速度；同时识别出保证局部等待时间现象（解的支撑向前传播被延迟）发生的初始数据平展性条件。该条件在噪声临界非线性情形下与确定性多孔介质方程相匹配（至多差一个对数修正），但随着非线性趋向线性保守噪声，对初始数据平展性的要求越来越高。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在线性保守噪声情形下，由于随机输运效应，无论初始剖面多么平展，都可能出现瞬时向前运动；而在非线性噪声情形下，与确定性情形一致（至多对数修正），等待时间现象可以发生。这验证了物理直觉上的预期行为。

Abstract: Starting from localized energy estimates, we prove finite speed of propagation for kinetic solutions to stochastic porous media equations with nonlinear conservative noise, the existence and uniqueness of which has recently been established. In particular, we propose a novel iteration technique which allows us to obtain a Stampacchia-type inequality involving one single integral quantity, despite the possibly different scaling behaviors of the porous media and the noise term. This allows us to apply stochastic filtering arguments developed for the case of linear source-type noise. Using related ideas, we identify flatness conditions on initial data which guarantee locally the occurrence of a waiting time phenomenon, i.e., the onset of forward propagation of the solution's support is locally delayed. The condition for the latter matches the one for the deterministic porous media equation up to a logarithmic correction in the case of critical nonlinearity in the noise, but it requires more and more flatness of the initial data as the nonlinearity tends towards linear conservative noise. This is in line with the expected behavior: In the case of linear conservative noise, instantaneous forward motion is possible due to the effects of stochastic transport, no matter how flat the initial profile is.

</details>


### [176] [Compactly Supported Solutions near a Nehari Critical Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30578)
*J. I. Dıaz,J. Hernandez,Y. Ilyasov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类带不定权函数的次线性Dirichlet问题的非负解，其中权函数m可以变号或非正，参数λ为实数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在不定次线性Dirichlet问题中，当权函数变号或非正时，非负解的存在性、唯一性及其对参数λ的依赖关系。

Method: 通过对问题的次线性结构进行分析，结合变分方法和比较原理等工具，研究参数λ的不同取值范围下非负解的存在性与性质。

Result: 该研究揭示了这类不定次线性问题非负解的存在性与参数λ及权函数m的符号变化之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: 该问题在不定权函数和次线性非线性项的组合下，非负解的结构依赖于特征值λ与权函数符号的交互作用。

Abstract: We study non-negative solutions of the indefinite sublinear Dirichlet problem $$ \left \{\ \begin{array}{ll} -Δu=λu+m(x)|u|^{α-1}u&\quad\hbox{ in $Ω$},\\ u=0&\quad \hbox{ on $\partial Ω$}, \end{array} \right . $$ where $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^{N}$ is a smooth bounded domain, $0<α<1$, $m\in L^{\infty }(Ω)$ is a sign-changing or non-positive weight, and $λ\in \mathbb{R}$.

</details>


### [177] [The flexible part of intermittent Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30582)
*Zhenxin Liu,Wenhu Zhong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对二维随机不可压缩欧拉方程的Onsager定理柔性部分展开研究，开发了随机且间歇性的Newton-Nash迭代方案，对于任意γ∈[0,1/3)，构造出γ-霍尔德连续的鞅解。这些解表现出耗散行为和间歇性，解决了二维随机不可压缩欧拉方程的间歇性Onsager定理柔性部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决二维随机不可压缩欧拉方程中Onsager定理的柔性部分，即构造不满足经典能量守恒的粗糙（低正则性）鞅解，并体现随机系统中的间歇性特征。

Method: 开发了一种随机且间歇性的Newton-Nash迭代方案，该方案融合了新的随机压力、Reynolds应力和间歇性扰动，以形式化随机与间歇性波动。

Result: 对于任意γ∈[0,1/3)，构造了γ-霍尔德连续的鞅解，这些解展现出耗散行为和间歇性，从而确立了二维随机不可压缩欧拉方程间歇性Onsager定理的柔性部分。

Conclusion: 该研究将Onsager定理的凸积分解法（convex integration）扩展到随机设置，并引入间歇性构造，完成了二维随机欧拉方程柔性部分的最优正则性阈值（1/3临界指数）的构建。

Abstract: This work is concerned with the flexible part of Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations. We develop a stochastic and intermittent variant of the Newton--Nash iteration scheme, which incorporates new stochastic pressure, Reynolds stress and intermittency perturbations to formalize the stochastic and intermittent fluctuations. For any $γ\in \left[ {0,\frac{1}{3}} \right)$, we employ this iteration scheme to construct $γ$-Hölder-continuous martingale solutions for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations. These martingale solutions exhibit dissipative behavior and intermittency, thereby settling the flexible part of intermittent Onsager theorem for the two-dimensional stochastic incompressible Euler equations.

</details>


### [178] [Revisiting Fazly-Wei-Xu pointwise estimates for the fourth-order Hénon equation via Bernstein's technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30625)
*Quôc Anh Ngô,Trung Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对四阶Hénon方程的C^4正解，建立了一个点态估计不等式。作者采用基于最大值原理的Bernstein技术替代原有文献中复杂的Moser迭代论证，不仅得到了与原文献相同的点态估计，还将适用范围推广到临界指数情形并放宽了解的boundedness要求。该估计可推导出R^n上具有正标量曲率和Q曲率的共形度量的存在性，并证明了系数2/(n-4)的sharp性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M. Fazly, J. Wei和X. Xu在Analysis and PDE (2015)中建立了四阶Hénon方程解的点态估计不等式，但其证明依赖于复杂的Moser型迭代论证，且仅适用于σ隐式地接近0的情形。作者希望提供一种更简洁、更具构造性的替代证明方法，同时扩展其适用范围。

Method: 作者采用Bernstein技术，结合最大值原理（maximum principle）来替代原有的Moser迭代论证。方法的核心在于恰当选取一个辅助函数（auxiliary function），从而无需依赖于解的boundedness，并能够处理临界指数情形p=(n+4+2σ)/(n-4)，同时将σ的条件放松为任意σ≥0。

Result: 作者成功建立了与原始文献相同的点态估计不等式，但适用范围更广：(1) 对任意σ≥0成立；(2) 在临界指数p=(n+4+2σ)/(n-4)处仍然成立；(3) 不再需要解的boundedness假设。此外证明系数2/(n-4)是最优的（sharp），首次回答了原文献中提出的问题。

Conclusion: 该点态估计的一个有趣推论是R^n上存在具有正标量曲率(scalar curvature)和Q曲率的共形度量(conformal metric)。整个证明比原文献方法更简洁且具有构造性，并首次解决了原文献中关于系数最优性的开放问题。

Abstract: In a remarkable work published in Analysis and PDE 8 (2015) 1541-1563, M. Fazly, J. Wei, and X. Xu establish the following pointwise estimate \[-Δu \geq \frac 2{n-4} \frac{|\nabla u|^2}u + \sqrt{\frac 2{p+1- \frac 8{n(n-4)}}} |x|^{\frac σ2} u^\frac{p+1}2 \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\] for any bounded, positive, $C^4$-solution $u$ to the the fourth-order Hénon equation \[Δ^2 u = |x|^σu^p \quad \text{in } \mathbf R^n\] with $n \geq 5$, $σ\geq 0 $ but implicitly near $0$, and $p>(n+4+2σ)/(n-4)$. Their argument relies on a sophisticated iteration argument in the fashion of the standard Moser proof. In this paper, we provide an alternative approach which relies on the Bernstein technique via the maximum principle. The heart of our argument is a suitable choice of an auxiliary function allowing us not only achieving the same pointwise estimate, which still holds even for $p = (n+4+2σ)/(n-4)$, but also relaxing the boundedness of solutions. In addition, the pointwise inequality actually holds for any $σ\geq 0$. This estimate leads to an interesting consequence of the existence of conformal metric of $\mathbf R^n$ having positive scalar and $Q$ curvatures. We also show that the coefficient $2/(n-4)$ is sharp, hence providing the first answer to the question in the op. cit. paper. Our proof appears to be simpler and constructive.

</details>


### [179] [Higher Regularity of Time-Periodic Solutions to Nonautonomous Hyperbolic Problems: Away from Resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30658)
*Irina Kmit,Lutz Recke*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维线性与非线性非自治一阶积分-微分严格双曲系统边值问题的时间周期解的高阶正则性及其与非共振行为的关系。证明了在系数足够光滑且满足适当数量非共振条件时，连续解和经典解具有C^k正则性。非自治情形下高阶正则性通常需要额外的非共振条件，且其数量取决于目标可微阶数；这些条件不仅充分而且在一般情况下也是必要的。相比之下，自治情形单个（或无需）非共振条件即可获得任意高阶正则性。文中还识别了一类无需任何非共振条件即可仅由数据正则性决定解的正则性的非自治双曲问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非自治双曲PDE的时间周期解在不同共振条件下的正则性行为尚不清晰，特别是高阶正则性所需的非共振条件数量与结构之间的关系，以及与非自治情形的本质区别，亟需系统研究。

Method: 以向量空间中的抽象正则性原理为主要技术工具，分别处理线性和非线性情形；在线性情形中非共振条件涉及主系数、对角低阶系数和边界反射系数，而非线性情形中条件还依赖于非线性和解本身。

Result: 证明了在适当数量的非共振条件下，解具有C^k正则性，且这些条件在一般情况下也是必要的。发现非自治情形需要随目标可微阶数增长的多个非共振条件，而自治情形单个条件即可；同时识别了一类无需非共振条件的非自治问题。

Conclusion: 高阶正则性与非共振条件之间的依赖关系是非自治双曲PDE的独特特征，这与自治情形形成鲜明对比。该研究揭示了非自治双曲系统解的更精细的正则性结构。

Abstract: We study higher regularity and its relation to nonresonant behavior for time-periodic solutions of boundary value problems for one-dimensional linear and nonlinear nonautonomous first-order integro-differential strictly hyperbolic systems. The boundary conditions include integral operators and various types of boundary reflections. We prove that continuous and classical solutions have $C^k$-regularity, provided the coefficients are sufficiently smooth and a suitable number of nonresonance conditions is satisfied. In the linear case, these conditions involve the principal coefficients, the diagonal lower-order coefficients, and the boundary reflection coefficients. In the nonlinear case, they also depend on the nonlinearities and on the solution itself. For nonautonomous hyperbolic systems, higher regularity generally requires additional nonresonance conditions, whose number depends on the desired order of differentiability. These conditions are not only sufficient but, in general, also necessary, revealing a distinctive feature of nonautonomous hyperbolic PDEs. By contrast, in the autonomous case, a single nonresonance condition (if one is needed at all) suffices to obtain arbitrarily high regularity. We also identify a class of nonautonomous hyperbolic problems for which no nonresonance conditions are required. In this case, the higher regularity of solutions is determined solely by the regularity of the data. The main technical tool underlying the proofs is an abstract regularity principle formulated in the setting of vector spaces.

</details>


### [180] [Endpoint Energy Atoms Force Local Pressure Concentration in Three-Dimensional Navier-Stokes Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30715)
*Hao Huang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明三维不可压缩Navier-Stokes流在平坦环面上的端动能测度点原子会强制产生实际压力的定量集中。通过同态约束-响应比较法，建立精确的相对压力-功恒等式，并利用Nash光滑化与介观局部化技术，证明压力模去时间函数及其梯度在原子点的邻域内非平方可积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立端点动能测度中的点原子与局部压力浓度之间的必要联系。长期以来，Navier-Stokes正则性理论关注速度场的奇性结构，但对压力在奇点附近的定量行为研究不足。作者希望在不依赖完全正则性假设的前提下，识别出由不可压缩约束产生的必要压力-功机制，从而深化对Navier-Stokes方程可能奇性的理解。

Method: 1) 引入同态约束-响应比较框架：在每个重启时间tau，将u(tau)按无压力的分量对流扩散方程演化，得到被动场z_tau；2) 定义残差r_tau = u - z_tau及g_tau = Q z_tau（Q为Leray投影），推导精确的相对压力-功恒等式；3) 利用Nash光滑化证明z_tau终态非原子，而r_tau保留至少m的终态原子质量；4) 通过介观局部化获得尺度显式的L2压力振荡与压力梯度局部质量的界；5) 将原理推广到约束螺线管Oseen演化，分析零初始响应的弱尾拓扑收敛性。

Result: 1) 若端动能测度含质量为m的点原子，则每个固定重启后，累积压力功的终态limsup至少为m/2；2) 在原子点的每个终态邻域内，压力（模去时间函数）及其梯度的L2范数发散（非平方可积）；3) 获得尺度显式的压力振荡与压力梯度质量下界；4) 相对压力-功原理对约束Oseen演化仍成立，零初始响应在弱尾拓扑中消失，但每个固定重启保留相同的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了端点原子导致压力集中这一必要机制，揭示了不可压缩约束在奇性形成中的核心作用。其意义在于：不依赖完全正则性假设即可推导出压力的定量奇性行为，为三维Navier-Stokes正则性理论提供了新的结构性约束，并可能为最终判定解的正则性/奇性提供新的判别工具。

Abstract: We prove that a point atom in an endpoint kinetic-energy measure of a three-dimensional incompressible Navier-Stokes flow on the flat torus forces quantitative concentration of the actual pressure. At each restart time tau, a same-state constraint-response comparison evolves u(tau) by the pressure-free componentwise advection-diffusion equation driven by u, and subtracts the resulting passive field z_tau. Writing r_tau = u - z_tau and g_tau = Q z_tau, we obtain an exact relative pressure-work identity. If the atom has mass m, Nash smoothing makes z_tau terminally non-atomic while r_tau retains terminal atomic mass at least m; hence, after every fixed restart, the terminal limsup of the accumulated pressure work is at least m/2. Mesoscopic localization yields scale-explicit lower bounds for gauge-invariant L2 pressure oscillation and for the local L2 mass of the pressure gradient in shrinking terminal cylinders. Consequently, on every terminal neighborhood of the atomic point, the pressure modulo functions of time and its gradient fail to be square integrable. The relative pressure-work principle extends to constrained solenoidal Oseen evolutions: as tau approaches T*, the zero-initial responses vanish in weak-tail topologies, although every fixed restart retains the same lower bound. This identifies a necessary pressure-work mechanism, generated by the incompressibility constraint, that links endpoint atoms to local pressure concentration, without requiring a full regularity hypothesis.

</details>


### [181] [Ultracontractivity of heat semigroups with non-local Robin boundary conditions via two-sided bounds for a positive eigenfunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30763)
*Christoph Schwerdt*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界Lipschitz域上的热半群，在非局部Robin边界条件下，证明了由一致椭圆算子生成的半群是超压缩的，其从L²(Ω)到L∞(Ω)的范数具有t^{-d/4}的短时阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Robin边界条件下的热半群性质已有充分研究，但当边界算子B允许破坏半群的正性时，超压缩性等关键性质是否仍然成立是未知的。本文旨在放宽正性条件，仅假设存在一个正控制算子C来支配B，在此更一般的框架下研究半群的超压缩性。

Method: 主要步骤是构造一个控制椭圆算子的正特征函数φ，满足0<δ≤φ≤M几乎处处成立。上界通过幂截断和Sobolev指数的迭代获得；下界通过与Neumann半群比较获得。上下界估计给出比较半群的L^∞界，再结合Nash不等式和对偶性导出超压缩性。

Result: 在边界算子B满足存在正算子C支配且C**1**∈L^∞(∂Ω)的假设下，证明了半群从L²(Ω)到L∞(Ω)的范数具有短时阶t^{-d/4}，即半群是超压缩的。

Conclusion: 即使边界算子破坏半群的正性，只要存在可支配它的正算子（且C作用于常函数上仍为有界），超压缩性仍然保持成立。这一结果为非局部Robin边界条件下椭圆算子的半群理论提供了更一般的框架。

Abstract: We study heat semigroups on bounded Lipschitz domains $Ω\subset \mathbb{R}^{d}$ with dimension $d>2$ under non-local Robin boundary conditions. The boundary operator $B \in \mathcal{L}( \mathrm{L}^{2}(\partialΩ))$ is allowed to destroy the positivity of the semigroup. We assume that there exists a positive operator $C \in \mathcal{L}(\mathrm{L}^{2}(\partialΩ))$ such that $$ |Bu| \leq C|u| \quad\text{for every }u\in \mathrm{L}^{2}(\partialΩ), \qquad C{\bf 1}\in \mathrm{L}^{\infty}(\partialΩ). $$ Under this assumption, we prove that the semigroup generated by the corresponding uniformly elliptic operator is ultracontractive. More precisely, its norm from $\mathrm{L}^{2}(Ω)$ to $\mathrm{L}^{\infty}(Ω)$ has short-time order $t^{-d/4}$. The main step is the construction of a positive eigenfunction $φ$ of a dominating elliptic operator such that $$ 0 \ < \ δ\ \leq \ φ\ \leq \ M $$ almost everywhere in $Ω$. The upper bound is obtained by power truncations and an iteration of Sobolev exponents. The lower bound follows by comparison with the Neumann semigroup. The two-sided estimate gives an $\mathrm{L}^{\infty}$-bound for the comparison semigroup. Nash's inequality and duality then yield ultracontractivity.

</details>


### [182] [Inhomogeneous nonlinear Schrödinger equation in Fourier-Lebesgue and modulation spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30777)
*Divyang G. Bhimani,Diksha Dhingra,Vijay Kumar Sohani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了非齐次非线性薛定谔方程（INLS）在Fourier-Lebesgue空间和调制空间中的低正则性适定性问题，分别针对p<2和p>2两种情况，通过数据分解方法在任意大初值下证明了全局适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为非齐次非线性薛定谔方程（INLS）建立更广泛的分析框架，特别是在低正则性条件下研究其在Fourier-Lebesgue空间和调制空间中的适定性问题，拓展了已有结果的应用范围。

Method: 采用数据分解方法（data decomposition method），并将其适配到Fourier-Lebesgue空间框架中。针对p<2和p>2两种参数情形分别进行分析处理，利用Fourier-Lebesgue空间的特殊性质建立低正则性适定性结果，并将方法推广到调制空间M^{p,p'}。

Result: 在Fourier-Lebesgue空间\hat{L}^p中（4/3<p<8）建立了INLS方程的低正则性适定性，并在任意大初值下证明了全局适定性；同时在调制空间M^{p,p'}（4/3<p<2）中获得类似结果。

Conclusion: 通过数据分解方法成功建立了INLS方程在Fourier-Lebesgue空间和调制空间中的全局适定性，为低正则性框架下研究含奇性位势的非线性Schrödinger方程提供了新的方法和更广泛的适用结果。

Abstract: The purpose of this work is to provide a broader framework for analyzing the inhomogeneous nonlinear Schrödinger equation (INLS) \[iu_t + u_{xx} \pm |x|^{-b}|u|^{α-1}u=0, \quad 1<α< 5-2b\; \text{and}\; 0< b\leq 1/4.\] Specifically, we establish low-regularity well-posedness in the Fourier-Lebesgue $\widehat{L}^{p}$ spaces for $4/3<p<8$. The analysis is carried out differently for the cases $p<2$ and $p>2$. Primarily, in both cases, we prove global well-posedness for arbitrarily large initial data via the data decomposition method adapted for the Fourier-Lebesgue spaces. Furthermore, we obtain analogous results for INLS in modulation spaces $M^{p,p'}$ for $4/3<p<2$.

</details>


### [183] [Tumor boundary instability induced by pressure feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30796)
*Yu Feng,Yeyu Zhang,Kewei Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类Hele-Shaw型肿瘤生长模型的边界稳定性。该模型由多孔介质方程的无压缩极限导出，通过渐近分析研究了均势压力相关的压力反馈参数对有限半径肿瘤和平面行进前沿边界扰动的影响，推导出稳定性判据，并发现平面前沿的稳定阈值是产生非对称行进波的岔点。结果表明压力反馈能在无反馈模型稳定时诱导边界不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤生长模型的边界稳定性对于理解肿瘤形态发生和浸润机制至关重要。Hele-Shaw型肿瘤模型能自然描述肿瘤密度在不同区域间形成的自由边界，但均势压力反馈如何影响边界稳定性尚不清楚，需要系统的渐近分析来揭示其机制。

Method: 采用渐近分析方法。针对有限半径肿瘤的边界扰动和平面行进前沿两类几何构型，引入压力反馈参数，推导对应的线性稳定性判据。对于平面前沿，进一步通过岔点分析证明稳定阈值处存在非对称行进行波的涌现。

Result: 推导出有限半径肿瘤和平面行进前沿两类情形的边界稳定性条件。证明平面前沿的稳定性阈值同时也是岔点，在这些点处可以产生非对称的行进波解。关键发现是压力反馈在某些参数区域内可以诱导边界失稳，而这些区域在无压力反馈的模型中原本是稳定的。

Conclusion: 压力反馈在肿瘤边界稳定性中扮演重要角色，能够产生新的不稳定性机制。该结果对理解肿瘤在体内压力环境下形态演变和浸润行为具有理论意义，也为后续非线性分析提供了基础。

Abstract: We consider a Hele--Shaw-type tumor growth model obtained from the incompressible limit of a porous-medium equation. The limiting model preserves the patch structure of the tumor density and therefore admits a single free-boundary formulation. We investigate how a pressure-feedback parameter associated with the homeostatic pressure affects boundary stability. Using asymptotic analysis, we study boundary perturbations of finite-radius tumors and planar traveling fronts and derive the corresponding stability criteria. For planar fronts, the stability thresholds are further shown to be bifurcation points from which nonsymmetric traveling waves emerge. Our results indicate that pressure feedback can induce boundary instability in parameter regimes where the model without pressure feedback remains stable.

</details>


### [184] [An Almgren-type formula for planar $p$-harmonic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30847)
*Yi Ru-Ya Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对非恒定的平面p-调和函数（1<p<∞），引入流量归一化频率函数N_*(x_0,r)，这是Almgren频率函数在非线性p-调和框架下的自然对应物，能精确恢复每个齐次p-调和剖面的齐次度。文章建立了相应的规范修正Almgren型单调性，重新解释了经典平面唯一延拓性质，并推导了流量归一化频率的定量收缩估计，同时指出阻碍该方法推广到高维的具体障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在p-调和函数的非线性设定中，线性情形的Almgren频率函数不再直接适用。此前Aronsson在孤立临界点邻域对hodograph表示进行了基础性分析。作者希望构建一个在非线性p-调和框架下能够自然替代Almgren频率函数的工具，以支持齐次度恢复、单调性和唯一延拓等关键分析。

Method: 作者基于Aronsson的hodograph表示分析，定义了流量归一化频率N_*(x_0,r)，其为体积积分（|Du|^p）与边界积分（|Du|^{p-2}(u-u(x_0))^2）之比的规范化形式。在此基础上建立规范修正（gauge-corrected）的Almgren型单调性公式，并利用频率函数重新阐释唯一延拓性质，进而推导定量收缩（pinching）估计。

Result: 流量归一化频率N_*精确恢复了每个齐次p-调和剖面的齐次度；建立了对应的规范修正Almgren型单调性；通过频率函数对经典平面唯一延拓性质给出新诠释；得到流量归一化频率的定量收缩估计，并识别出阻止方法向高维推广的特定障碍。

Conclusion: 流量归一化频率函数是平面p-调和分析中Almgren频率函数的正确非线性类比物，为齐次性刻画、单调性和唯一延拓提供了统一框架。但方法受限于平面，存在特定结构障碍使其无法直接推广到高维情形。

Abstract: For a nonconstant planar $p$-harmonic function $u$, with $1 < p < \infty$, and according to previous results, most notably Aronsson's fundamental analysis of the hodograph representation in a neighborhood of an isolated critical point, we introduce the flux-normalized frequency $$
  N_*(x_0,r) = \frac{r\displaystyle\int_{B_r(x_0)} |D u|^p\,dx} {\displaystyle\int_{\partial B_r(x_0)} |D u|^{p-2}(u-u(x_0))^2\,dS}. $$ This ratio constitutes the natural counterpart of Almgren's frequency function in the nonlinear setting, as it recovers precisely the degree of homogeneity for every homogeneous $p$-harmonic profile. We further establish the corresponding gauge-corrected version of Almgren-type monotonicity and reinterpret, in terms of the frequency function, the classical planar unique continuation property. In addition, we derive a quantitative pinching estimate for the flux-normalized frequency and identify the specific obstruction that prevents a straightforward extension of the method to higher dimensions.

</details>


### [185] [Conservation Laws with Discontinuous Flux: A First Application to Traffic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30859)
*Roberta Bianchini,Maya Briani,Benedetto Piccoli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为文献[ABS2025]中提出的模型提供了一个交通流解释，将具有不连续梯度相关通量的守恒律适配到宏观交通流背景下，针对凹通量函数引入了修正的Riemann求解器以防止密度越界，并将弱解存在性理论推广到更一般的两相模型（含自由流状态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将文献[ABS2025]中提出的抽象数学模型（具有不连续梯度相关通量的守恒律）转化为具体的宏观交通流模型，同时需要解决最大密度约束不被违反的问题，并扩展弱解存在性理论至更实际的交通流场景。

Method: 1）将带不连续梯度依赖通量的守恒律适配到宏观交通流背景，假设通量函数f(u)和g(u)在密度域[0,u_max]上为凹函数；2）引入针对该设定修正的Riemann求解器，防止最大密度约束被违反；3）将文献[ABS2025]中建立的弱解存在性理论进行推广；4）构建更广泛的两相模型的Riemann求解器，其中在[0,u_max]的子集上有f(u)=g(u)（即自由流状态），并在此更一般的框架下建立弱解的存在性。

Result: 成功将模型转化为交通流解释，提出了修正的Riemann求解器以保证最大密度约束，推广了弱解存在性理论，并在包含自由流状态的两相模型框架下建立了弱解的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了不连续梯度相关通量守恒律模型与宏观交通流之间的桥梁，通过修正的Riemann求解器保证了物理约束（最大密度限制），并将弱解存在性结果从原始模型推广到了更一般且更贴近实际的两相交通流模型。

Abstract: This work provides a traffic flow interpretation of the model recently introduced in \cite{ABS2025}. We adapt the conservation law with discontinuous gradient-dependent flux to a macroscopic traffic setting, assuming concave flux functions $f(u)$ and $g(u)$ defined on the density domain $[0,u_{\max}]$. To prevent violations of the maximal density constraint, we introduce modified Riemann Solvers tailored to this setting and generalize the existence theory for weak solutions developed in \cite{ABS2025}. Finally, we construct Riemann Solvers for a broader two-phase model incorporating a free-flow regime, in which $f(u)=g(u)$ on a subset of $[0,u_{\max}]$, and establish existence of weak solutions in this more general framework.

</details>


### [186] [Necessity of Ladyzhenskaya \& Ural'tseva's sufficient conditions in nonuniform ellipticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30898)
*Cristiana De Filippis*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Ladyzhenskaya和Ural'tseva关于非均匀椭圆问题内部梯度估计的充分条件实际上也是必要的。非参数极小曲面表现出与解的局部Lipschitz正则性相容的最大非均匀性速率，且Finn以及Bombieri、De Giorgi和Miranda的经典理论无法推广到Bernstein类型2以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Ladyzhenskaya和Ural'tseva提出的非均匀椭圆问题内部梯度估计的充分条件是否为必要条件，并研究极小曲面问题中非均匀性速率与解的Lipschitz正则性之间的极限关系。

Method: 通过构造反例或利用极小曲面理论，分析非参数极小曲面所对应的非均匀性速率，并与Ladyzhenskaya和Ural'tseva的条件进行对比，证明该条件的必要性。

Result: 证明了Ladyzhenskaya和Ural'tseva的充分条件同时也是必要的；非参数极小曲面恰好达到与局部Lipschitz正则性相容的最大非均匀性速率；Finn及Bombieri、De Giorgi和Miranda的经典理论止步于Bernstein类型2，无法进一步推广。

Conclusion: 非均匀椭圆问题的梯度估计理论与极小曲面理论之间存在精确的临界匹配，Ladyzhenskaya和Ural'tseva的条件在极小曲面情形下是最优且不可改进的。

Abstract: The sufficient conditions of Ladyzhenskaya \& Ural'tseva \cite{lu70} for interior gradient estimates in nonuniformly elliptic problems are found to be also necessary. Specifically, nonparametric minimal surfaces exhibit the maximal nonuniformity rate compatible with the local Lipschitz regularity of solutions, and the classical theory of Finn \cite{fin54} and Bombieri \& De Giorgi \& Miranda \cite{bdm69} doesn't extend beyond Bernstein genre $2$.

</details>


### [187] [Entire monotone solutions of the anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation in dimension 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30904)
*Haowen Lu,Song Wang,Juncheng Wei,Yuanze Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文基于Mooney-Yang各向异性极小图，证明了当N≥4时各向异性Allen-Cahn方程存在非超平面水平集的弱稳定解；并作为副产品，构造了N≥5时该方程沿一个方向单调但非一维的光滑解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的De Giorgi猜想等结果表明，在欧氏情形下Allen-Cahn方程的稳定解或单调解在某些维度下被迫退化为单维解。本文旨在探索加入各向异性结构（由均匀椭圆积分F定义的H(p))后，这一退化行为是否仍然成立，即能否构造出各向异性Allen-Cahn方程的非平凡稳定解及非一维单调解。

Method: 利用Mooney-Yang构造的各向异性极小图（anisotropic minimal graph），将其作为Allen-Cahn方程解的几何背景，通过构造与极小图相关联的稳定解。在N≥4时证明弱稳定解的存在性，并进一步利用更高维度的构造在N≥5时得到单调但非一维的光滑解。

Result: 证明了当N≥4时，各向异性Allen-Cahn方程存在弱稳定解，其水平集不是超平面；当N≥5时，构造出沿一个方向单调但非一维的光滑解。这表明各向异性结构打破了欧氏情形中稳定解/单调解的刚性退化。

Conclusion: 各向异性Allen-Cahn方程在合适维度下允许非平凡（非超平面水平集）的稳定解和非一维单调解，这与欧氏均匀情形的刚性结果形成对比，揭示了各向异性对相分离模型解的几何结构有本质影响。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation $-\operatorname{div} a(Du)+W'(u)=0$ in $\mathbb{R}^N$, where $a(p):=DH(p)$ with $H(p)=\frac{1}{2}F(p)^2$ and $F$ a uniformly elliptic integrand, and $W(u)=\frac{1}{4}(1-u^2)^2$. Based on the Mooney-Yang anisotropic minimal graph, we prove that the anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation admits a stable solution for $N\geq4$ in the weak sense, whose level sets are not hyperplanes. As a byproduct, we also construct a smooth solution of the above anisotropic Allen-Cahn equation for $N\geq5$ that is monotone in one direction but is not one-dimensional.

</details>


### [188] [On a pointwise estimate for a weighted rough singular integral operator in stratified Lie groups and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30970)
*Diego Chamorro,Oscar Jarrín*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在分层Lie群框架下，针对由满足自然尺寸条件和关于权函数满足消逝性质的核所定义的一类加权粗糙奇异积分算子，建立了新的逐点估计。该估计通过梯度加权极大函数和加权Morrey空间信息来刻画算子的作用，并据此推导了新的加权泛函不等式，最终应用于Heisenberg群上粗糙版稳态Navier-Stokes方程的唯一性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分层Lie群（特别是Heisenberg群）的非交换调和分析框架下，经典的奇异积分算子理论需要对核函数施加正则性假设。作者希望去除核的正则性条件，仅在自然的尺寸条件与消逝性质下研究加权粗糙奇异积分算子，并建立相应的点态估计，从而获得加权泛函不等式并应用于偏微分方程（Navier-Stokes方程）的唯一性问题。

Method: 作者在分层Lie群上构造了加权粗糙奇异积分算子T_{Ω, ϖ}，其中核Ω仅满足尺寸条件且关于权ϖ具有消逝性质（不假设任何正则性）。通过引入加权极大函数作用于函数梯度的信息，并结合加权Morrey空间的框架，建立了该算子对函数f作用的逐点控制估计。由此逐点估计进一步推导出加权泛函不等式。

Result: 得到了加权粗糙奇异积分算子的新的逐点估计，该估计将算子作用控制为梯度的加权极大函数与加权Morrey空间信息之组合；由此推导出了若干新的加权泛函不等式；并作为应用，在Heisenberg群上获得了粗糙版本稳态Navier-Stokes方程解的唯一性结果。

Conclusion: 该研究将加权粗糙奇异积分算子的理论推广至分层Lie群的一般框架，在去除核正则性假设的同时仍能获得有效的点态估计，并通过建立加权泛函不等式将抽象算子理论成功应用于Navier-Stokes方程唯一性这一具体偏微分方程问题，展示了该方法的理论价值与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In this article, we present a new pointwise estimate for a weighted rough singular integral operator in the setting of stratified Lie groups. This operator, $T_{Ω, \varpi}$, is based on a kernel $Ω$ and a weight $\varpi$, where the kernel satisfies a natural size condition and a cancellation property with respect to the weight $\varpi$. Moreover, we do not assume any kind of regularity on these objects. This weighted rough singular integral operator, applied to a function $f$, is estimated through a combination of information involving a weighted maximal function of the gradient of $f$ and a weighted Morrey space. We also deduce from this pointwise estimate some new weighted functional inequalities and, as an application, we obtain a uniqueness result for a rough version of the stationary Navier-Stokes equation over the Heisenberg group.

</details>


### [189] [A block-Toeplitz approach to the spectrum of the Neumann-Poincaré operator on self-similar chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30991)
*Matias Ruiz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了定义在非Lipschitz域（由无限自相似链状不相交光滑域组成并在极限点累积）上的Neumann-Poincaré算子在Sobolev-Slobodeckij空间W^{s,p}(Γ)的均值为零子空间上的谱。利用链的离散几何缩放，建立了算子精确的块Toeplitz表示，并表征了本质谱、Fredholm区域、能量空间中的孤立特征值、渐近谱分布及特征值计数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neumann-Poincaré算子的谱分析在势理论、边界积分方程和复合材料均匀化等领域具有重要应用。此前的工作大多局限于Lipschitz域或光滑边界，而实际应用中常遇到非Lipschitz几何（如具有累积极限点的自相似结构）。在非Lipschitz情形下（特别是无限自相似链结构），经典谱理论不再直接适用，需要新的数学工具来刻画其谱性质。

Method: 1) 利用链的离散几何缩放，构造块Toeplitz表示Neumann-Poincaré算子及单层、双层算子；2) 引入仅依赖于单一缩放参数α=(d-1)/p-s的算子值符号，证明其在0<α<d时属于Wiener代数；3) 应用块Toeplitz算子理论刻画本质谱与Fredholm区域；4) 在能量空间H^{-1/2}_0(Γ)（对应α=d/2）中，通过算子值Wiener-Hopf分解证明谱为实谱并刻画本质谱外的孤立特征值；5) 应用算子值Szegő极限定理推导大有限截断的渐近谱分布，并基于算子符号建立特征值计数公式；6) 对同心圆环链进行显式解析计算，对圆盘链进行数值近似验证。

Result: 成功建立了Neumann-Poincaré算子在非Lipschitz自相似域上的块Toeplitz表示，确定了当0<α<d时算子符号属于Wiener代数，由此完整刻画了本质谱和Fredholm区域。在能量空间H^{-1/2}_0(Γ)中证明了谱的实性，并通过Wiener-Hopf因子化表征了本质谱外的孤立特征值。利用Szegő极限定理获得了有限截断的渐近谱分布，并建立了基于算子符号的特征值计数公式。通过同心圆环链的解析解和圆盘链的数值近似对理论结果进行了验证。

Conclusion: 本文为具有离散自相似缩放的非Lipschitz域上的Neumann-Poincaré算子建立了完整的谱理论框架。该工作不仅推广了经典Lipschitz域情形的谱结果，还通过Toeplitz算子和Wiener-Hopf技术提供了本质谱、Fredholm区域、孤立特征值及渐近谱分布的精确描述，为后续在哈密顿方程、逆散射和奇异结构声学散射等领域的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We characterise the spectrum of the Neumann-Poincaré operator acting on a mean-zero subspace of the Sobolev-Slobodeckij space $W^{s,p}(Γ)$, where $Γ$ is a non-Lipschitz domain, consisting of an infinite self-similar chain of disjoint smooth domains accumulating at a limit point. We exploit the discrete geometric scaling of the chain to obtain an exact block-Toeplitz representation of the Neumann-Poincaré operator, as well as the single- and double-layer operators. The corresponding operator-valued symbol depends entirely on the single scaling parameter $α=(d-1)/p-s$ and belongs to the Wiener algebra whenever $0<α<d$. This allows us to exploit block-Toeplitz operator theory to characterise the essential spectrum and Fredholm regions within this regime. In the energy space $H^{-1/2}_0(Γ)$, corresponding to $α= d/2$, we prove the spectrum is real and characterise any isolated eigenvalues outside the essential spectrum through an operator-valued Wiener-Hopf factorisation. Furthermore, we apply an operator-valued Szegő limit theorem to derive the asymptotic spectral distribution for large finite truncations and establish an eigenvalue counting formula based on the operator symbol. We illustrate these results through explicit analytical computations for a chain of concentric annuli and numerical approximations for a chain of disks.

</details>


### [190] [Revisiting Picard's proof of the Picard--Weber--Weck selection theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31054)
*Yi-Sheng Lim,Timo Sprekeler,Marcus Waurick*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 论文重新审视了Picard经典论文中的论证，为弱Lipschitz域在欧几里得空间中的Maxwell紧致性提供了独立且自足的证明，绕开了微分形式框架、Gaffney不等式、Calderon延拓定理及PDE正则性理论等复杂工具。作为副产品，仅利用Fourier基的L^2完备性给出了经典Gaffney估计（对立方体）的独立证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Picard的原始论文虽提供了Maxwell紧致性的论证，但依赖于一系列较为繁复的数学工具，如微分形式、Gaffney不等式（需光滑域）、Calderon延拓定理以及PDE或方程组在光滑域上的正则性理论。作者希望给出一个更直接、自足且独立的证明，降低技术门槛，同时深化对该性质本身的理解。

Method: 作者在欧几里得空间中直接构造证明，绕开微分形式等高级框架。对于弱Lipschitz域，采用更初等的技术路径；对于立方体情形，仅利用Fourier基的L^2完备性来验证Gaffney估计，从而建立证明所需的紧致性结论。

Result: 成功在弱Lipschitz域上独立证明了Maxwell紧致性性质，并作为副产品，仅借助Fourier基的L^2完备性即获得了立方体上Gaffney估计的一个新的独立证明。

Conclusion: 本文表明Maxwell紧致性可以在不依赖多种高级分析工具的情况下直接证明，为相关领域提供了更简洁、更自足的技术路径，并有推广潜力。

Abstract: Revisiting the rationale provided by Picard in the seminal paper [13], we provide an independent, self-contained proof of the Maxwell compactness property for weak Lipschitz domains in the Euclidean setting, detouring technical complications like the differential forms setting, Gaffney's inequality for smooth domains, Calderon's extension theorem or regularity theory for PDEs, or systems thereof, on smooth domains that are used in this context. As a by-product we provide an independent proof of the classical Gaffney estimate for cubes using only $L^2$-completeness of the Fourier bases.

</details>


### [191] [Global smooth solutions to the inhomogeneous Landau-Fermi-Dirac equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31071)
*William Golding,Christopher Henderson*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了空间非齐次的Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程（Coulomb势），利用Pauli不相容原理带来的先验L^∞界，传播速度方向的多项式衰减，得到局部质量和能量密度的无条件上界，排除流体力学量中内爆的可能性。结合修正的质量扩散方法实现去饱和，证明了对具有速度多项式衰减的粗糙初值存在全局经典解。该结果与经典Landau和Boltzmann方程形成鲜明对比——后者至今没有已知的可比非微扰全局存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Landau和Boltzmann方程在非微扰框架下的全局存在性问题长期悬而未决，尽管学者们持续努力，仍缺乏可比的全局存在性结果。Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程作为经典Landau方程的量子修正，其Pauli排斥原理提供了额外的L^∞先验界，这可能为突破这一困境提供新的途径。作者旨在利用这一量子结构优势，建立不依赖于微扰假设的全局存在性理论。

Method: （1）利用Pauli不相容原理提供的先验L^∞界，传播速度方向的多项式衰减估计，从而获得局部质量和能量密度的无条件上界；（2）结合修正的质量扩散（mass spreading）方法实现去饱和，即从饱和状态出发恢复解的非退化性；（3）通过弱解和更简单的函数空间构建局部存在性证明，更接近抛物型方程的理论框架；（4）全过程几乎完全自包含，仅依赖线性动力方程的一般稳健估计。

Result: 对具有速度方向多项式衰减的粗糙初值，证明了Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程（Coulomb势）全局经典解的存在性。所得局部质量和能量密度的无条件上界排除了流体力学量中内爆的可能性，即保证了宏观量的非聚焦性。

Conclusion: Landau-Fermi-Dirac方程在非微扰框架下存在全局经典解，与经典Landau和Boltzmann方程形成鲜明对比。本方法几乎完全自足，为组织、适配和应用动力学方程中的线性和非线性估计提供了简明路线图，可能为经典Landau/Boltzmann方程的类似问题提供新的建模和推广思路。

Abstract: We consider the spatially inhomogeneous Landau-Fermi-Dirac equation with Coulomb potential, a quantum modification of the classical Landau equation for fermions. Mathematically, the Pauli exclusion principle manifests as an additional a priori $L^\infty$-bound for solutions. Using this bound, we propagate polynomial decay in velocity, yielding unconditional upper bounds on the local mass and energy densities, thereby ruling out the possibility of implosions in the hydrodynamic quantities. Combining this estimate with a modified mass spreading method that yields desaturation, we deduce the existence of global-in-time classical solutions for rough initial data with polynomial decay in velocity. This result stands in stark contrast to the theory for the classical Landau and Boltzmann equations, for which no comparable nonperturbative global existence result is known despite sustained effort.
  Our treatment is almost entirely self-contained, using only robust, generic estimates for linear kinetic equations. In particular, our proof of local existence, in contrast to prior works, more closely mirrors the theory for parabolic equations using weak solutions and simpler function spaces. It may provide a concise roadmap to organizing, adapting, and applying the various linear and nonlinear estimates to obtain well-posedness for kinetic equations.

</details>


### [192] [Stationary kinetic Krylov-Safonov estimates in space dimension one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31158)
*Yuzhe Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了在一维空间中，非散度形式的稳态动力学Fokker-Planck方程解的内部Hölder正则性，且该估计不依赖于扩散系数的小振动条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学Fokker-Planck方程在非散度形式下的正则性理论尚不完善，尤其是对扩散系数缺少小振动条件限制时，解的Hölder正则性是否仍成立是一个待解决的问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者在一维空间背景下，针对非散度形式的稳态动力学Fokker-Planck方程，证明了其解满足内部Hölder正则性。关键创新在于所获得的估计不依赖于扩散系数的任何小振动条件，说明该正则性结果在更广泛的系数范围内成立。

Result: 成功证明了一维空间中非散度形式稳态动力学Fokker-Planck方程解的内部Hölder正则性，且该估计对扩散系数没有任何小振动假设要求。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在一维情形下，即使扩散系数具有较大的振荡，非散度形式的稳态动力学Fokker-Planck方程的解仍然具有良好的内部Hölder正则性，这扩展了此类方程正则性理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove interior Hölder regularity for solutions of the stationary kinetic Fokker-Planck equation in non-divergence form in space dimension one. The estimate is independent of any small-oscillation condition on the diffusion coefficient.

</details>


### [193] [Finite-energy spacetimes with torus symmetry. Cauchy stability of Einstein-Euler and Einstein-Navier-Stokes areal flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31171)
*Bruno Le Floch,Philippe G. LeFloch*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了具有T²对称性的T³上真空和物质时空的有限能量柯西稳定性，涵盖一般本构方程下的爱因斯坦-欧拉和爱因斯坦-纳维-斯托克斯模型，通过JKL形式的爱因斯坦面积流建立演化-约束系统，并导出了最大原理、不变量域、能量单调性、时空能量估计及全变差估计等关键工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中具有T²对称性的时空是研究宇宙学解和弱正则性理论的经典模型，但此前缺乏在一般物质本构方程下、不依赖具体粘性机制的有限能量柯西稳定性结果。作者旨在建立一套统一的稳定性框架，覆盖真空、无界质量-能量密度及任意有限快度情形，填补该领域在一般方程和正则性下的理论空白。

Method: （1）以对称轨道面积为时间函数，引入JKL爱因斯坦面积流公式——将几何（波映照结构）、物质（双曲平衡律）与扭曲量和切向动量（加权输运方程）耦合为一阶演化-约束系统；（2）通过纳维-斯托克斯势和弛豫率映射构造两类双曲爱因斯坦-纳维-斯托克斯模型（粒子产生模型和耗散能量模型），分别满足发散自由物质应力与总应力-能量守恒；（3）对正则有限能量流建立最大原理、不变量域原理、几何与能量单调性公式、类空和类时能量估计以及全变差估计。

Result: 在膨胀阶段建立了有限能量柯西稳定性，在收缩阶段直到空间体积坍缩前均保持稳定。所得估计独立于粘性（或弛豫）机制，覆盖真空、无界质量-能量密度和任意有限快度情形，并推广到满足参考数学熵不等式的弱正则爱因斯坦-欧拉面积流。

Conclusion: 该研究为一般本构方程下的T²对称爱因斯坦流建立了统一的有限能量稳定性理论，其估计不依赖具体耗散机制，同时适用于真空和物质情形，并延伸至弱正则设定，具有广泛的宇宙学应用前景。

Abstract: We establish finite-energy Cauchy stability for vacuum and matter spacetimes with $T^2$ symmetry on $T^3$, under general constitutive equations satisfying hyperbolicity and mild asymptotic conditions at vacuum and large mass-energy density. First, using the area of the symmetry orbits as a time function, we introduce the JKL formulation of Einstein areal flows}, a first-order evolution-constraint system coupling a wave-map structure for the geometry to hyperbolic matter balance laws and weighted transport equations for the twists and the momentum tangent to the symmetry orbits. Second, we also define two classes of hyperbolic Einstein-Navier-Stokes models, constructed from (as we call them) a Navier-Stokes potential and a relaxation rate map. The proposed particle-production model has divergence-free matter stress and non-negative particle-number production, while the proposed dissipated-energy model conserves particle number and dissipates fluid mass-energy, while an auxiliary stress restores the total stress-energy conservation required by the contracted Bianchi identity. For regular finite-energy (Einstein, Euler, Navier-Stokes) flows, we establish maximum and invariant-domain principles, geometric and energy monotonicity formulas, spacelike and timelike energy estimates, and total-variation estimates. Third, together, these estimates yield finite-energy Cauchy stability in the future expanding regime and, in the contracting regime, until the spacelike volume collapses. They are independent of the viscosity (or relaxation) mechanisms and cover vacuum, unbounded mass-energy density, and arbitrary finite rapidity. They also extend to weakly regular Einstein-Euler areal flows satisfying a reference mathematical entropy inequality.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Light-induced nonconservative static forces in many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29122)
*Jinze Li,Xuemei Yang,Zhiyuan Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究量子多体系统中周期驱动（光场）在固态系统电子序参量上产生的静态力，发现由于耗散（即光吸收）的存在，这些力可以具有非保守分量。作者在激子绝缘体和不相称电荷密度波两个体系中证明了该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解周期驱动（光场）对慢集体自由度产生有效静态力的普遍规律，特别是在存在耗散时这些力的性质是否会发生变化，从而揭示光与电子有序态相互作用的新物理。

Method: 理论上分析量子多体系统中光场对电子序参量产生的静态力，重点考察耗散（光学吸收）如何引入非保守力分量；并通过两个具体例子（激子绝缘体中的带间激发、不相称电荷密度波中的传播光场）进行演示和验证。

Result: 发现光产生的静态力包含由耗散引起的非保守分量。在激子绝缘体中，光通过带间激发对激子序参量的相位产生非保守力，导致相位加速并表现为激子凝聚体光子发射峰移动；在不相称电荷密度波材料中，传播光场对序参量相位产生非保守力，驱动电荷密度波滑动，通过拓扑Thouless泵浦产生直流电流。

Conclusion: 周期驱动产生的有效静态力并非总是保守的，耗散（光吸收）可引入非保守力分量，这一机制能够驱动电子序参量的相位动力学，产生可观测的物理效应（如光子发射峰移动和拓扑泵浦直流电流）。

Abstract: In a quantum many-body system, a periodic drive can often generate effective static forces on the slow collective degrees of freedom. We study the static forces generated by light on electronic order parameters in solid-state systems. We show that the forces can have nonconservative components that originate from dissipation, i.e., optical absorption. This effect is demonstrated in two nontrivial examples. In excitonic insulators, via interband excitations, the light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of the excitonic order parameter. This force leads to an acceleration of the phase, which manifests as a shift of the photon emission peak from an exciton condensate. In materials with an incommensurate charge density wave, a propagating light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of its order parameter. It drives the charge density wave into sliding motion, leading to a DC electric current via topological Thouless pumping.

</details>


### [195] [Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Process Driven by Multiple Dichotomous Noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29226)
*Silvio Kalaj,Dongho Lee,Enzo Marinari,Jae-Hyung Jeon,Pascal Viot,Gleb Oshanin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究由K个独立二值（dichotomous）噪声叠加驱动的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck（OU）过程，得到了平稳位置分布及所有累积量的精确表达式，并揭示了平稳态丰富的结构（紧支撑、代数分支奇点、边缘发散、多重极值）；同时建立了与有界跳随机飞行过程的映射，并分析了大K高斯交叉及无序平均后的稳态性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典OU过程由平衡高斯白噪声驱动，但许多真实系统受有界、非平衡、具有有限关联时间的涨落驱动。作者希望超越高斯白噪声框架，研究具有有限幅度与切换率的二值噪声叠加驱动的OU类过程，以揭示非平衡有界噪声下平稳态的普适结构与统计特征。

Method: 作者对K个独立二值（telegraph）噪声叠加驱动下的广义OU过程进行精确分析：推导平稳位置分布的闭式表达式及全部累积量；建立与异质有界跳随机飞行过程的映射以获得概率论解释；研究K趋于无穷时到高斯统计的交叉行为；对于指数分布淬火无序幅度，精确推导无序平均后的稳态分布。

Result: 平稳分布呈现意外的丰富结构：紧支撑、代数分支点奇异性、边缘发散及多重极值；随机飞行映射提供了清晰的概率解释；大K极限下恢复高斯统计；无序平均后产生带非平凡代数指数的指数尾，稳态性质被根本改变。

Conclusion: 二值噪声叠加驱动的OU过程虽形式上类似经典OU过程，但其平稳态在定性上截然不同，具有丰富的奇异结构与概率内涵；无序的引入进一步从物理本质上改变了稳态的定性行为，为有界非平衡噪声驱动系统的统计物理提供了新的精确结果。

Abstract: We study a generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by a superposition of $K$ independent dichotomous noises with arbitrary fixed amplitudes and switching rates. Unlike the classical Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by equilibrium Gaussian white noise, the present system is governed by bounded nonequilibrium fluctuations with finite correlation times. We obtain exact expressions for the stationary position distribution and all cumulants, and show that the stationary state possesses an unexpectedly rich structure, including compact support, algebraic branch-point singularities, edge divergences, and multiple extrema. We establish a mapping onto a heterogeneous random-flight process with bounded jumps, yielding a transparent probabilistic interpretation of the stationary measure. We further analyze several limiting regimes, including the crossover to Gaussian statistics for large numbers of noise sources. For ensembles with exponentially-distributed quenched amplitudes, we derive exact disorder-averaged stationary distributions and show that disorder fundamentally alters the stationary state, producing exponential tails decorated by algebraic prefactors with non-trivial exponents.

</details>


### [196] [Phase Diagram and Critical Behaviour of the Two-Dimensional Potts Model with Long-Range Quenched Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29710)
*Rodolfo Rocha,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Marco Picco*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有空间相关无序的q=3和q=8 Potts模型，利用Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇的卷绕概率精确刻画了相图，通过磁学可观测量阐明了磁化率双峰结构的物理起源，并验证了所有研究区间的超标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Chippari等人先前提出的相图（其中鉴定了随参数a变化的从有限无序固定点到无限无序固定点的转变），本文旨在通过更精确的方法（卷绕概率）细化该相图，并澄清自旋模型中磁化率双峰结构的来源，同时检验超尺度关系的有效性。

Method: 采用Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇的卷绕概率（wrapping probabilities）来刻画临界性质，使用磁学可观测量（magnetic observables）来分析磁化率行为，研究对象为具有幂律衰变指数a的空间相关无序的q=3和q=8 Potts模型。

Result: 成功细化了q=3和q=8 Potts模型在空间相关无序下的相图，阐明了磁化率双峰结构的物理起源，并确认在所有研究的参数区间内超尺度关系均成立。

Conclusion: 卷绕概率是研究此类无序系统中临界行为的有效工具，该工作深化了对空间相关无序Potts模型临界现象的理解，并为不同无序固定点之间的转变提供了更精确的描述。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram and critical properties of the $q = 3$ and $q = 8$ Potts models with spatially correlated disorder governed by a power-law decay with exponent $a$. Building on the phase diagram proposed by Chippari et al. [3], where a transition from finite-disorder to infinite-disorder fixed points was identified as a function of a, we refine this picture by using wrapping probabilities of Fortuin-Kasteleyn clusters. Furthermore, using magnetic observables, we clarify the physical origin of the double-peak structure in the magnetic susceptibility and establish the validity of the hyper-scaling relation across all investigated regimes.

</details>


### [197] [Representation Transitions Reveal Predictive Structure in Complex Systems: A Trajectory-Level Reconstruction in a Critical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29777)
*Jiaqi Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过独立数值重模拟，系统比较了从标量统计到离散分类、轨迹结构表示、替代检验再到连续低维压缩等多种表征形式对同一临界复杂系统轨迹集预测性能的影响，发现决定预测成败的关键不是相关性强度而是表征是否保留了与预测相关的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的轨迹往往包含传统群体平均标量统计无法捕捉的结构，而表征形式的选择可能从根本上决定预测结构是否可及，但这一点尚未被系统研究。

Method: 对同一临界复杂系统的轨迹集成进行横跨多种表征形式的系统性搜索，包括单一标量统计、两种独立离散多特征分类、轨迹级结构表征与诊断性替代检验、连续低维压缩，并通过独立数值重模拟而非文献综述进行验证，采用八折尺度移除交叉验证评估。

Result: 预测失败并非由相关性不足所致：某个离散表征的最强单特征与目标的相关性（r=0.588）甚至高于成功连续表征的主要统计量（r=0.540），但前者无法区分集成中两个最具后果的案例；而连续表征在八折独立尺度移除验证中稳健成功（r=0.677-0.717，均p<0.001）。决定因素不是相关性强度，而是表征是否保留了与预测相关的结构。

Conclusion: 表征形式的选择决定了复杂系统中的预测结构是否可及，应将其视为独立的科学变量而非下游分析选择。

Abstract: Trajectories in complex systems often contain structure that is not captured by the population-averaged, scalar statistics traditionally used to describe them. Here we reconstruct, through independent numerical re-simulation rather than literature review, a systematic search across representational forms - from single scalar statistics, through two independent discrete multi-feature classification attempts, to a trajectory-level structural representation and a diagnostic surrogate test, to a continuous low-dimensional compression - applied to the same underlying trajectory ensemble in a critical complex system. Predictive failure is not explained by insufficient correlation: one discrete representation's own strongest individual feature correlates with the target more strongly ($r=0.588$) than the eventual successful representation's own headline statistic ($r=0.540$), yet fails to separate the ensemble's two most consequential cases - while the continuous representation succeeds, robustly, across eight independent scale-removal folds ($r=0.677$-$0.717$, all $p<0.001$). The determining factor was not correlation strength but whether the representation preserved the structure relevant to prediction. These results indicate that the choice of representation determines whether predictive structure in complex systems becomes accessible - a scientific variable in its own right, not a downstream analysis choice.

</details>


### [198] [Branching stochastic mechanics. I. Clustering and connected correlations within a branching-process representation of the Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29807)
*Eric Dumonteil,Benoît Bischoff,Alain Letourneau,Loïc Thulliez,Corentin Doutre*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Schrödinger-Nagasawa变换将波函数表示为正向前向和后向扩散场的乘积（其积为Born密度），并将它们提升为分支超过程Φ_F和Φ_B。在Bohm/Fisher控制的分支机制下，场的均值精确再现了Schrödinger动力学，而连通扇区在束缚态中表现为超临界/临界/次临界团簇，自由空间中临界维度d_c=2。进一步让分支率响应于Φ_FΦ_B的涨落，定义符号核C_FB，其反对称分支给出正配对密度ρ_BSM。配对方程分离为集体和相对扇区，谱消去选择Born集体模，涨落抑制选择反相关源通道，产生有限屏蔽长度ξ_FB=√(D_eff/μ_FB)，实现扩展Born密度与有限关联范围的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学中扩展态（如Born密度非局域分布）与粒子化/有限关联尺度（粒子像组织）之间存在概念上的张力。作者试图回答一个根本问题：有限程关联能否隐藏在扩展量子态的统计量中？他们希望构建一个随机框架，在不破坏Born密度（即标准量子统计预言）的前提下，解释粒子状结构的涌现。

Method: 将Schrödinger-Nagasawa变换推广到分支超过程框架。具体而言：(1) 将波函数的正/反向前向扩散场提升为分支超过程，其Bohm/Fisher控制的分支机制生成"替代延续"的谱系；(2) 在Born密度上评估分支率，使场的均值精确匹配Schrödinger演化；(3) 分析连通扇区的尺度行为（束缚与自由空间）以及临界维度；(4) 让分支率对涨落乘积Φ_FΦ_B做出响应，在互反基中分解为光滑参考R加上中心化涨落ψ_F、ψ_B，定义符号核C_FB；(5) 通过配对方程的集体/相对扇区分离，用谱消去和涨落抑制条件确定Born模和反相关源通道，最终导出屏蔽长度ξ_FB。

Result: 研究得到以下核心结果：(1) 均值层面，分支超过程精确复现Schrödinger动力学；(2) 连通扇区在束缚态中表现出超临界-临界-次临界团簇转变，自由空间中临界维度d_c=2；(3) 稳态本征模保持扩展，但次临界团簇的特征尺度缩至约化de Broglie波长；(4) 在对分支上，正配对密度ρ_BSM(x)=-C_FB(x,x)的对角要求匹配Born轮廓，而离对角衰减决定关联程；(5) 关联屏蔽长度ξ_FB=√(D_eff/μ_FB)，其中D_eff为相对扩散率、μ_FB为正弛豫率，证明扩展Born密度与有限关联范围可在单一随机核中共存。

Conclusion: 该工作从随机过程角度建立了量子态统计与粒子像组织之间的桥梁：扩展的Born密度并不排除有限程关联结构的存在——二者通过一个随机符号核C_FB和谐共存。屏蔽长度ξ_FB给定了粒子化关联的尺度，暗示量子态的统计描述与粒子状局部结构的涌现并不矛盾。这一框架可能为量子测量理论、退相干和隐变量解释提供新的数学工具。

Abstract: Can finite-range correlations hide in the statistics of an extended quantum state? The Schrödinger-Nagasawa transform represents the wave function by positive forward and backward diffusion fields whose product is the Born density. We promote them to branching superprocesses, \(Φ_F\) and \(Φ_B\): diffusion samples stochastic paths, whereas Bohm/Fisher-controlled branching generates genealogies of alternative continuations. With rates evaluated on the prescribed Born density, their means reproduce Schrödinger dynamics exactly. The connected sector exhibits supercritical, critical, and subcritical clustering in confinement and has critical dimension \(d_c=2\) in free space. Stationary eigenmodes remain extended; subcritical clusters acquire the reduced de~Broglie scale. We then let the branching rate respond to the fluctuating product \(Φ_FΦ_B\). In the reciprocal basis, the fields equal a smooth reference \(R\) plus centered fluctuations \(ψ_F,ψ_B\), defining the signed kernel \(C_{\rm FB}(x,y)=\mathbb E_ω[ψ_F(x)ψ_B(y)]\). On the anticorrelated branch, \(ρ_{\rm BSM}(x)=-C_{\rm FB}(x,x)\) is the positive paired density. Stationary recovery requires its diagonal to match the Born profile, while off-diagonal decay defines the correlation range. The pair equation splits into collective and relative sectors: spectral cancellation selects the Born collective mode, while suppression of the leading density fluctuation selects the anticorrelated source channel. With relative diffusivity \(D_{\rm eff}\) and positive relaxation rate \(μ_{\rm FB}\), correlations have screening length \(ξ_{\rm FB}=\sqrt{D_{\rm eff}/μ_{\rm FB}}\). Thus an extended Born density and a finite correlation range can coexist in one stochastic kernel, suggesting particle-like organization.

</details>


### [199] [Asymmetry-controlled resonant transport in a Brownian flashing ratchet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29991)
*Sudipta Mandal,Dipanjan Chakraborty,Debasish Chaudhuri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维布朗闪烁棘轮中的定向输运现象，采用分段线性的不对称周期势。通过Fokker-Planck方程的数值求解和布朗动力学模拟，发现稳态电流对切换频率呈非单调依赖，存在共振最大值，其位置取决于势不对称度δ和势垒高度Δ。在中等不对称度(|δ|<0.5)下，电流满足一个将势参数依赖与频率依赖分离的标度形式，通过适当的电流和频率标度可实现数据塌缩。电流幅度随δ线性变化，共振频率满足ν(δ,Δ)=ν₀(Δ)/[1-b₀δ²]，该(1-δ²)⁻¹标度可用不对称势两分支上的耦合弛豫来物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解布朗棘轮中不对称势对定向输运的影响机制，特别是切换频率与势参数如何共同决定输运效率，以及如何通过标度律揭示其普适物理规律。

Method: 采用分段线性不对称周期势的一维布朗闪烁棘轮模型，通过Fokker-Planck方程的数值求解和布朗动力学模拟两种方法，系统地计算稳态电流随切换频率的变化关系，并分析电流与势不对称度δ及势垒高度Δ的标度关系。

Result: 发现稳态电流对切换频率呈非单调依赖（共振行为），共振位置依赖于δ和Δ。在|δ|<0.5时，电流满足分离势参数与频率依赖的标度形式，实现数据塌缩。电流幅度与δ线性相关，共振频率ν=ν₀(Δ)/[1-b₀δ²]，遵循(1-δ²)⁻¹标度律。

Conclusion: 共振频率对势不对称度的(1-δ²)⁻¹依赖可由不对称势两分支间的耦合弛豫机制给出物理诠释，为设计高效布朗棘轮输运装置提供了定量标度依据。

Abstract: We investigate directed transport in a one-dimensional Brownian flashing ratchet with a piecewise-linear asymmetric periodic potential. Numerical solutions of the Fokker--Planck equation and Brownian dynamics simulations reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of the stationary current on the switching frequency, with a resonant maximum whose position depends on the potential asymmetry $δ$ and barrier height $Δ$. For moderate asymmetry, $|δ|<0.5$, the current obeys a scaling form that separates the dependence on the potential parameters from a common frequency dependence, resulting in a data collapse upon appropriate scaling of the current and frequency. The current amplitude varies linearly with $δ$, while the resonance frequency follows $ν(δ,Δ)=ν_0(Δ)/ [1-b_0\, δ^2]$. We interpret the resulting $(1-δ^2)^{-1}$ scaling in terms of coupled relaxation along the two branches of the asymmetric potential, which provides a physical basis for the observed dependence of the resonant frequency on the potential asymmetry.

</details>


### [200] [Chemical potentials from structure factors: II. Charged multi-component mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30060)
*Xiaoyu Wang,Roya Savoj,Musahid Ahmed,Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将S0方法从电中性多组分混合物扩展到带电混合物，通过组合成分空间框架与静态结构因子小波数极限的库仑处理，成功计算了带电多组分混合物的化学势，并应用于多卤化物水盐溶液和熔盐混合物的混合自由能计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电多组分混合物的化学势对电解质热力学至关重要，但从原子模拟中计算这些化学势一直很困难。需要一种能够从平衡分子动力学模拟中可靠计算带电混合物化学势的方法。

Method: 将Part I（中性多组分混合物）中开发的成分空间框架与静态结构因子小波数极限的库仑处理相结合。该方法将热力学相关的中性成分涨落与禁止的宏观电荷涨落分离，并考虑电荷中性约束。

Result: 成功计算了多种多卤化物水盐溶液的化学势，揭示了离子特异性热力学效应；同时计算了熔盐混合物的混合自由能，证明了正确描述长波静电相关性对于这些计算的重要性。

Conclusion: 扩展后的S0方法能够可靠地从平衡分子动力学模拟中计算带电混合物的化学势，其正确描述长波静电相关性是获得准确混合自由能的关键，为电解质和熔盐体系的热力学研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: The chemical potentials of charged multi-component mixtures are central to electrolyte thermo- dynamics, but remain difficult to compute from atomistic simulations. The S0 method enables computing chemical potentials of mixtures from equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations. Here we extend the S0 method to charged mixtures by combining the composition-space framework developed in Part I: Neutral Multi-component Mixtures with a Coulombic treatment of the small- wavenumber limits of static structure factors. This approach separates thermodynamically relevant neutral composition fluctuations from forbidden macroscopic charge fluctuations, and further ac- counts for charge-neutrality constraints. We use the method to compute the chemical potentials of multiple-halide aqueous salt solutions, elucidating the ion-specific thermodynamic effects. We also calculate the mixing free energies of molten salt mixtures, demonstrating the importance of correctly describing long-wavelength electrostatic correlations.

</details>


### [201] [d'Q = TdS for Infinitesimal Irreversible Processes: On the Differential Form of the Clausius Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30421)
*Hiroshi Shimahara*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文证明了在无穷小准静态不可逆过程中，Clausius不等式的正确一阶形式应为等式 d'Q=TdS，而非传统认为的 d'Q < TdS。这从纯热力学角度证明了内部熵产生是二阶或更高阶小量，并在不借助玻尔兹曼关系的情况下确认熵在准静态不可逆过程中仍是定义良好的状态量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学教科书将Clausius不等式的微分形式 d'Q < TdS 应用于无穷小不可逆过程，但这一处理在数学上存在混淆。作者旨在澄清对于无穷小准静态不可逆过程，正确的首阶关系应为等式，从而解决熵作为状态量在准静态不可逆过程中的定义合理性问题。

Method: 通过严格的数学分析，从Clausius不等式的积分形式出发，考察无穷小准静态不可逆过程的首阶展开。作者证明在考虑一阶小量时，d'Q 与 TdS 相等，而内部熵产生项只能出现在二阶或更高阶，从而修正了教科书中的微分形式表述。

Result: 证明了无穷小准静态不可逆过程中正确的首阶形式是 d'Q=TdS，内部熵产生为二阶或更高阶小量。这从纯热力学角度（不依赖玻尔兹曼关系）确认了熵在包含准静态不可逆过程的情形下仍然是良定义的状态量。

Conclusion: 该结果纠正了Clausius不等式微分形式的传统表述，为熵作为状态量的普适性提供了更严谨的热力学理论支撑，并消除了对不可逆过程中熵定义合理性的理论疑虑。

Abstract: Conventionally, the differential form of the Clausius inequality is adopted for infinitesimal irreversible processes. We show that the correct first-order form for infinitesimal quasi-static irreversible processes is the equality d'Q=TdS rather than d'Q < TdS. This provides a purely thermodynamic proof that internal entropy production is second-order or higher and confirms that entropy remains a well-defined state quantity even when quasi-static irreversible processes are included, without resorting to the Boltzmann relation.

</details>


### [202] [Time-delayed feedback turns Arrhenius escape logarithmic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30624)
*Roy Podgaetsky,Vishwajeet Kumar Arnab Pal,Ohad Shpielberg*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现，通过时间延迟反馈在束缚力中引入的非马尔可夫性可以消除热逃逸的Arrhenius指数标度。超过势阱极小值曲率设定的阈值后，延迟会使势阱失稳，热噪声触发的不稳定性随后被确定性放大到边界，产生“弹弓式”逃逸轨迹。此时逃逸时间随势垒呈对数增长，涨落服从Gumbel分布，且最优延迟可使逃逸快于自由扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热逃逸服从Arrhenius定律，逃逸时间随势垒高度呈指数增长，这对于加速活化过程（如化学反应、分子裂解等）构成了巨大瓶颈。作者希望寻找一种可调且实验可行的控制参数来打破这一指数瓶颈，从而加快受势垒阻碍的动力学过程。

Method: 通过分析时间延迟反馈对束缚力（禁锢势）的影响，表明延迟会改变势阱的局部稳定性。当延迟强度超过由势阱极小值曲率决定的阈值时，势阱失稳，此时热噪声仅仅充当触发不稳定的种子，之后逃逸过程由确定性放大机制主导。作者据此推导了逃逸时间的标度关系、涨落统计特性，并研究了最优延迟下的逃逸性能。

Result: 在失稳机制下，逃逸时间从Arrhenius指数标度转变为势垒高度的对数标度；逃逸时间涨落服从Gumbel极值分布；存在最优延迟参数，使得逃逸时间甚至快于自由扩散所需时间。

Conclusion: 时间延迟反馈可作为加速活化过程的可调、实验可行的控制参数，为克服Arrhenius指数瓶颈提供了新的物理机制，对受热激活过程主导的各类系统具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Thermal escape is governed by the Arrhenius law, where the mean escape time scales exponentially with the barrier height. We show that the non-Markovianity induced by time-delayed feedback in the confining force removes this exponential scaling. Beyond a threshold set by the curvature of the minimum, the delay destabilizes the well, and the thermal noise seeds an instability that is subsequently amplified deterministically to the boundary leading to \textit{slingshot} escape trajectories. The escape time becomes logarithmic in the barrier, its fluctuations follow a Gumbel law, and an optimal delay enables escape faster than free diffusion. Our results propose time delay as a tunable and experimentally feasible control parameter for accelerating activated processes.

</details>


### [203] [Effective Hardcore Exclusion Without Exclusion: Two-Species Pair Annihilation Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30630)
*Su-Chan Park,Foster Thompson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个无硬核排斥的粒子模型，通过非线性密度依赖的非对称扩散在A+B→∅湮灭反应体系中重现t^{-1/3}密度衰减标度，证明湮灭反应的密度衰减指数并非仅由孤立物种的输运性质决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前t^{-1/3}衰减行为仅在具有硬核排斥的系统中观察到，作者希望探索该标度行为是否可以不依赖硬核排斥而在更一般的粒子系统中实现，从而深化对反应-扩散系统中反常幂律衰减普适性的理解。

Method: 构建一个无硬核排斥的模型，粒子在单物种孤立情况下表现为超扩散且属于Kardar-Parisi-Zhang普适类。模型中两种物种均经历依赖于局域密度的非线性非对称扩散速率；通过改变微观过程涉及粒子数（二体或三体过程）来调控衰减标度，并解析或数值分析多种设置下密度衰减行为。

Result: 当至少一个物种通过涉及粒子对的微观过程诱导非对称扩散时，密度衰减呈现t^{-1/3}标度；而当非对称过程涉及三粒子组时，则恢复t^{-1/4}的对称扩散标度。这证明即使没有硬核排斥，t^{-1/3}衰减也可实现。

Conclusion: 对湮灭反应中密度衰减指数起决定作用的并非孤立物种的输运性质本身，而是微观非对称过程所涉及的粒子团簇大小，这为反应-扩散系统的普适分类提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Reaction-diffusion systems with two species that mutually annihilate through $A+B\to\emptyset$ reactions display a slow decay of the total density $ρ$ which follows an anomalous power law in low dimensions. For hardcore particles in one dimension, this decay follows $ρ\sim t^{-1/4}$ under symmetric diffusion and $ρ\sim t^{-1/3}$ under asymmetric diffusion without relative bias between the two species. The $t^{-1/3}$ behavior, in particular, has so far been observed exclusively in systems with hardcore exclusion. Here we construct a model of particles without hardcore exclusion that reproduces this same $t^{-1/3}$ scaling. In our model, both species undergo asymmetric diffusion with a rate that depends nonlinearly on the local density, a form of transport that, in the absence of the other species, is superdiffusive and falls into the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang universality class. The scaling behavior of $t^{-1/3}$ occurs when at least one of the two species undergoes asymmetric diffusion induced by microscopic processes involving pairs of particles, while asymmetric processes involving groups of three particles lead to $t^{-1/4}$ scaling associated with symmetric diffusion. This demonstrates that the pair annihilation density decay exponent is not determined exclusively by the transport properties of the isolated particle species.

</details>


### [204] [On the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30871)
*Giuseppe Del Vecchio Del Vecchio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将统计力学中的弛豫问题重构为操作层面上的局部时间统计量（Z(t)的直方图估计）的弛豫。预测对象是对未来样本测量的信念，逻辑依赖于观测者的损失函数选择。熵被赋予学习论解释，即观测者与系统未知过去之间的互信息，其稳态值依赖可用信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学中的弛豫问题缺乏对"操作对象"的明确说明——即什么物理量在弛豫、由谁观测、如何观测。作者希望通过使操作对象显式化（局部时间统计量）来澄清不可逆弛豫的根源，并统一熵、预测与信息三者的关系。

Method: 将弛豫研究对象设定为无钟观测者在均匀随机时间点上对信号Z(t)采样所得到的局部时间统计量（即估计直方图）；预测被定义为对未来的样本外测量结果的信念；引入依赖于观测者选择的损失函数来刻画预测形式；对有限有界N体系统分析全局不可逆弛豫的存在性及特殊初始条件；用观测者与系统未知过去之间的互信息给出熵的学习论诠释。

Result: 对于任意N≥1的有限有界系统，全局不可逆弛豫的预测可以发生，但存在特殊初始条件使弛豫不发生；预测的具体形式取决于观测者所选择的损失函数；熵的稳态值在完全一般性下依赖于观测者可用的信息量。

Conclusion: 弛豫本质上是观测者预测信念的弛豫，而非系统自身属性的变化；熵的新定义赋予其主观信息论含义，其稳态取决于观测者的信息获取程度，揭示了统计力学预测过程中观测者角色的不可忽略性。

Abstract: We reformulate the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics by making explicit what are the \emph{operational} objects subject to relaxation: the local time statistics of the recorded signal $Z(t)$. These local time statistics are simply the estimated histograms of observations $\{Z(t_i)\}_{i=1}^M$ performed at uniformly random times $\{t_i\}_{i=1}^M$ by a clockless observer. The subject of prediction is a belief about a future fresh out-of-sample reading of a measurement outcome whose distribution is inferred from the mathematical model believed to be true. For finite bounded systems of $N\ge 1$ degrees of freedom global irreversible relaxation of predictions can occur but special initial conditions exist. The form of the predictions depends on certain loss functions whose choice is up to the particular observer. Finally, entropy is given a learning interpretation as mutual information between the observer and the unknown past of the system under consideration and, in complete generality, its stationary value depends on the information available.

</details>


### [205] [Kinetic temperatures and inertial effects in a nonequilibrium bead-spring model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30809)
*Jetin E Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一个由两个任意质量耦合珠子构成的非平衡稳态模型，与两个不同温度的热浴接触。通过协方差矩阵方法和欠阻尼Langevin动力学数值模拟，发现不可逆性度量（熵产生、热流）在珠子质量交换下保持不变，而能量存储观测量则依赖质量排列。研究表明动力学温度是系统热力学的自然变量，统一了能量、输运和不可逆性的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡稳态系统中的质量排列如何影响热力学性质，特别是在奇异质量极限下能量观测量的路径依赖性与输运和不可逆性度量的鲁棒性之间的根本区别，以及探索能够统一描述能量、输运和不可逆性的自然变量。

Method: 采用协方差矩阵方法结合欠阻尼Langevin动力学的数值模拟，分析两个任意质量耦合珠子系统的稳态概率分布、热输运和熵产生。通过交换珠子质量来检验各观测量的不变性，并在无穷质量极限下分析基于动力学温度的本构关系。

Result: 发现熵产生和热流在质量交换下保持不变，而能量存储观测量依赖于质量排列。动力学温度的差值控制输运和熵产生，其和通过广义均分关系决定平均能量。在无穷质量极限下，仅用动力学温度表示的本构关系仍有意义。推导出定义平衡型正则分布的有效温度，并发现时间平均观测量收敛显著快于系综平均。

Conclusion: 动力学温度是组织非平衡稳态系统能量、输运和不可逆性的统一变量。该工作揭示了能量观测量在奇异质量极限中的路径依赖性，而输运和不可逆性度量始终保持明确定义，为理解非平衡热力学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate a nonequilibrium steady-state model consisting of two coupled beads with arbitrary masses in contact with two thermal baths at different temperatures. Using a covariance-matrix approach together with numerical simulations of the underdamped Langevin dynamics, we characterize steady-state probability distributions, heat transport, and entropy production. We show that irreversibility measures such as entropy production and heat current are invariant under an exchange of the bead masses, whereas energy-storage observables depend explicitly on the mass arrangement in a symmetrical set up. This reveals a fundamental distinction: energy observables exhibit path dependence in singular mass limits, while transport and irreversibility remain well defined. We show that kinetic temperatures provide the natural variables governing the thermodynamics of the system: their difference controls transport and entropy production, while their sum determines the mean energy via a model specific generalized equipartition relation. In the infinite-mass limit, only constitutive relations expressed in terms of kinetic temperatures remain meaningful. Thus, energy, transport, and irreversibility are unified through kinetic temperatures as the organizing variables. We also derive an effective temperature that defines an equilibrium-like canonical distribution. Finally, we analyze the notion of ergodicity and show that the time-averaged observables converge significantly faster than the ensemble averages.

</details>


### [206] [Inertia-Driven Information Flow and Symmetry Breaking in a Nonequilibrium Two-Bead System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30849)
*Jetin E. Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个耦合到两个热浴的非平衡双珠系统中的信息流产生机制，发现该系统在过阻尼和欠阻尼两种动态区域都能作为信息流发生器，欠阻尼动态揭示出在热不对称-惯性参数空间中沿特定路径存在信息流的发散现象，而这一现象在过阻尼极限下是隐藏的。通过对称性微扰分析，作者发现了类似Ginzburg-Landau框架的几何结构，其中对称参考态根据扰动方向可对应最小值或最大值，且质量不对称会移动产生的极大值。Hessian特征值和特征向量分析揭示了主模的能级交叉和沿恒定扩散路径的双峰性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非平衡纳米系统中信息流的产生机制，为设计信息引擎和类麦克斯韦妖机制提供理论依据；同时揭示惯性和微观异质性如何塑造非平衡系统中的信息景观。

Method: 采用两珠系统模型分别耦合两个热浴，在过阻尼和欠阻尼两种动态框架下分析信息流；通过对称性微扰分析信息流响应景观，构建类似Ginzburg-Landau的几何框架；运用Hessian矩阵特征值-特征向量分析研究质量不对称对响应极大值的影响和主模行为。

Result: 欠阻尼动力学揭示了在热不对称-惯性参数空间中特定路径上标度信息流的发散行为，这是过阻尼极限下被隐藏的现象。对称参考状态根据扰动方向可表现为最小值或最大值；过阻尼平坦景观在惯性的作用下发展出有限曲率；质量不对称使极大值发生移动；Hessian分析揭示了主模能级接触和恒定扩散路径上的双峰性。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了理解惯性和微观异质性如何塑造非平衡系统信息景观的最小框架，为信息引擎和类恶魔机制在纳米机器中的实现提供了可能路径，并可扩展到更复杂的异质网络系统。

Abstract: We investigate information-flow generation in a nonequilibrium two-bead system coupled to two heat baths. We show that the system acts as an information-flow generator in both overdamped and underdamped regimes, with the underdamped dynamics revealing a divergence of the scaled information flow along specific paths in the thermal asymmetry--inertia parameter space that is hidden in the overdamped limit. This information generation suggests a possible route toward information engines and demon-like mechanisms in nanomachines. A symmetry-perturbation analysis of the response landscape of information flow reveals a geometric structure reminiscent of a Ginzburg--Landau framework: the symmetric reference state can correspond to a minimum or maximum depending on the perturbation direction, while the flat overdamped landscape develops a finite curvature under inertia. Mass asymmetry shifts the resulting maxima, and Hessian eigenvalue and eigenvector analysis reveals level touching of principal modes and bimodality along a constant-diffusion path. These results establish a minimal framework for understanding how inertia and microscopic heterogeneity shape information landscapes in nonequilibrium systems, with potential extensions to more complex heterogeneous networks.

</details>


### [207] [Compensation in continuous symmetric trilayered planar ferrimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30941)
*Olivia Mallick,Muktish Acharyya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究了三层A-B-A型各向异性XY磁模型的平衡性质，发现系统在特定参数范围内表现出高温临界行为和低温补偿行为，并绘制了完整相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三层各向异性XY磁模型的平衡性质，特别是其临界行为和补偿行为，以理解多层磁性系统的相变特征。

Method: 采用Metropolis单自旋随机更新算法的蒙特卡洛模拟，研究层内铁磁、层间反铁磁相互作用的三层各向异性（单点）XY模型。

Result: 系统在特定相互作用参数范围内表现出高温临界行为（亚晶格磁化和总磁化均消失）和低温补偿行为（总磁化为零但亚晶格磁化非零）。补偿温度和临界温度均随单点各向异性的增大而增大。有限尺寸研究表明在临界温度附近存在相关性增长。

Conclusion: 三层A-B-A型各向异性XY磁模型在特定参数下兼具临界与补偿行为，各向异性能调控两种特征温度，并绘制了综合相图。

Abstract: The trilayered (A-B-A type) anisotropic (single site) XY magnetic model is considered with the intra-plane ferromagnetic and
  the inter-plane antiferromagnetic interactions. The equilibrium properties of such trilayered anisotropic XY system has been studied by
  Monte Carlo simulation with Metropolis single spin (randomly chosen) update algorithm. In a given range of interaction parameters, the system has been found to exhibit the high temperature {\it critical} (vanishing sublattice magnetisations and total magnetisation) and low temperature {\it compensation} (vanishing total magnetisation even for non-vanishing sublattice magnetisations) behaviours. The compensation temperature and the critical temperature both are found to increase with the increase of single site anisotropy. The comprehensive phase diagram is drawn. The finite size study reveals the growth of correlations near the critical temperature.

</details>


### [208] [Overcoming critical slowing down in frustrated spin systems by learned multiscale sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31114)
*Gabriele Bandini,Giulio Biroli,Patrick Charbonneau,Andrea Gambassi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出用小波条件重整化群（WCRG）采样方法学习而非构造集体涨落的簇，从而克服传统簇算法在阻挫系统中失效的根本限制。在二维软自旋阻挫模型中，WCRG以O(log2 L)的采样复杂度复现系统在各相的主要统计性质，远超标准局部MCMC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swendsen-Wang和Wolff等传统簇算法是缓解临界慢化的最成功MCMC方法，但在即使极弱阻挫存在下也会失效。作者希望绕过这一根本局限，通过机器学习的方式学习相关簇结构，而非人工构造。

Method: 利用小波条件重整化群（WCRG）采样方法：学习阻挫二维软自旋模型中集体涨落的概率分布，然后从粗尺度到细尺度递归生成构型，通过采样条件小波分布实现多尺度生成。

Result: WCRG方法在不同相中均能复现系统主要统计性质，包括局域场分布和结构因子。在类似Ising临界点处，每个尺度的条件动力学仅需O(1)次扫描即可去相关，整体采样复杂度为O(log2 L)，远优于标准局部MCMC。同时揭示了采样精度取决于能量模型的表达能力这一关键权衡。

Conclusion: 学习式多尺度采样能够克服传统簇算法无法处理的阻挫系统中的临界慢化问题，但WCRG方法的快速采样精度受限于用于估计小波条件分布的能量基模型的表达能力。

Abstract: Cluster algorithms, such as the Swendsen--Wang and Wolff methods, are among the most successful MCMC methods for mitigating critical slowing down in statistical systems. These constructive cluster algorithms, however, fail in the presence of even extremely weak frustration. Here, we sidestep this fundamental limitation by learning rather than constructing the relevant clusters. Specifically, we use the wavelet conditional renormalization group (WCRG) sampling method to learn the probability distribution of collective fluctuations of a frustrated two-dimensional soft-spin model. Configurations are then generated recursively from coarse to fine scales by sampling conditional wavelet distributions. The WCRG method reproduces the main statistical properties of the system across different phases, including the local-field distribution and the structure factor. At an Ising-like critical point, the conditional dynamics remains decorrelated within $\mathcal{O}(1)$ sweeps at each scale, yielding an overall sampling complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\log_2 L)$, thus making WCRG much more efficient than standard local MCMC methods. These results show that learned multiscale sampling can overcome critical slowing down in frustrated systems for which conventional cluster algorithms fail. By assessing the sampling accuracy of different observables, we also clarify the main tradeoff of the WCRG method: the accuracy of the fast sampling scheme depends on the expressiveness of the energy-based model used to estimate the wavelet conditional distributions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Reciprocity Separates Gradient Flow from Rotation in Conservative Physical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30778)
*Ruiwu Niu,Xiaowen Bi,Michaël Antonie van Wyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究物理学习系统中决定系统遵循传统梯度下降轨迹还是独特学习轨迹的机制。以有向分层输运网络为规范模型，发现守恒约束限制学习方向，邻接匹配产生对称物理响应，非负模态反馈导致互易闭环响应和重加权梯度流，而反对称边界分量产生旋转闭环响应，使学习路径可转向。数值检验证实了响应结构并揭示了轨迹漂移如何抵消局部优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学习允许可训练材料或网络利用自身物理响应传播误差信号，减少对单独编程的反向计算需求。核心问题是：什么决定这类系统遵循传统梯度下降还是沿着本质不同的学习轨迹演化？理解这种差异对于物理学习系统的设计与可预测性至关重要。

Method: 采用有向分层输运网络作为规范模型，其中每个节点重新分配固定量的流量，学习过程保持正性和总质量守恒。通过匹配响应类分析，研究邻接匹配如何产生对称物理输出响应，以及非负模态反馈和反对称边界分量如何分别产生互易与旋转的闭环响应。使用数值一致性检验验证响应结构，并预测跨新网络族的局部效应符号。

Result: 研究发现：守恒约束仅限制允许的学习方向；匹配响应类中邻接匹配给出对称物理响应；非负模态反馈产生互易闭环响应和重加权梯度流；反对称边界分量使闭环响应旋转化，学习路径可转向。转向并非天然有益，其有限步效应由局部曲率决定，累积效应取决于步长选择和路径上访问的新状态。数值检验复现了精确响应结构，预测了局部效应符号，并展示了轨迹漂移如何抵销局部优势。

Conclusion: 这些结果区分了守恒、互易性和非互易性在物理学习中的各自作用，阐明了物理学习系统偏离或保持传统梯度下降轨迹的条件，为理解和设计物理学习系统提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Physical learning lets a trainable material or network use its own physical response to carry error signals, reducing the need for a separately programmed backward computation. We ask what determines whether such a system follows conventional gradient descent or evolves along a genuinely different learning trajectory. Our canonical model is a directed layered transport network in which every node redistributes a fixed amount of flow, so learning preserves positivity and total mass. In this model, conservation constrains only the allowable learning directions. Within the matched response class studied here, adjoint matching gives the physical output response a symmetric form. Non-negative mode-wise feedback then produces a reciprocal closed-loop response and a reweighted gradient flow. Adding an antisymmetric boundary component makes the closed-loop response rotational: the learning path can turn while the error driving that update still decreases at that moment. Turning is not automatically beneficial. Its finite-step effect is set by local curvature, and its accumulated effect also depends on step selection and on the new states visited along the path. Numerical consistency checks reproduce the exact response structure, predict the sign of the local effect across new network families, and show how trajectory drift can negate a local advantage. These results separate the roles of conservation, reciprocity, and nonreciprocity in physical learning.

</details>


### [210] [Reproducible macroscopic dynamics in a closed-loop human-AI learning system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30946)
