Result: 发现一致的不对称披露效应：Qwen3-14B(+13.9pp)、Qwen3-32B(+13.0pp)、Qwen3-235B(+5.8pp)等模型泄漏恶意指令的概率显著高于良性指令。LLM监督器在全部8个模型中复现该不对称性，在82%的检测器判定为干净的恶意思维链中识别出具体指令。CAA因果实验表明转向向量可双向调控隐藏行为，且良性/恶性向量高度相似(余弦0.804-0.970)，说明存在共享的隐藏方向。

Conclusion: 思维链监督存在系统性盲区：模型对恶意指令的披露不对称性意味着基于思维链的AI监督可能无法可靠地对齐模型行为。但由于恶意与良性指令激活的是相同的隐藏方向，这为设计缓解策略提供了针对性方向——干预该共享方向可能同时改善两类合规性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning traces are increasingly proposed as a mechanism for AI oversight: a monitor inspecting a model's reasoning can, in principle, detect misbehavior invisible from outputs alone. This assumes CoT surfaces what a model is instructed to do regardless of the instructions given. We test this assumption along two axes. First, we introduce the Instruction-Compliance Gap (ICG): the difference in probability that a model's CoT explicitly references a hidden system prompt directive when that directive is malign versus benign. Across 100 task pairs and 8 frontier reasoning models from 5 families, we find consistent asymmetric disclosure, a higher probability of leaking malign hidden instructions than benign ones, in Qwen3-14B (Wilcoxon $p=0.0001$, $+13.9$pp), Qwen3-32B ($p=0.0011$, $+13.0$pp), Qwen3-235B ($p=0.035$, $+5.8$pp), and similar results with MiniMax-M2.5 and DeepSeek-R1. The detector has 100% precision against two independent blinded labelling passes, and an LLM monitor reading only the reasoning trace reproduces the asymmetry in all 8 models against directive-free controls, identifying the specific directive in 82% of malign traces which the detector classifies as clean. Second, steering vectors extracted in MiniMax-M2.5 via Contrastive Activation Addition causally induce hiding from bare prompts and suppress it from prompts that would otherwise produce it, replicating in Qwen3-14B under a pre-registered design. Benign and malign-derived hiding vectors are highly similar (cosine $0.804$ in MiniMax-M2.5; $0.970$ in Qwen3-14B), implying that in these models the disclosure asymmetry arises from differential activation of a shared hiding direction rather than separate mechanisms.

</details>


### [244] [Titans-QFWP: A Regime-Aware Hybrid Quantum Fast Weight Programmer for Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29093)
*Ming-Kai Hung,Jun-Hao Chen,Yun-Cheng Tsai,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Titans-QFWP，一种混合强化学习架构，将量子快速权重编程器与Titans风格记忆（持久性、意外、遗忘）相结合用于自适应投资组合优化。在468只S&P 500股票上以约3000个可训练参数的等参数计数基准下，取得中位数年化收益率0.4260、Calmar比率8.5504、信息比率0.8427的优异表现。消融实验表明量子门控重塑了记忆组件角色，支持回撤控制和上行潜力保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投资组合优化方法在高维市场特征下难以有效建模，需要一种能够同时处理复杂市场动态、实现自适应配置、并且在参数受限条件下保持稳健性能的强化学习架构。

Method: 提出Titans-QFWP混合架构：(1)集成量子快速权重编程器与Titans风格记忆系统（Persistence/Surprise/Forgetting）；(2)引入增强A3C^2框架，包含匈牙利对齐K-means聚类和缩放对数收益奖励机制，处理高维市场特征。

Result: 在468只S&P 500股票、约3000个可训练参数的EPC基准下，中位数ARR为0.4260、Calmar比率为8.5504、IR为0.8427。消融显示量子门控重新分配了记忆组件功能：Persistence用于回撤控制，Surprise贡献收益生成，Forgetting提供额外稳定化。

Conclusion: 量子表示稳定化使模型能够在市场回撤期间进行防御性配置，同时保留上行潜力，验证了量子-记忆混合架构在自适应投资组合优化中的有效性。

Abstract: We propose Titans-QFWP, a hybrid reinforcement learning architecture integrating a Quantum Fast Weight Programmer with Titans-style memory (Persistence, Surprise, and Forgetting) for adaptive portfolio optimization. To address high-dimensional market features, we introduce an enhanced A3C^2 framework with Hungarian-aligned K-means clustering and scaled log-return rewards. Evaluated on 468 S&P 500 stocks under an Equal-Parameter-Count (EPC) benchmark with approximately 3,000 trainable parameters, Titans-QFWP achieves strong performance (median ARR 0.4260, Calmar 8.5504, IR 0.8427). Ablation results reveal that quantum gating fundamentally reshapes memory component roles, with Persistence supporting drawdown control, Surprise contributing to return generation, and Forgetting providing additional stabilization. By stabilizing these quantum representations, the model enables defensive allocation during market drawdowns while preserving upside potential.

</details>


### [245] [Development of an Autonomous AI Coding Agent using Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) and Gemini LLM Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29096)
*Pravin Game,Vipin Ramakrishnan,Prathamesh Wagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自主AI编码智能体，将Gemini 2.5 Flash LLM与定制化蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）结合，通过"自批评"评估器将代码生成建模为搜索过程，在复杂逻辑提示上达到92%的成功率，优于标准零样本生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM的"一次性"生成能力在处理复杂编程任务时存在逻辑幻觉和算法性能下降的问题，无法满足从自然语言生成安全、生产级代码的自动化需求。

Method: 该框架使用Gemini 2.5 Flash API提供核心推理能力，采用定制化的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）将代码生成视为搜索问题；通过"自批评"（Self-Critic）评估器测试不同的实现路径，并根据准确性和难度进行排序，利用反向传播来改进决策框架；整体通过基于Flask的Web界面提供即时反馈和语法高亮功能。

Result: 实验结果表明，MCTS-based方法在复杂逻辑提示上的成功率达到92%，超越了标准零样本生成模型。

Conclusion: 该自主AI编码智能体通过结构化决策机制有效弥合了LLM生成内容与生产就绪软件之间的差距，为复杂代码生成任务提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The ongoing changes in software engineering requirements have created a substantial need for automated tools which can create secure source code from natural language input. The performance of traditional Large Language Models (LLMs) becomes limited by their "one-shot" capability which results in logical hallucinations together with reduced algorithmic performance during complicated operations. The research presents an autonomous AI Coding Agent which establishes a connection between LLM-generated content and production-ready software through its organized methodology for decision making. Our framework uses the Gemini 2.5 Flash API for essential reasoning capabilities while employing a tailored Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) method to solve code generation challenges as a search operation. The agent uses a "Self-Critic" evaluator system to test different implementation methods which it ranks according to their accuracy and difficulty level before it improves its operational framework through backpropagation. The system operates through a Flask-based web interface which delivers instant feedback together with syntax highlighting features. Our experimental results show that the MCTS-based method achieves a 92% success rate on complex logical prompts while surpassing standard zero-shot generation models.

</details>


### [246] [Temperature-Adaptive Transformed Teacher Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29099)
*Hiroaki Aizawa,Yoshikazu Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对TTM（Transformed Teacher Matching）的样本级逆温度自适应更新方法，通过局部最小化温度缩放教师分布与学生预测之间的KL散度来实现。该方法推导出逆温度的一阶和二阶闭式导数，可用中心化logits的方差和协方差统计量表达，从而得到高效的曲率感知更新，仅需一次softmax计算和常数次类加权求和。实验表明该自适应方法普遍提升TTM和WTTM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温度缩放在知识蒸馏中至关重要但其作用尚未被完全理解。TTM虽然通过仅对教师分布施加温度缩放并解释为带隐式Rényi熵正则化的标准蒸馏，但仍依赖固定温度，未说明如何针对单个样本自适应教师侧温度，这限制了蒸馏性能的进一步提升。

Method: 提出样本级逆温度更新机制：通过局部最小化温度缩放教师分布与学生预测之间的KL散度来更新逆温度；推导出关于逆温度的一阶和二阶闭式导数，并将其表示为在变换教师加权下中心化教师与学生logits的方差和协方差统计量；实现曲率感知的高效更新，每次仅需一次softmax评估和常数次类级加权求和。

Result: 在标准图像分类蒸馏基准上的实验表明，所提出的温度自适应方法普遍改善了TTM和WTTM的性能，并且与先前的温度自适应蒸馏基线相比具有竞争力或更优表现。

Conclusion: 通过样本级逆温度自适应更新，TTM框架得以完善，解决了固定温度缺乏适应性的局限，提供了一种高效且有效的温度调节机制，在图像分类蒸馏任务中取得了优于或可比于现有温度自适应方法的性能。

Abstract: Temperature scaling is a core component of knowledge distillation, yet its role and effect are still not fully understood. Transformed Teacher Matching (TTM) clarifies the role of temperature scaling by applying it only to the teacher distribution and interpreting the resulting objective as standard distillation with an implicit Rényi entropy regularization on the student. However, TTM still relies on a fixed temperature and does not specify how the teacher-side temperature should be adapted for individual samples. In this paper, we introduce a sample-wise inverse-temperature update for TTM by locally minimizing the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the temperature-scaled teacher distribution and the student's prediction. We derive closed-form first and second derivatives with respect to the inverse temperature, and show that they can be expressed using variance and covariance statistics of centered teacher and student logits under the transformed teacher weighting. This yields an efficient curvature-aware update that requires one softmax evaluation and a constant number of class-wise weighted sums. Experiments on standard image classification distillation benchmarks show that our temperature adaptation generally improves TTM and WTTM, while remaining competitive with or outperforming prior temperature-adaptive distillation baselines.

</details>


### [247] [Explainable Machine Learning for Broadband Adoption Disparities: Tract-Level Prediction and SHAP-Based Factor Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29110)
*Xiao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的机器学习框架，用于在美国普查区（census-tract）粒度上刻画宽带采用差异。使用65个来自ACS 2022的社会经济、人口和基础设施特征训练LightGBM模型，通过空间五折交叉验证（R²=0.533）和51州留出交叉验证（R²=0.525）验证泛化能力。TreeSHAP分析揭示收入和受教育程度是主导因素，SHAP聚类识别出三种探索性因子画像：连接良好中等型（约49K区）、负担能力受限严重型（约21K区）和乡村老年型（约13K区）。作为筛选工具，ML方法在前10%区中捕获38.0%的总采用缺口，优于仅收入启发式的35.2%（+2.8个百分点），并在遗憾缩减上缩小了19%的差距。时间稳定性检验确认排名稳定性（rho=0.784）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国通过《基础设施投资与就业法案》拨款约650亿美元用于宽带扩展，但针对这些投资的目标定位缺乏循证方法。作者旨在将解释性机器学习引入宽带接入政策规划，弥补传统启发式方法（如仅基于收入）在识别高风险区域方面的不足，为政策制定者提供基于数据细粒度定位的决策支持工具。

Method: 使用LightGBM模型，基于ACS 2022的65个社会经济、人口和基础设施特征，对83,359个普查区的宽带采用差异进行建模。采用空间五折交叉验证评估性能，并用51州留出交叉验证验证跨区域泛化。利用TreeSHAP进行特征归因分析，识别主导因子组；通过SHAP值聚类揭示三种不同的因子画像。将ML选择与收入仅启发式比较作为筛选工具，并用县块bootstrap进行统计显著性检验。最后通过在ACS 2017上训练并预测ACS 2022进行时间稳定性检查。

Result: 模型在空间五折交叉验证中达到R²=0.533和Spearman rho=0.763；州留出验证R²=0.525。TreeSHAP显示收入和受教育程度是主导因子（工程化的交互项吸收了其组成特征的归因）。SHAP聚类发现三种因子画像：Well-Connected Moderate（~49K）、Affordability-Limited Severe（~21K）和Rural-Elderly（~13K）。ML筛选工具在前10%区中捕获38.0%的总缺口，优于收入仅启发式的35.2%（+2.8个百分点，p<0.002），遗憾缩减19%。时间稳定性rho=0.784。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是逐普查区的因子分解：SHAP识别哪些特征组（收入/教育、乡村性、年龄）与每个普查区预测的采用缺口最密切相关，从而为差异化调查和针对性宽带投资提供决策支持。模型在预测精度、泛化能力和时间稳定性方面均表现良好，证明了ML方法可以超越简单的收入启发式，作为政策筛选工具具有实际价值。

Abstract: The United States has allocated approximately $65 billion through the Infrastructure Investment and Jobs Act for broadband expansion, yet evidence-based methods for targeting these investments remain underdeveloped. This paper presents an explainable machine learning framework for profiling broadband adoption disparities at census-tract granularity across 83,359 tracts nationwide. Using 65 socioeconomic, demographic, and infrastructure features derived from the American Community Survey 2022, we train a LightGBM model under spatial five-fold cross-validation, achieving R^2 = 0.533 and Spearman rho = 0.763; state-held-out cross-validation (51 folds) confirms generalization (R^2 = 0.525). TreeSHAP analysis identifies income and education as the dominant factor group (with the engineered interaction term absorbing attribution from its constituent features), and SHAP-based clustering reveals three exploratory factor profiles: Well-Connected Moderate (~49K tracts), Affordability-Limited Severe (~21K tracts), and Rural-Elderly (~13K tracts). As a screening tool, ML-based tract selection captures 38.0% of the total adoption gap within the top 10% of tracts versus 35.2% for income-only heuristics (+2.8 pp, p < 0.002, county-block bootstrap); in regret-reduction terms, the model closes 19% of the remaining gap between income-only and oracle selection. The primary contribution is the per-tract factor decomposition: SHAP identifies which feature groups (income/education, rurality, age) are most strongly associated with each tract's predicted gap, and informs differentiated investigation. A temporal stability check, training on ACS 2017 and predicting ACS 2022 with zero survey-year overlap, confirms ranking stability (rho = 0.784, noting hyperparameters tuned on 2022 data).

</details>


### [248] [Locked at the Entrance, Open Inside: Where RLVR Narrows the Solution Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29188)
*Qiancheng Zhou,Ruizhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）如何压缩推理解决方案空间的问题。通过Countdown任务（可穷举枚举解空间），作者发现覆盖率的显著下降（最高67%）主要发生在推理轨迹的入口处（首个操作数/运算符选择），而非后续执行阶段。入口前缀的恢复可使低访问族的完成率提升一个数量级以上。定位到入口后，作者发现晚期层参数插值可将解覆盖率提升37%且不损失pass@1。早期熵塌缩在多个数学基准和更大模型上普遍存在，但并非强化学习推理优化的必然产物——SFT和分阶段SFT-DPO-RLVR管道可保留更多早期熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽提升pass@1但导致策略解空间收缩，削弱测试时缩放收益。作者希望弄清收缩发生在推理轨迹的哪个环节——是策略无法访问有效的解族（接入失败），还是无法执行已启动的计算（执行失败），以便针对性干预。

Method: 使用Countdown任务，其解空间可按首操作数和运算符枚举为离散的入口族。在PPO（Qwen2.5-3B）和GRPO（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct）两种训练设置下，分析解覆盖率变化、入口前的逐token似然变化，并通过注入未选择的入口前缀来恢复完成率。还测试了表层提示、晚期层参数插值等干预手段，并在6个数学基准（7B/14B模型）上验证早期熵塌缩的普遍性，与SFT及SFT-DPO-RLVR管道对比。

Result: 解覆盖率最多下降67%，即便在跨检查点都解对的问题上也会减半。收缩集中在入口：首个算术运算前的逐token似然变化比下游推理大11x-16x。注入未选择的入口前缀可将低访问族的完成率从0.018恢复到0.212（PPO）。表层提示无效，但晚期层参数插值将覆盖率提升37%且不损失pass@1。早期熵塌缩在6个基准、7B/14B模型上普遍存在，但SFT基线保留两倍以上覆盖率，SFT-DPO-RLVR管道保留早期熵。

Conclusion: 推理广度丢失发生在入口处而非推理过程中（"lost at the door, not inside the room"）。早期分支锁定（early branch locking）是RLVR推理优化中的机制性问题，但可以通过入口定向干预缓解，且不是推理优化的必然副产品。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) substantially improves single-sample accuracy (pass@1) but causes the policy's solution space to contract, diminishing the returns of test-time scaling. In this work, we investigate where inside a reasoning trajectory this breadth is lost: does the policy fail to access a valid solution family, or does it fail to execute computation once initiated? To disentangle access from execution, we analyze the Countdown task, whose solution space can be exhaustively enumerated into discrete entrance families defined by the first operand and operator, across PPO on Qwen2.5-3B and GRPO on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct. Across both training setups, solution coverage falls by up to 67%, halving even on problems solved across all checkpoints. We show that this contraction is heavily concentrated at the entrance: per-token likelihood shifts are 11x--16x larger prior to the first arithmetic operation than during downstream reasoning. Supplying only an unselected entrance prefix restores completion rates in low-access families by over an order of magnitude (0.018 -> 0.212 under PPO), demonstrating that alternative solutions remain executable but are no longer initiated. Guided by this localization, we find that while surface prompting fails to recover diversity, entrance-targeted interventions succeed: late-layer parameter interpolation with early checkpoints increases solution coverage by 37% at no loss in pass@1. Finally, we show that early-step entropy collapse recurs across six math benchmarks with 7B and 14B models, but is not an inevitable byproduct of reasoning optimization: an SFT baseline preserves more than double the coverage, and staged SFT--DPO--RLVR pipelines retain early-step entropy. In summary, reasoning breadth is lost at the door, not inside the room. Code: https://github.com/ershiyidian/early-branch-locking.

</details>


### [249] [HalluPrism: When Multimodal Uncertainty Should Diagnose, Not Decide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29193)
*Aman Prakash,Sourish Dasgupta,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HalluPrism，一种基于行为诊断的多模态大模型幻觉归因框架，通过视觉退化、空白图像替换、基座/关系检测三类探针生成（V, L, A）联合签名，显著提升失败族分类AUROC（从0.78到0.95），并区分了失败诊断与弃权评分的不同目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型对不同失败原因产生的错误答案可能赋予相似的置信度，导致单一置信度无法有效区分失败模式。传统的置信度评估无法揭示错误背后的结构性原因，需要一种行为诊断方法来刻画失败的具体类型。

Method: 提出HalluPrism诊断框架：(1) 对答案进行视觉退化（visual degradation）处理以测量视觉扰动敏感性V；(2) 用空白图像替换以测量图像移除后置信度保持度L；(3) 执行基座/关系检查以度量稳定性A。由此生成三维签名（V, L, A），并在四个基准、四个MLLM、58K+样本上进行验证，使用XGBoost进行联合分析。

Result: 在58K+样本上，图像移除置信度保持（L）最为普遍，而基座/关系探针不稳定性（A）更能区分失败族。48个源-目标检查中仅18个对角线对齐，需将坐标联合解读。联合签名将HallusionBench的AUROC从0.634提升到0.769，VizWiz从0.707提升到0.817。池化XGBoost分析中AUROC从标量置信度的0.78提升到三维签名的0.95，加入置信度后达0.97。但该签名不能自动改善正确性排序，三种直接标量化方法可能损害排序性能。

Conclusion: 多模态不确定性应先刻画失败结构再决定是否弃权或纠正；失败诊断与弃权评分是两个不同目标，需要分开处理。直接标量化签名用于排序可能适得其反，提示应保留结构化的失败表征信息。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can assign similar confidence to answers that fail for different reasons. We propose HalluPrism, a behavioral diagnostic that re-runs an answer after visual degradation, blank-image replacement, and grounding or relation checks. These targeted probes yield a signature over visual-perturbation sensitivity (V ), image-removal confidence retention (L), and grounding/relation-probe instability (A). Across 58K+ examples from four benchmarks and four MLLMs, image-removal confidence retention is most prevalent, while grounding/relation-probe instability better separates failure families. Only 18 of 48 source-target checks are diagonally aligned, so the coordinates should be interpreted jointly rather than as independent causal sources. With the dataset fixed, the joint signature improves failure-family AUROC from 0.634 to 0.769 on HallusionBench and from 0.707 to 0.817 on VizWiz, with smaller gains on POPE and VSR. In pooled XGBoost analysis, AUROC rises from 0.78 with scalar confidence to 0.95 with (V, L, A) and 0.97 when confidence is added. The same signature does not automatically improve correctness ranking. The three tested direct scalarizations can harm it. These results separate failure diagnosis from abstention scoring: multimodal uncertainty should characterize failure structure before it is used to decide whether to abstain or correct.

</details>


### [250] [PokaiTrainer: Scaling Belief-State Search to Competitive Pokémon VGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29197)
*Max Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了PokaiEngine和PokaiTrainer，将决策时均衡搜索扩展到官方宠物小精灵双打（VGC）这一打破传统可解子游戏假设的博弈场景，最终在Showdown天梯上达到约59%的胜率并进入格式前500名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策时均衡搜索在扑克等场景中已实现超人类水平，但依赖三个关键假设：每步决策动作有限、随机性仅来自发牌、玩家轮流行动。VGC双打格式同时打破这三个假设——双方从数百个联合动作中同时选择、每个联合动作产生数百种随机结果、对手的备用宝可梦和属性分配隐藏。因此需要探索如何在此类复杂博弈中构建强力的智能体。

Method: 论文提出两个核心组件：1) PokaiEngine，基于Rust的战斗引擎，通过单次遍历计算联合动作的完整加权结果分布，与Pokémon Showdown保持约99%的一致性且计算成本远低于采样；2) PokaiTrainer，将Student of Games算法适配到该规模，将每个决策建模为公共信念状态上的贝叶斯矩阵博弈，并在显式计算预算下逐步扩展子博弈树。

Result: 在Showdown三局两胜天梯上，该智能体在150场对局中对平均约1320 Elo的人类玩家取得59%的胜率，稳定在1350-1400 Elo区间，巅峰时期短暂进入该格式的前500名。

Conclusion: 决策时均衡搜索框架可以被成功扩展到同时行动、大规模动作空间、复杂随机性和隐藏信息的博弈场景中，为通用博弈智能体的构建提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Decision-time equilibrium search carried poker to superhuman play, but it has so far relied on tractable subgames: a handful of actions per decision, chance confined to card deals, one player moving at a time. Competitive Pokémon in its official doubles format (VGC) breaks all three assumptions at once. Both players act simultaneously from joint menus in the hundreds, each joint action resolves to hundreds of stochastic outcomes, and the opponent's reserves and stat allocations are hidden. We set out to build a strong VGC agent and report what that took. PokaiEngine, our Rust battle engine, enumerates a joint action's full weighted outcome distribution in one pass, at ${\sim}99\%$ parity with Pokémon Showdown and a fraction of the cost of sampling it. On top of the engine, PokaiTrainer adapts Student of Games to this scale, solving every decision as a Bayesian matrix game over public belief states and growing subgames under an explicit compute budget. On the live Showdown best-of-three ladder, the agent wins 59% of 150 sets against a human field averaging ${\sim}1320$ Elo. It settles into a 1350-1400 Elo band, and at its peak briefly entered the format's top 500.

</details>


### [251] [RL-FAT: Reinforcement Learning for Fair Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29247)
*Tejaswini Medi,Levan Mikeladze,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-FAT，一种基于强化学习的公平对抗训练框架，通过策略梯度反馈和公平强调损失来缓解对抗训练中不同类别鲁棒性不均衡的问题，在保持竞争力的同时显著降低类间鲁棒性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络容易受到对抗扰动影响，对抗训练（AT）是常用的提升鲁棒性的方法。然而，平均鲁棒准确率的提升往往掩盖了类别间鲁棒性的显著差异：某些类别变得更鲁棒，而其他类别仍被不成比例地攻击。这种不均衡引发了对抗公平性问题，尤其在期望所有类别都具备可靠鲁棒性的视觉任务中。

Method: RL-FAT将预测分布解释为策略（policy），结合基于正确性的预测奖励与类别级价值估计来计算类别特定的优势（advantage），用于策略梯度优化，使模型自适应地关注类别级误分类。此外，引入公平强调对抗损失，对具有高对抗损失的类别施加更强的训练压力，从而缓解类别间鲁棒性差异。

Result: 大量实验表明，RL-FAT在保持与标准对抗训练基线相当的鲁棒准确率的同时，显著降低了类别间的鲁棒性不均衡。

Conclusion: 将强化驱动适应与公平强调正则化相结合，RL-FAT不仅提升了对抗鲁棒性，还促进了跨类别更均衡的鲁棒性分布，为解决对抗训练中的公平性问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks remain highly vulnerable to adversarial perturbations, and adversarial training (AT) has become a widely used approach for improving robustness. However, improvements in average robust accuracy often mask substantial class-wise disparities: while some classes become more robust, others may remain disproportionately vulnerable under attack. This imbalance raises an important adversarial fairness concern, particularly in vision tasks where reliable robustness is expected across all categories. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{RL-FAT}, a reinforcement-learning-inspired fair adversarial training framework that uses policy-gradient based feedback from adversarial predictions. RL-FAT interprets the prediction distribution as a policy and combines correctness-based prediction rewards with class-wise value estimates to compute class-specific advantages for policy-gradient optimization. This enables the model to adaptively focus on class-wise misclassification. Furthermore, we introduce a fairness-emphasis adversarial loss that assigns stronger training pressure to classes with high adversarial loss, thereby mitigating class-wise robustness disparity. By combining reinforcement-driven adaptation with fairness-emphasis regularization, RL-FAT improves adversarial robustness while promoting a more balanced robustness distribution across classes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive robust accuracy and substantially reduces class-wise robustness imbalance compared with standard adversarial training baselines.

</details>


### [252] [Adaptive Multi-Branching for Shallow Decision Tree Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29262)
*Hanul Park,Jeonghoon Choi,Juseong Kim,Sanghun Sel,Giltae Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MBNDT（多分支神经决策树与自适应剪枝），通过可微的多路分裂和自适应分支掩码，在保证根到叶路径较短的前提下提升深度受限决策树的精度，在21个OpenML基准上取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二叉决策树在严格最大深度预算下表达能力不足，因为每个内部节点只做单一阈值判断，导致浅层树下难以获得高精度。

Method: MBNDT是一个单棵轴对齐的决策树，端到端训练，内部节点在选定特征上学习有序阈值并配合分支掩码自适应调整有效分叉数（arity）；训练后将模型转换为确定性单路径树用于推理。

Result: 在21个OpenML二分类基准上，MBNDT在深度约束的单树基线中取得了最佳平均排名和平均平衡精度；消融实验表明多路分裂是性能提升的来源。其代价是叶子数多于其他单树基线。

Conclusion: MBNDT适用于优先考虑短而有界决策路径下精度、而非追求全局最小树规模的场景；多路分裂是浅层树表达力提升的关键。

Abstract: Decision trees are attractive for tabular prediction tasks because each prediction follows an interpretable sequence of feature-threshold tests. Under a strict maximum-depth budget, however, conventional binary trees can be under-expressive, since each internal node makes only a single threshold decision. We study shallow-depth tree induction, where the goal is to improve accuracy while keeping root-to-leaf paths short. We propose the Multi-Branch Neural Decision Tree with Adaptive Pruning (MBNDT), a single axis-aligned tree trained end-to-end with differentiable multi-way splits. Each internal node learns ordered thresholds over a selected feature and a branch mask that adapts its effective arity, and the trained model is converted to a deterministic single-path tree for inference. Across 21 OpenML binary-classification benchmarks, MBNDT achieves the best average rank and mean balanced accuracy among depth-constrained single-tree baselines; a controlled ablation isolates multi-way splitting as the source of the gain. These gains come with an explicit trade-off: MBNDT realizes more leaves than the other single-tree baselines, making it best suited when accuracy under short, bounded decision paths is prioritized over minimal global tree size.

</details>


### [253] [When Do Larger Batches Help Scale LLM Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29296)
*Ziniu Li,Jinbo Wang,Guanhua Huang,Feiyuan Zhang,Pengbo Li,Alex Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型强化学习（RLHF）中批大小对训练时间的影响，将算法层面和系统层面的效果分开分析。算法层面通过重新调优批相关超参数，在相同累计样本数下得到近似批大小不变的训练轨迹；系统层面利用生成（内存带宽受限）与训练（随token数扩展）的计算不对称性，得出决策规则：只有吞吐量增益超过样本效率损失时，更大批大小才降低到达目标的时间。实验表明平方根学习率缩放带来批大小不变的曲线，更大批大小提升生成吞吐量最高2.29倍，GRPO中结合吞吐量提升和学习率重调可将时间降低29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更大的批大小理论上能降低随机梯度方差从而加速训练，但每个更新消耗更多样本且执行时间更长，这种统计收益能否转化为更低的墙钟时间尚不清楚。作者希望解开算法与系统两方面效果，给出清晰的决策规则。

Method: 将训练过程分为算法和系统两个层面：算法层面在相同累计样本数下对比不同批大小配置，并重新调优批依赖的超参数（如采用平方根学习率缩放）；系统层面利用自回归生成（低并发时受内存带宽限制）与训练（工作量与token数近似成比例）之间的计算不对称性，导出决策规则并验证于GRPO和PPO。

Result: 算法层面，Adam配合平方根学习率缩放在有限批大小范围内产生近似批大小不变的训练曲线；系统层面，更大批大小在固定硬件上将生成吞吐量提升最多2.29倍。在GRPO中，结合更高吞吐量与学习率重调可将时间降低最多29%，而不重调的增大批大小尽管吞吐量更高，反而更慢。

Conclusion: 增大批大小是否加速取决于吞吐量增益是否超过样本效率惩罚。在RLHF场景下，必须同时重调批相关超参数（如学习率）才能兑现吞吐量收益；仅增大批大小而不重调反而会更慢。

Abstract: Larger batches reduce the variance of stochastic gradients per update and are therefore often expected to accelerate training. Yet whether this statistical benefit translates into lower wall-clock time-to-target remains unclear, because each update consumes more samples and may take longer to execute. We study this tradeoff in reinforcement learning for large language models. We separate its algorithmic and systems effects by comparing learning and execution along their natural axes. At the algorithmic level, we compare configurations at equal cumulative sample counts while retuning batch-dependent hyperparameters. Over a bounded range of batch sizes, this procedure yields an approximately batch-size-invariant family whose members follow similar sample-indexed learning trajectories. At the systems level, we exploit the computational asymmetry between rollout generation and training: autoregressive generation is often memory-bandwidth-bound at low concurrency, whereas training work scales approximately with the number of processed tokens. Combining these two views yields a direct decision rule: a larger-batch configuration reduces time-to-target only when its throughput gain exceeds its samples-to-target penalty. Experiments with GRPO and PPO support both sides of this decomposition. At the algorithmic level, square-root learning-rate scaling with Adam produces approximately batch-size-invariant learning curves over a bounded range of batch sizes. At the systems level, larger batches improve generation throughput by up to 2.29x on fixed hardware. In GRPO, combining higher throughput with learning-rate retuning reduces time-to-target by up to 29%, whereas increasing the batch without retuning is slower despite its higher throughput.

</details>


### [254] [MEL: Coordinate-Preserving EEG Tokenization for fMRI Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29304)
*Xiangyu Liu,Zeting Yan,Zhitong Yin,Boyang Li,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MEL（Multi-band EEG Latent-state Tokenization），一种坐标保持的EEG表示框架，通过将EEG历史组织为滞后-通道-频率令牌来对齐fMRI响应，解决EEG-to-fMRI任务中的表示-接口不匹配问题，在VU基准和外部Oddball数据上优于NeuroBOLT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG-to-fMRI转换对医学神经影像和脑状态监测至关重要，但现有工作主要追求更强的解码器，忽视了表示-接口不匹配问题：fMRI响应具有延迟、时间积分和空间分布特性，而通用EEG编码常纠缠时间滞后、通道身份和频带结构，阻碍了有效对齐。

Method: MEL框架将每个目标fMRI响应锚定到其前面的EEG历史，组织为滞后-通道-频率神经状态令牌，显式捕获血流动力学延迟和频谱-空间动态，以容量受控的读出方式对齐fMRI相关的EEG表示，而非完全依赖模型规模扩展。

Result: 在VU EEG-fMRI基准和外部Oddball数据上的实验表明，MEL相比强NeuroBOLT基线提升了预测性能。消融和控制实验证明性能增益来自结构化EEG表示本身，而非数据泄漏、捷径统计或解码器容量。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模血流动力学延迟与频谱-空间动态的结构化EEG表示，可以有效桥接EEG与fMRI之间的模态鸿沟，而无需依赖模型规模的无限扩展，为多模态神经解码提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Translating electroencephalography (EEG) into functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is important for medical neuroimaging, clinical brain-state monitoring, and multimodal neural decoding, because it aims to infer spatially organized hemodynamic activity from fast and accessible electrophysiological recordings. Existing EEG-to-fMRI studies mainly pursue stronger decoders, but the problem is also constrained by a representation-interface mismatch: fMRI responses are delayed, temporally integrated, and spatially distributed, whereas generic EEG encodings often entangle temporal lag, channel identity, and frequency-band structure. We propose Multi-band EEG Latent-state Tokenization (MEL), a coordinate-preserving EEG representation framework that anchors each target fMRI response to its preceding EEG history and organizes it into lag-channel-frequency neural-state tokens. By explicitly capturing hemodynamic latency and spectral-spatial dynamics, MEL aligns fMRI-pertinent EEG representations with capacity-controlled readouts without depending entirely on model scaling. Experiments on VU EEG-fMRI benchmarks and external Oddball data show that MEL improves prediction over strong NeuroBOLT baselines. Ablations and controls further indicate that the gains come from structured EEG representation rather than leakage, shortcut statistics, or decoder capacity.

</details>


### [255] [Information-Based Calibration of Uncertainty Quantification in Product-of-Experts Gaussian Process Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29349)
*Yean Hoon Ong,Paolo Barucca,Wei Pan,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GP-pro-c，一种基于信息增益的校准方法，用于修正产品-of-experts高斯过程回归模型中后验方差过高估计的问题，在保持预测精度的同时降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局高斯过程回归计算成本为三次方，难以扩展到大规模数据；产品-of-experts GP模型将局部GP专家模型组合以捕捉全局相关性，但训练于不相交数据子集的局部专家会导致后验方差的过高估计，影响不确定性估计的可靠性。

Method: 利用高斯过程中信息增益的单调性和子模性，定义校准比率（calibration ratio）来减小各局部GP模型的后验方差，从而实现对产品-of-experts GP模型方差的校准。

Result: 在四个合成函数和六个回归数据集上的实验表明，与未校准的GP-pro模型相比，GP-pro-c在负对数似然（NLL）上平均降低2.3%，在期望归一化校准误差（ENCE）上平均降低12.0%。

Conclusion: GP-pro-c有效缓解了后验方差的过高估计问题，在保持预测精度的同时降低了计算复杂度，为可扩展GP模型中的不确定性估计提供了有前景的方案，并可作为高维大规模贝叶斯优化的替代代理模型。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) regression with a single global GP (GP-glo) incurs cubic computational cost, limiting scalability to large datasets. Product-of-experts GP models (GP-pro), which combine local GP models to capture global correlations, alleviate this computational burden. However, training local experts on disjoint data subsets can lead to overestimated posterior variances. We propose GP-pro-c, a product-of-experts GP model that calibrates these variances using an information-based method. The method exploits the monotonicity and submodularity of information gain in GPs to define a calibration ratio that reduces the posterior variance of individual local GP models. We evaluate GP-pro-c using negative log-likelihood (NLL), root mean squared error (RMSE), and expected normalised calibration error (ENCE). Experiments on four synthetic functions and six regression datasets show that GP-pro-c achieves average reductions of 2.3% in NLL and 12.0% in ENCE compared with the uncalibrated GP-pro model. The proposed method mitigates posterior variance overestimation while maintaining predictive accuracy and reducing computational complexity. GP-pro-c provides a promising approach for uncertainty estimation in scalable GP models and may serve as a useful surrogate model for Bayesian optimisation with high-dimensional and large-scale data.

</details>


### [256] [Spatial Entropy based Partitioning for Spatiotemporal Graph Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29360)
*Qiming Guo,Wenbo Sun,Ye Wang,Wenlu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IsleNet，一种面向时空图（ST-graph）的精确且高效的机器学习卸载（unlearning）方法。通过空间熵引导的分区构建平衡局部连通子图，并以轻量虚拟边重连；卸载请求时仅重训练受影响子图编码器和虚拟边层，确保精确移除同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空图广泛应用于交通、天气和医疗监控等领域。GDPR和CCPA等隐私法规要求从训练模型中完全删除未授权的数据，但在时空图上实现这一点非常困难：由于信息和消息在时空两个维度上全局传播，完全擦除一个节点的影响需要昂贵的全图重训练。因此需要在时空图卸载中同时满足精确性（exactness）和高效性（efficiency）。

Method: IsleNet采用空间熵引导的分区（spatial-entropy-guided partitioning）创建平衡且局部连通的子图，并通过轻量虚拟边（virtual edges）重新连接这些子图。当收到卸载请求时，仅对受影响的子图编码器和虚拟边层进行重训练，从而实现精确移除并降低开销。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准数据集上的实验表明，IsleNet在卸载时能达到全图准确率的94%，同时将卸载时间减少最高达一个数量级。

Conclusion: IsleNet通过分区与虚拟边机制，在保证时空图数据精确卸载的同时大幅降低了重训练成本，为隐私合规要求下的时空图模型提供了一种高效的解决方案。代码已公开。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal graphs underpin applications such as traffic forecasting, weather forecasting, and healthcare monitoring. Privacy regulations such as the GDPR and the CCPA require the complete removal of unauthorized data from trained models, but achieving this on a spatiotemporal graph is difficult: because information propagates globally through both spatial and temporal message passing, fully erasing a node's influence forces costly full-graph retraining. ST-graph unlearning requires both exactness and efficiency. We propose IsleNet, which uses spatial-entropy-guided partitioning to create balanced, locally coherent subgraphs and reconnects them with lightweight virtual edges. Upon an unlearning request, only the affected subgraph encoder and virtual-edge layer are retrained, ensuring exact removal with low cost. Experiments on four real-world benchmarks show that IsleNet attains up to 94% of full-graph accuracy while reducing unlearning time by up to an order of magnitude. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wenlu-lab/STGraphUnlearning.

</details>


### [257] [Unlearning on Spatio-Temporal Graphs through Subgraph Virtual Edge Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29369)
*Qiming Guo,Wenbo Sun,Chen Pan,Ye Wang,Wenlu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CallosumNet，一个受胼胝体结构启发的时空图遗忘框架，通过虚拟边重建子图并用轻量级元图整合层恢复子图间的时空依赖，实现高效且完整的单节点遗忘，性能接近完整重训练的黄金模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图遗忘方法主要针对静态图或局部数据移除设计，无法高效处理时空图上的单节点遗忘。因为时空图中每个节点会在空间和时间两个维度上全局扩散信息，导致遗忘一个节点的成本几乎等同于完整重训练模型。隐私法规（如GDPR、CCPA）对"被遗忘权"的严格要求使得这一问题迫切需要解决。

Method: CallosumNet包含两个关键技术贡献：(1)受生物学胼胝体结构启发，利用虚拟边重构子图；(2)通过轻量级元图整合层恢复子图间相互关联的时空依赖关系。

Result: 在四个多样的真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，CallosumNet能够实现完整的遗忘效果，同时保持与黄金模型（完整重训练模型）非常接近的准确率。

Conclusion: CallosumNet为时空图上的高效节点遗忘提供了一种受生物启发的解决方案，在保证完整遗忘的同时显著降低计算成本，并已公开代码供研究社区复现和使用。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal graphs are widely used in modeling complex dynamic processes such as temporal forecasting, molecular dynamics, and healthcare monitoring. Recently, stringent privacy regulations such as GDPR and CCPA have introduced significant new challenges for existing spatio-temporal graph models, requiring complete unlearning of unauthorized data. Since each node in a spatio-temporal graph diffuses information globally across both spatial and temporal dimensions, existing unlearning methods primarily designed for static graphs and localized data removal cannot efficiently erase a single node without incurring costs nearly equivalent to full model retraining. To address this, we propose CallosumNet, a spatio-temporal graph unlearning framework biologically inspired by the corpus callosum structure. CallosumNet makes two key technical contributions: (1) it reconstructs subgraphs using biologically-inspired virtual edges; and (2) it restores interlinked spatio-temporal dependencies among subgraphs via a lightweight meta-graph integration layer. Empirical results on four diverse real-world datasets show that CallosumNet achieves complete unlearning while maintaining accuracy very close to the gold model. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/wenlu-lab/STGraphUnlearning.

</details>


### [258] [Fully Distributed GNE Algorithms for Multi-Robot Placement without Consensus on Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29388)
*Shao-An Yin,Mingyi Hong,Nicola Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向共享线性等式约束广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP）的全分布式连续时间算法，无需交换拉格朗日乘子即可收敛到任意广义纳什均衡（GNE），降低了通信开销并提升了隐私保护，同时提供离散时间方案并在多机器人布置任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在求解强单调博弈的变分GNE（v-GNE）时需要交换拉格朗日乘子，这带来了较高的通信开销和隐私泄露风险。因此需要一种无需乘子交换的全分布式算法来降低通信成本并增强隐私保护。

Method: 提出一种在共享线性等式约束下的全分布式连续时间算法，通过避免乘子交换机制实现GNE收敛，并给出相应的离散时间实现方案。

Result: 该算法无需交换拉格朗日乘子即可收敛到任意GNE，在多机器人布置任务上得到验证，显著降低通信开销并改善隐私。

Conclusion: 该全分布式算法为共享约束GNEP提供了一种通信高效且隐私友好的求解方案，且收敛目标不再局限于v-GNE，而是可达任意GNE，具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Recent machine learning research has increasingly focused on equilibrium analysis in non-cooperative games rather than solely on optimal solutions. Many such problems involve shared constraints and can be formulated as Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems (GNEPs). For strongly monotone games, existing methods compute consensus-based variational GNEs (v-GNEs) by exchanging Lagrange multipliers. We propose a fully distributed continuous-time algorithm for shared linear equality constraints that converges without multiplier exchange and reaches any GNE, reducing communication overhead and improving privacy. Discrete-time schemes are also provided, and the method is validated on a multi-robot placement task.

</details>


### [259] [Selection, Representation, and Execution in Sparse Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30070)
*Abdul Qadir Ibrahim,Martin Burger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对傅里叶神经算子（FNO）提出稀疏性并不总是等同于更小的模型和更低的推理成本。作者区分了表示稀疏性、参数量稀疏性、理论算子计数和实测运行时四种稀疏性概念，并通过实证研究测试从稠密FNO到稀疏FNO的各条路径。研究发现在执行网格上粗化降低理论成本但不减少实测延迟，加入修正项可恢复精度但增加耗时，即使83%的参数压缩在常规执行下仍比稠密基线慢。因此需要更严格的有用稀疏性定义：部署的算子必须保持解精度，并将减少的支持映射到真正更廉价的执行路径上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究认为稀疏表示能同时实现模型更小和推理成本更低的目标，但在FNO中这两个目标并不等价甚至可能冲突。移除部分学习算子可能不改变底层变换和稠密计算，而改变评估网格可能引入额外开销。作者旨在厘清不同稀疏性概念之间的区别，并实证检验各稀疏化路径的实际效果。

Method: 本文将稀疏性区分为四个层面：表示中的稀疏性、存储参数的稀疏性、理论运算量（FLOPs）中的稀疏性以及实测运行时中的稀疏性。通过实证研究，分别测试了从稠密FNO到稀疏FNO的各条转换路径：包括在执行网格上进行粗化（coarsening）、添加修正项（correction term）以恢复精度、以及参数压缩等不同策略。

Result: 实验结果表明：执行网格粗化虽然降低了理论运算成本，但并未减少实测延迟；引入修正项可以恢复精度，却使模型运行更慢；即使实现83%的参数缩减，在普通执行条件下仍然比稠密基线更慢。这说明理论层面的稀疏化并不等同于实际运行时的加速。

Conclusion: 作者提出需要更严格的有用稀疏性定义：部署的算子必须同时满足两个条件——保持解的精度，并且将缩减后的支撑（support）真正映射到更廉价的执行路径。否则，稀疏化只是形式上的，缺乏实际部署价值。

Abstract: Sparse representations are often expected to make models smaller and also reduce inference cost. For Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs), these objectives are not equivalent or do not always align: removing parts of the learned operator can leave the underlying transforms and dense computations unchanged, while changing the grid on which the model is evaluated can introduce overhead of its own. We therefore distinguish sparsity in the representation, in the stored parameters, in the theoretical operation count, and in measured runtime, and present an empirical study of several routes toward sparse FNOs that tests each transition between them separately. Coarsening the execution grid reduces the theoretical cost without reducing measured latency, and adding a correction term recovers accuracy at the cost of making the model slower. Even an 83\% parameter reduction remains slower than the dense baseline under ordinary execution. These results motivate a stricter definition of useful sparsity: the deployed operator must preserve solution accuracy and map its reduced support to a genuinely cheaper execution path.

</details>


### [260] [Where Induction Runs Out: Description-Length Difficulty and the Memorisation Gap in Integer-Sequence Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29411)
*Sabilashan Ganeshan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用可精确计算的参考学习器——在P-递归（全纯）递归类上的两部分最小描述长度（MDL），评估OEIS整数序列基准实际衡量了什么。三个发现：（1）MDL难度本质上是参数计数，发现点nd几乎完全由所选算子的阶数和度数的组合可辨识性下界预测，且对项幅值不变；（2）在大规模数据上出现"荒野"现象：20,000个OEIS序列中，89.98%在某个前缀上能拟合递归、但全长无法拟合，归纳获得理论又失去且永不恢复；（3）在按MDL机制分层的序列上评估三个语言模型，推翻了预注册假设——模型在MDL无理论的地方并非胡编乱造，而是适当模糊化；自信错误反而集中在"容易"层，表面能力更多是识别序列而非归纳规则。因此OEIS基准实质衡量记忆，MDL提供廉价且无污染的难度信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OEIS整数序列被越来越多用于基准测试语言模型的数学推理能力，但这类基准实际测量的对象尚不清楚。作者希望揭示这些基准究竟衡量的是什么能力——是真正的归纳推理还是记忆-识别，从而为可靠的推理评估提供理论支撑。

Method: 作者引入一个可精确计算的参考学习器：在P-递归（全纯）递归类上使用两部分最小描述长度（MDL），对每个序列的每个前缀进行计算。该框架提供了无污染、可计算的难度信号，并将序列划分为不同机制（拟合/荒野区）。随后用MDL分层去测试三个语言模型，与预注册假设对比。

Result: （1）MDL难度等价于算子阶数和度数的参数计数，发现点nd被组合可辨识性下界精确预测，且对项幅值不变（Fibonacci缩放12个数量级nd不变）；（2）在大规模语料中出现"荒野"机制：89.98%的序列在某个前缀可拟合递归但全长不可拟合；（3）三个LLM在按MDL分层的序列上的行为推翻了预注册假设：模型在无理论层不产生自信错误（适当回避），而是自信错误集中在易层/记忆层，说明明显能力主要来自序列识别而非规则归纳。

Conclusion: OEIS派生基准在很大程度上衡量的是记忆而非数学归纳能力；MDL为基准设计提供了一种廉价、无污染且理论严谨的难度信号，可帮助区分记忆与推理。

Abstract: Integer sequences from the On-Line Encyclopedia of Integer Sequences (OEIS) are increasingly used to benchmark mathematical reasoning in language models. We ask what such benchmarks actually measure, using an exactly computable reference learner: two-part minimum description length (MDL) over the class of P-recursive (holonomic) recurrences, evaluated on every prefix of a sequence as terms arrive. Three findings follow. First, MDL difficulty is a parameter count. The discovery point nd, the first prefix length at which a symbolic hypothesis beats verbatim storage, is predicted almost exactly by a combinatorial identifiability bound on the selected operator's order and degree. It is invariant to term magnitude: scaling Fibonacci over twelve orders of magnitude leaves nd unchanged, because a hypothesis must encode its own initial conditions and the magnitude cancels. Second, at scale the learner exhibits a regime our curated corpus could not produce even once: across 20,000 OEIS sequences, 89.98% of those that fit a recurrence on some prefix fit none at full length. We call this the wilderness -- induction acquires a theory, loses it, and never recovers. Third, evaluating three language models on sequences stratified by these MDL regimes refuted our pre-registered hypothesis: models do not confabulate where MDL reports no theory, but hedge appropriately. Confident errors are inverted, concentrating on the easy stratum, where apparent competence tracks recognition of the sequence rather than induction of its rule. OEIS-derived benchmarks therefore substantially measure memorisation, and MDL supplies a cheap, contamination-free difficulty signal they currently lack. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [261] [Scalable Clinical Data Infrastructure and Comparative ML Evaluation for Hospitalisation Risk Prediction in Elderly Patients with Multiple Long-Term Conditions using CPRD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29419)
*Asra Aslam,Volodymyr Chapman,Maurice M. O'Connell,Aseel S. Abuzour,Michael Abaho,Danushka Bollegala,Gary Leeming,Eduard Shantsila,Andrew Clegg,Lauren E. Walker,Iain Edward Buchan,Samuel D. Relton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在CPRD Aurum中构建了覆盖260种临床条件(含17种复杂条件的专门检测逻辑)的患者时间线流水线,并在预测12个月全因急诊入院风险的任务上,对TG-CNN、LASSO逻辑回归和随机森林进行了严格基准测试。结果显示LASSO在留出测试集上判别力最高(AUC-ROC 0.733),且经Platt校准后是唯一校准斜率可接受的模型(0.817),而TG-CNN虽在交叉验证中略高但严重校准不足(0.391)。作者认为LASSO而非判别力最高的模型最适合直接临床部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习架构在EHR患者轨迹建模中被越来越多地提出,但其相较于更简单、可解释模型的实际优势很少在真实临床环境中接受严格的实证检验。作者旨在通过严格的基准测试,检验复杂深度模型是否真正优于简单基线模型,并强调仅凭判别力(AUC)不足以支撑高风险临床部署决策。

Method: 构建了一个全面的患者时间线流水线,应用于CPRD Aurum中的老年患者,纳入260种临床条件,通过三层自动化框架分类,其中17种复杂条件使用专门的检测逻辑。在此基础设施上,对比TG-CNN、LASSO逻辑回归和随机森林三种模型,用于预测12个月全因急诊入院风险。采用交叉验证和留出测试集评估判别力,并通过Platt校准评估校准质量(校准斜率)。

Result: 交叉验证下TG-CNN的均值AUC-ROC略高于LASSO(0.712 vs 0.705);但在留出测试集上LASSO判别力最高(AUC-ROC 0.733,RF为0.710,TG-CNN为0.702)。Platt校准后,LASSO是唯一具有可接受校准斜率(0.817)的模型,随机森林(0.759)和TG-CNN(0.391)均存在显著校准不足。

Conclusion: 判别力(AUC)单独不足以作为临床部署的完整标准;校准质量和可解释性在高风险决策支持中至关重要。LASSO尽管不是判别力最高的模型,却是最适合直接临床部署的模型。研究为机器学习与医疗社区提供了关于数据基础设施、模型选择以及校准和可解释性价值的经验教训。

Abstract: Deep learning architectures are increasingly proposed for patient trajectory modeling in electronic health records (EHRs), yet their advantage over simpler, more interpretable models is rarely subjected to rigorous empirical scrutiny in real-world clinical settings. We present a comprehensive patient timeline pipeline applied to elderly patients in CPRD Aurum, incorporating 260 clinical conditions classified via a three-tier automated framework including specialised detection logic for 17 complex conditions. Using this infrastructure, we benchmark Temporal Graph Convolutional Neural Networks (TG-CNN) against Logistic Regression with LASSO regularisation and Random Forests for predicting 12-month all-cause emergency hospitalisation risk, motivated by (but not filtered to) the elevated risk of adverse drug reactions. Under cross-validation, TG-CNN achieves a marginally higher mean AUC-ROC than LASSO (0.712 vs. 0.705), whereas on the held-out test set LASSO achieves the highest discrimination of three models (AUC-ROC 0.733, versus 0.710 for Random Forest and 0.702 for TG-CNN). We show, that discrimination alone is an incomplete criterion for clinical deployment: after Platt calibration, LASSO is the only model with an acceptable calibration slope (0.817), while Random Forest (0.759) and, TG-CNN (0.391) remain substantially miscalibrated. We argue that LASSO, not the highest-discriminating model, is the model best suited to direct clinical deployment. We present lessons for the machine learning and healthcare community regarding data infrastructure, model selection, and value of calibration and interpretability in high-stakes decision support.

</details>


### [262] [One Capability or Many? Testing the Economic Validity of Frontier AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29420)
*Louis Yiven Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过潜在变量模型分析了421个模型配置在12个基准（其中4个为经济基准）上的表现，发现单一因素解释了74.5%的共同方差，且该因素主要反映模型发布日期的时间趋势（R²=0.505）。经济基准并未形成独立的能力维度，但在留一基准交叉验证中，多因素表示对经济得分的预测优于单因素一般指数，表明经济基准提供了增量预测信息，而非独立的潜在能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型排行榜基于经济基准（如软件工程、银行工作流程等专业任务）对系统进行排名，这些排名影响组织采购、监管审查和工作预期。然而，这些基准衡量的是一种独立于一般测试能力的特殊能力，还是仅仅重新表达了随模型改进而上升的单一能力轴，这一构念效度问题尚未被研究过。

Method: 在哈希固定的排行榜快照上，对421个模型配置在12个基准（4个为经济基准）上的表现进行分析。将基准视为项目、模型视为受访者，构建潜在变量模型，预先设定四种假设及其阈值。测试单一因素解释力、日期趋势的影响，并通过留一基准交叉验证检验多因素与单因素表示的预测表现。

Result: 单一因素解释了74.5%的共同方差，并追踪模型发布日期（R²=0.505）。去除日期趋势后，该比例下降14.9个百分点；在每基础模型一行的情况下下降24.1个百分点。在控制规模后，计算量在去除日期后几乎没有额外贡献。预先固定的维度规则下，经济基准不构成独立因素，但留一基准测试显示多因素表示在预测经济得分上优于单一一般指数（合并Δ-MSE=0.037，95%自助置信区间[0.019, 0.055]）。

Conclusion: 经济基准为大体由日期驱动的一般因素增加了增量预测信息，但证据不支持将其视为独立的潜在能力。排行榜仍是衡量整体进展的可靠指南，但相隔数月发布的模型之间的大部分差距是日历效应，因此在将同时期模型的小差距解读为能力差异之前，应进行日期调整。

Abstract: Frontier-model leaderboards now rank systems based on economic benchmarks, tests of how well models carry out professional tasks from software engineering to banking workflows, and those rankings inform what organisations buy, what regulators scrutinise, and expectations of how work will change. Whether such benchmarks measure a capability distinct from general test-taking, or re-express the one axis along which every benchmark rises as models improve, is a question of construct validity that has not yet been studied. We test it on a hash-pinned leaderboard snapshot of 421 model configurations across twelve benchmarks, four of them economic, treating benchmarks as items and models as respondents in a latent-variable model with four hypotheses and their thresholds fixed before analysis. A single factor explains 74.5% of common variance and tracks model release date (R^2 = 0.505), so the leading axis of capability is substantially a time trend; where prior work controls for scale, compute adds little once date is removed. Removing the date trend lowers that share by 14.9 points, and by 24.1 with one row per base model. Under the dimensionality rule fixed in advance the economic benchmarks form no distinct factor, yet a leave-one-benchmark-out test with factors re-estimated inside every fold shows that a multi-factor representation predicts held-out economic scores better than a single general index (pooled Delta-MSE 0.037, 95% bootstrap interval [0.019, 0.055]). Economic benchmarks therefore add incremental predictive information to a largely date-driven general factor, and the evidence does not support treating them as a distinct latent capability. Leaderboards remain a sound guide to overall progress, but most of the gap between models released months apart is calendar, so a small gap between contemporaneous models should be date-adjusted before being read as a capability difference.

</details>


### [263] [Behavioral Latency as Weak Event-Time Supervision for EEG Reaction-Time Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29428)
*Anuar Aimoldin,Ayana Mussabayeva,Yedige Mussabayev,Xue Liu,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将单次试验EEG的反应时（RT）解码重新形式化为事件时间后验建模问题。与直接标量回归预测RT不同，模型估计响应相关事件时间的后验分布p(t_event|X)，并取其均值作为RT估计，将行为潜伏期视为潜在响应相关时间的弱观测。在Healthy Brain Network对比变化检测EEG任务上，分布事件时间监督一致优于标量回归和时序读出对照组，且该效果在多时间骨干架构中稳健，并提供后验几何、噪声校准和移位裁剪推理等全面分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG反应时预测将RT视为窗口级标签做标量回归，但忽略了RT本质上包含的响应相关时间动态信息。作者认为应把RT作为响应相关事件时间的弱观测，通过建模事件时间后验分布而非直接预测标量来提升预测性能并增强可解释性。

Method: 1) 事件时间后验建模：模型不直接预测RT，而是估计p(t_event|X)后验分布，取其均值作为RT预测；2) 分布级事件时间监督替代标量回归损失；3) 在Healthy Brain Network对比变化检测任务上采用subject-disjoint、release-separated协议评估；4) 通过对照实验隔离监督目标和架构变量（4种时间骨干）；5) 采用posterior geometry分析、observation-noise calibration、shifted-crop inference测试等诊断工具进行深入分析。

Result: 分布事件时间监督在5个随机种子下一致提升held-out RT预测性能，优于标量回归和时序读出对照组。增益源于事件时间分布监督本身而非期望读出机制；效果跨4种时间骨干稳健且不归因于模型规模。后验几何揭示浓度度、目标对齐和区间行为；观察噪声校准区分潜在浓度与RT预测不确定性；移位裁剪推理表明shift-jitter提升鲁棒性和均值敏感性，但敏感性仍低于理想裁剪相对定位，存在明确的等变性差距。

Conclusion: 事件时间后验建模为连接单次试验EEG动态与行为时序提供了概率化和可解释的框架。它将RT从窗口级标量标签重新概念化为潜在响应事件的弱观测，不仅改善了预测性能，还赋予了后验分布以诊断价值，同时揭示了当前模型在时间等变性方面的不足，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Single-trial EEG analyses are often organized around events and latencies, yet EEG-based reaction-time (RT) prediction is posed as scalar regression on a fixed stimulus-locked window. RT is treated as a window-level label rather than timing evidence about response-relevant dynamics. Here we reformulate trial-wise RT decoding as event-time posterior modeling. Instead of predicting RT directly, the model estimates a posterior over response-relevant event times, $p(t_{\mathrm{event}}\mid X)$, and uses its mean as the RT estimate. This treats behavioral latency as a weak observation of latent response-relevant timing. We evaluate this formulation on the Healthy Brain Network contrast change detection EEG task under a subject-disjoint, release-separated protocol. Across five seeds, distributional event-time supervision consistently improves held-out RT prediction relative to scalar regression and temporal-readout controls. Controlled objective comparisons isolate supervision of the event-time distribution, rather than expectation-based readout alone, as the source of this gain. Architecture controls show that the effect persists across four temporal backbones and is not explained by model scale. Beyond point prediction, posterior geometry characterizes concentration, target alignment, and interval behavior, while observation-noise calibration separates latent concentration from predictive uncertainty over RT. Shifted-crop inference probes shortcut use versus temporal localization. Matched shift-jitter improves robustness, increases mean sensitivity, and moves predictions more often in the expected crop-relative direction. Sensitivity remains below ideal crop-relative localization, leaving a clear equivariance gap. Together, these results establish event-time posterior modeling as a probabilistic and interpretable formulation for linking single-trial EEG dynamics to behavioral timing.

</details>


### [264] [Does Latent Planning Survive Point Clouds? Action-Conditioned JEPA World Models for Geometric Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29434)
*Fabio F. Oberweger,Michael Schwingshackl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将三种经典JEPA世界模型设计（冻结编码器、分布先验、动作敏感）扩展至点云场景，验证了潜在空间预测在几何观测上的可行性。结果表明分布先验模型在所有基准上与图像基线统计等价，动作敏感模型在几何运动最强时取得最佳结果，且规划能承受训练中未见的高丢弃率。通过将命令3D目标与当前潜在构造目标潜在，实现了无需目标观测的自然目标接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA世界模型在图像上已被证明可实现潜在空间规划，但其对点云这类几何观测是否有效尚不明确。点云具有稀疏、无序、自我遮挡等特性，且仅有0.3-15%场景点运动，潜在预测的慢特征最优与3D自监督的几何捷径可能相互冲突。作者旨在验证JEPA设计能否自然迁移至点云。

Method: 将三种经典JEPA设计（冻结编码器、分布先验、动作敏感）提升到点云输入，重建稳定世界模型基准使得除了观测模态外与图像基线完全一致。实验中系统比较了三种设计在点云上的规划性能，并通过实验结果（可解码性、注意力分析）揭示机制。

Result: 三种设计均无崩溃地完成规划：分布先验模型在所有基准上与重新评估的图像基线统计等价；动作敏感模型在几何运动最强的受控比较中取得最强结果。探测显示物体位置几乎可完美线性解码，注意力集中在少数移动点。规划可承受训练中未见的高丢弃率，但范围噪声会破坏最薄场景。

Conclusion: 点云上的JEPA潜在预测是可行的且无需崩溃，对象位置的可解码性和注意力聚焦移动点解释了其稳健性。此外，几何观测使命令的3D目标成为自然的规划目标接口，可从目标和当前潜在构造目标潜在，无需目标观测即可保持成功率。

Abstract: JEPA world models make latent-space planning a practical route to control, but they are built almost exclusively on images. Whether latent prediction survives geometric observations is unclear: point clouds are sparse, unordered, and self-occluded, and with 0.3-15% of scene points moving, the slow-feature optimum of latent prediction compounds with the geometric shortcut of 3D self-supervision. We lift three canonical JEPA designs to point clouds, frozen-encoder, distribution-prior, and action-sensitive, and re-sense the stable-worldmodel benchmark so that only the observation differs from the image baselines. All three plan without collapse: the distribution-prior model is statistically equivalent to its re-evaluated image counterpart on every benchmark, and the action-sensitive model attains the strongest result in our controlled comparison where the most geometry moves. Probing explains why: object positions are almost perfectly linearly decodable and attention falls on the few moving points. Planning withstands heavy dropout never seen in training, though range noise defeats the thinnest scene. Geometry finally makes a commanded 3D target a natural goal interface: we construct the goal latent from the target and the current latent, at no cost in success rate, without a goal observation.

</details>


### [265] [Reference-Grafting Matches Fine-Tuning at Eliciting Sandbagged Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29458)
*Linh Le,Hong Kiat Tan,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"reference-grafting"（参考嫁接）方法，通过沿对比方向将激活坐标设置为诚实参考值，可在不进行权重更新或使用训练标签的情况下，从"装傻"（sandbagging）模型中挖掘被隐藏的能力，恢复率高达94-101%，匹配微调诱导的效果，且仅需2-5个配对样本即可拟合方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装傻（sandbagging）指模型故意在评估中表现不佳但保留底层能力，这威胁到前沿模型治理所依赖的安全评估。此前"Elicitation Game"发现微调能挖掘装傻模型的隐藏能力，而加性激活引导（additive activation steering）失败。本文旨在重新审视这一结论，寻找更有效的激活层面的能力挖掘方法。

Method: 提出reference-grafting方法：将激活沿对比方向（contrast direction）的坐标设置为在诚实参考中取的值，并在由主动学习选择的少量回路（circuits）上执行。对比了微调诱导、强化学习诱导和密码锁定代码生成等不同装傻场景，并分析了底层机制。

Result: 在11个微调密码锁定模型（1.5-32B，三种架构谱系）上，恢复诚实-装傻差距的94-101%，与微调诱导相当而无需权重更新或训练标签；仅需2-5个配对样本即可拟合方向。对强化学习诱导的装傻和密码锁定代码生成同样有效。嫁接有效的原因是微调锁定是一个阈值化的门控——嫁接坐标在接近诚实参考的阈值前，保持准确率在装傻水平，这解释了为何加性引导和坐标归零常失败。方向追踪的是装傻诱导方式而非被隐藏的内容，且对于circuit-breaking这类重置方法失效。

Conclusion: Reference-grafting为装傻模型的隐藏能力挖掘提供了比加性激活引导更有效的方法，揭示了装傻的底层机制是阈值化门控，且装傻的诱导方式决定方向特征。但该方法受限于固定的激活编辑，无法应对circuit-breaking这类逐次前向传播中重置激活的方法。

Abstract: Sandbagging, in which a model deliberately underperforms on an evaluation despite retaining the underlying capability, threatens the safety evaluations that frontier-model governance depends on. The Elicitation Game found that fine-tuning elicits hidden capability from sandbagging model organisms whereas additive activation steering fails. We revisit that verdict with reference-grafting, which sets an activation's coordinate along a contrast direction to the value it takes in an honest reference, at a small set of circuits chosen by active learning. Across eleven fine-tuned password-locked models (1.5-32B, three architecture lineages), it recovers +94 to +101% of the honest-sandbagging gap, matching fine-tuning elicitation without weight updates or training labels; two to five paired examples suffice to fit the direction. Similar recovery holds for reinforcement-learning-induced sandbagging and for password-locked code generation. Grafting works because the fine-tuned lock is a thresholded gate: held-out accuracy stays at the sandbagged level until the grafted coordinate crosses a threshold near the honest reference, which is why additive steering and zeroing the coordinate often fail. The direction tracks how the sandbagging was induced rather than what is withheld -- fit on grade-school science (ARC) it elicits withheld hazardous knowledge (WMDP), yet prompting, fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning each carry a different direction. Circuit-breaking marks the boundary: it reroutes activations on every forward pass, so the fixed edits we test are re-broken downstream and do not restore coherent generation.

</details>


### [266] [Denoising as Projection: Constrained Optimization with Gradient-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29507)
*Runyu Zhang,Jiawei Zhang,Gioele Zardini,Saurabh Amin,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种投影梯度引导的扩散采样更新方法，利用Stein去噪算子作为对数据几何的近似投影，将目标梯度融入去噪步骤，实现推理期优化采样，并在线性流形、紧致凸集和紧致黎曼子流形三种设定下证明下降性和有限时间收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型不仅用于从学习的数据分布中采样，也越来越多地用于生成满足特定任务目标的最优样本。常见的做法是通过外部目标的梯度来引导逆向扩散过程，但当数据分布支撑在结构化可行集（如流形或约束集）上时，梯度引导可能使样本偏离学习到的数据几何结构。

Method: 提出一种简单的投影梯度引导扩散更新方法。关键观察是Stein去噪算子可以充当对数据几何的近似投影。该方法将目标梯度融入去噪步骤，构成一种仅需预训练去噪器和梯度评估的推理期方法。作者将该更新分析为在学习的可行几何上的非精确投影梯度方法，覆盖三种设定：线性流形、紧致凸可行集和紧致黎曼子流形。

Result: 在三种设定下均证明了下降性质和有限时间收敛保证。数值实验支持理论解释，并展示了该方法如何在目标下降与保持学习几何之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的投影梯度引导更新是一种有效的推理期约束优化采样方法，既能驱动目标函数下降，又能通过Stein去噪算子的近似投影性质保持样本落在学习到的数据几何上。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used not only for sampling from learned data distributions, but also for generating samples that optimize task-specific objectives. A common approach is to guide the reverse diffusion process using gradients of an external objective. However, when the data distribution is supported on a structured feasible set, such as a manifold or a constraint set, gradient guidance can move samples away from the learned data geometry. In this paper, we study a simple projected-gradient-guided diffusion update based on the observation that the Stein denoising operator can act as an approximate projection onto the data geometry. The proposed update incorporates the objective gradient inside the denoising step, yielding an inference-time method that uses only a pretrained denoiser and gradient evaluations. We analyze this update as an inexact projected-gradient method for constrained optimization over learned feasible geometries. Our theory covers three settings: linear manifolds, compact convex feasible sets, and compact Riemannian submanifolds. In all these settings, we prove descent and finite-time convergence guarantees. Numerical experiments support the theoretical interpretation and illustrate how the proposed update balances objective descent with preservation of the learned geometry.

</details>


### [267] [A Causal Model for Locating and Unlocking Sandbagging in Model Organisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29461)
*Hong Kiat Tan,Linh Le,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种关于沙袋效应(sandbagging)如何在残差流中运行的因果模型：早期层将沙袋意图写入单一坐标轴，后期层读取该坐标轴并提交答案。作者研究了三种指令微调模型和四种安装沙袋锁的方式，提出单层嫁接(single-layer graft)能在多数场景下恢复模型能力，并进一步提出上下文嫁接(context grafting)方法，利用密码的缓存KV激活在所有注意力层上恢复完整能力，且恢复效果对密码内容不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙袋模型（在评测中故意表现不佳但保留真实能力）会严重低估前沿模型的部署与治理评估结果，导致模型真实能力被掩盖。理解沙袋效应在模型内部如何运作的机制，是设计有效审计方法的前提，但目前缺乏对此机制的因果层面解释。

Method: 作者提出一个两阶段因果模型：早期层将沙袋意图写入残差流中的一个单一坐标轴，后期层读取该轴并决定答案。研究覆盖三种指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3-8B, Mistral-7B）和四种沙袋锁安装方式（提示、微调、强化学习、电路截断），并设计了两种干预方法：单层参考嫁接（将沙袋轴在特定层替换为诚实值）和上下文嫁接（重放密码的缓存键值激活并将其作为附加上下文供所有注意力层读取）。

Result: 单层嫁接在33次运行中的28次（提示、微调和RL锁）恢复了模型能力，中位隐藏能力恢复率达96%；但电路截断锁通过一层波段重写整个状态，单层嫁接在所有层均失败。上下文嫁接则在所有三种模型上均能可证明地且经验性地恢复完整能力，且恢复效果对密码具体内容出奇地不敏感。

Conclusion: 该因果模型为审计员提供了一种设计干预性审计技术的框架，可用于检测和揭示沙袋模型的能力。上下文嫁接作为一种鲁棒的干预手段，即使面对电路截断这种更隐蔽的沙袋实现方式，也能可靠地恢复被隐藏的能力。

Abstract: Sandbagging models strategically underperform on evaluations while retaining the capabilities being measured. The evaluations that guide frontier-model deployment and governance then understate what these models can do. To understand the mechanism, we propose a causal model of how sandbagging is carried in the residual stream. Early layers write the sandbagging intent onto a single axis of the stream, and a later layer reads that axis and commits the answer. We study three instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3-8B, and Mistral-7B) and four ways of installing a sandbagging lock (prompting, fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and circuit breaking). Each lock decides from the prompt whether to sandbag, and the fine-tuned and circuit-broken locks answer honestly whenever a password appears. The causal model predicts a window of layers, after the last sandbagging write and before the answer commit, in which a single-layer reference graft of the sandbagging axis to its honest value restores the full capability. The single-layer graft recovers the capability in 28 of the 33 runs of the prompted, fine-tuned, and RL-trained locks, with a median held-out recovery of 96%. The circuit-broken lock rewrites the whole state through a band of layers, and the single-layer graft fails at every layer. We therefore introduce a second intervention, context grafting, which replays the password's cached key/value activations so that every layer's attention reads them as additional context. Context grafting provably and empirically restores the full capability on all three models, and the recovery is surprisingly insensitive to the exact password content. More broadly, an auditor can use this causal model to design interventional auditing techniques for sandbagging models.

</details>


### [268] [Knowledge Distillation under Teacher Misspecification: An Order-Parameter Analysis of the Gap between Teacher Mimicry and Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29472)
*Kazuyuki Hara,Hideitsu Hino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究知识蒸馏中"师生差异"与"真实任务误差"之间的关系，发现蒸馏误差对教师模型与真实生成模型之间的失配参数完全不变，而真实误差和两者之间的差距则随失配严格增加，且差距可作为区分"教师失配"与"容量受限"失败的最小诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏通常用师生差异来监控训练进度，但最终关心的应该是学生模型相对真实任务的误差。本文旨在揭示这两个目标之间的关系——特别是当教师模型无法完全表示真实任务中的潜在因子时，蒸馏误差是否仍能反映学生模型的真实表现。

Method: 构建一个最小三方模型：真实教师（生成模型）、教师、学生，均为软委员会机器（soft committee machines）。真实教师包含一个教师无法表示的共享潜在因子，失配强度由单一标量控制。利用在线蒸馏的序参量描述，并在误差函数激活下借助闭式（arcsine型）表达式，严格证明学习动力学和蒸馏误差对失配参数不变，而真实误差和差距随失配严格增加。

Result: 蒸馏误差对失配参数完全不变；真实误差和差距Δ随失配严格增加，增长率被真实教师复杂度线性放大；数值相图证实蒸馏误差等值线不动而真实误差地形整体抬升，教师失配区域随增强而扩大。

Conclusion: 研究给出了对仅用教师模仿指标评估蒸馏的定量警告，并确定差距Δ作为区分教师失配与容量受限失败的最小诊断工具。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation trains a small student model to reproduce the outputs of a large teacher model, and its progress is typically monitored through the teacher--student discrepancy. The quantity of ultimate interest, however, is the student's error with respect to the true task. We study the relation between these two objectives in a minimal three-party model, a true teacher (generative model), a teacher, and a student, all soft committee machines, in which the true teacher contains a shared latent factor that the teacher cannot represent, with mismatch strength controlled by a single scalar $\dmiss$. Within an order-parameter description of online distillation, and exploiting closed-form (arcsine-type) expressions for all errors under error-function activations, we prove that the learning dynamics and the distillation error $\Ets$ are exactly invariant to $\dmiss$, whereas the true error $\Etzs$ and the gap $Δ=\Etzs-\Ets$ are strictly increasing in $\dmiss$, with a rate that is amplified linearly by the complexity $M_0$ of the true teacher. Numerical phase diagrams over the plane spanned by true-teacher complexity and student capacity confirm the predicted deformation: the contours of $\Ets$ do not move while the landscape of $\Etzs$ rises systematically, and a teacher-miss regime, where mimicry succeeds but the task fails, expands with $\dmiss$. The results give a quantitative warning against evaluating distillation solely through teacher-mimicry metrics and identify the gap $Δ$ as a minimal diagnostic for distinguishing teacher-miss from capacity-limited failure.

</details>


### [269] [Convergence rates for the RMSprop optimizer with full control of the hyperparameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30382)
*Steffen Dereich,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了RMSprop优化器在凸随机优化场景下误差界常数的超参数一致有界性问题，给出了期望目标函数误差的非渐近上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMSprop、Adam和AdamW等自适应SGD方法在AI训练中广泛使用，但其误差估计中的常数是否能随超参数（如ε、β）保持一致有界这一问题长期悬而未决，即便在凸随机优化问题中也缺乏理论保证。

Method: 通过构造合适的二阶矩过程逆矩界（inverse moment bounds），将RMSprop的误差分解为初始化项（指数衰减）、随机逼近余项（γ_n阶）和记忆误差（(1-β)^2阶），并显式给出所有误差常数，使其对步长、β和ε∈[0,1]（含ε=0）均匀可控。

Result: 对每一步梯度迭代n=1,2,3,...均成立非渐近误差估计，误差常数对超参数一致有界，彻底解决了RMSprop的理论开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作本质上解决了RMSprop在凸随机优化中误差常数随超参数爆炸的问题，其逆矩界证明技术可能为Adam/AdamW的理论分析提供借鉴。

Abstract: Popular adaptive stochastic gradient descent (SGD) methods to train artificial intelligence (AI) systems include the RMSprop, the Adam, and the AdamW optimizers, where the adaptivity parts in Adam and AdamW basically just coincide with RMSprop. Such adaptive methods involve several hyperparameters including the regularization parameter $ε$ (which ensures that one does not divide by 0 and is often chosen to be very close to zero such as $10^{-8}$ in PyTorch by default) and the second moment decay parameter $β$ (which is often chosen to be very close to $1$ such as 0.99 (RMSprop) and 0.999 (Adam and AdamW) in PyTorch by default). Despite the high relevance of such methods, it remains an open research problem to provide error estimates for such methods with the error constants being not exploding but uniformly bounded with the respect to the hyperparameters, even in the situation of convex stochastic optimization problems.
  It is the key contribution of this work to essentially solve this problem for RMSprop. Specifically, we bound the expectation of the stopped evaluation of the objective function at the RMSprop process from above by the sum of an initialization term that decays exponentially in the training time, a stochastic approximation remainder of order $γ_n$, and a memory error of order $( 1 - β)^2$ with the error constants being uniformly controlled over all admissible choices of the step sizes, the second moment decay parameter $β$ and the regularization parameter $ε\in[0,1]$ (also covering $ε=0$). Our non-asymptotic error estimates hold not just for all sufficiently large n but hold for every gradient step $n=1,2,3,...$ with all error constants being explicitly specified. The key innovative new feature in the proof of our analysis are suitable inverse moment bounds for the second moment process in RMSprop.

</details>


### [270] [Learning Human Health and Diseases from 24-hour Wrist Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29494)
*Yong Wang,Dylan McGagh,Katya Broomberg,Zizheng Zhang,Jonathan Carter,Junayed Naushad,Laura Brocklebank,Yang Sun,George Nicholson,Dianjianyi Sun,Canqing Yu,Jun Lv,Maxim Barnard,Hubert Lam,Andrew Steptoe,David W. Eyre,Liming Li,Zhengming Chen,Naomi Wray,Spiros Denaxas,Gary S. Collins,Huaidong Du,Aiden Doherty,Hang Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sensori，一个基于自监督学习的基础模型，直接从24小时原始三轴腕部运动数据中学习通用健康表征。基于美英中四个人群队列共122,640名参与者、683,617人日的自由生活记录进行评估，该模型在独立队列中无需重训练即可泛化，并在52/102种现患疾病分类和26/87种新发疾病风险预测中显著提高了临床协变量基础上的性能，尤其对神经和精神疾病增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可穿戴加速度计研究往往将丰富的高频原始信号降维为一小组预定义的日常行为汇总指标（如步数、活动量），丢失了大量细微的运动信息。开发能够直接从原始信号中学习通用健康表征的基础模型，有望充分挖掘腕部运动数据在被动健康监测和疾病预测方面的潜力。

Method: Sensori采用自监督学习范式，以24小时原始三轴腕部加速度信号为输入，学习通用健康表征。模型在四个来自英国、中国和美国的基于人群的队列中进行开发和评估，涵盖122,640名参与者、683,617人日的自由生活记录。表征质量通过多种维度检验，包括运动行为多样性、人口学特征、健康轴和身体功能；并在独立队列中验证跨人群和测量设备设置的泛化性。

Result: Sensori的表征在独立队列中展现出无需重训练的跨人群泛化能力。在叠加常见临床协变量后，模型在102种符合条件的现患疾病分类中显著改善了52种（AUROC中位增量0.060，范围0.012-0.242），在87种符合条件的疾病中改善了26种的新发风险预测（Uno's C-index中位增量0.064，范围0.025-0.172），其中神经和精神疾病的增益最为突出。

Conclusion: 研究确立了24小时腕部运动作为一种丰富且可扩展的健康信息来源，Sensori基础模型展示了从原始加速度数据中提取通用健康表征的可行性，为人群规模下的被动健康监测和疾病预测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Much of human health and function unfolds beyond the clinic, through the movements of everyday life. Wrist-worn accelerometers capture these movements continuously, yet their rich signals are often reduced to a small set of predefined behavioural summary measures. Here, we present Sensori, a self-supervised foundation model that learns general-purpose health representations directly from 24 hours of raw tri-axial wrist movement. We developed and evaluated the model across four population-based cohorts from the United Kingdom, China and the United States, comprising 122,640 participants contributing 683,617 person-days of free-living recordings. Sensori condensed each day of movement into a representation that captured diverse movement behaviours, demographic characteristics, health axes and physical function. Evaluation in independent cohorts showed that these representations generalised across populations and measurement settings without retraining. When added to common clinical covariates, Sensori significantly improved prevalent disease classification for 52 of 102 eligible conditions (median delta AUROC, 0.060; range, 0.012-0.242) and incident disease risk prediction for 26 of 87 eligible conditions (median delta Uno's C-index, 0.064; range, 0.025-0.172), with the largest gains for neurological and psychiatric disorders. These findings establish 24-hour wrist movement as a rich and scalable source of health information, with the potential to support passive health monitoring and disease prediction at population scale.

</details>


### [271] [Trajectory-Initialized Neural Double Q-Routing for Large-Scale Overhead Hoist Transport Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30512)
*Cheng Gu,Qiusheng Zhao,Anbang Liu,Shaochong Lin,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural Double Q-routing方法，用于大规模半导体工厂OHT（顶棚悬吊运输）系统中的车辆调度路由问题。该方法用共享的state-action值网络替代传统的按目的地索引的Q表，通过离线仿真预热（return-to-go回归）和在线Double-Q更新、局部拥塞修正及事件分层结构回放进行优化，在100/150/200个OHT的九种设置下将平均完工时间相对表格化Double Q-routing降低0.8%-8.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模工业机器人车队共享受限的物理基础设施，车辆行驶时间依赖安全间距、交叉口通行、下游阻塞和站点竞争等时变因素。静态最短路径路由无法刻画这些时变交通代价，而表格化Q-routing虽能在线自适应，但每个目的地-节点-动作值独立学习，限制了在稀疏访问路由上下文中的信息共享，且启动阶段对不准确的值估计敏感。

Method: 将按目的地索引的表格替换为共享的state-action值网络。网络通过混合模拟器生成路由轨迹的return-to-go回归进行离线预热，然后在线使用Double-Q更新、局部拥塞修正和事件分层结构化回放进行细化训练。

Result: 在九种匹配的车队规模-到达率设置（100/150/200个OHT）中，该框架将平均完工时间相对表格化Double Q-routing降低0.8%-8.8%。在六个150和200-OHT设置中取得最低平均完工时间，而Dijkstra在三个100-OHT设置中仍最优。八个设置中完成任务数与表格化Double Q-routing差距在1%以内，八个设置中95百分位完工时间下降。在两种匹配启动场景中，离线初始化使完成的任务数最多增加23%，尾部完工时间最多减少15%。

Conclusion: Neural Double Q-routing通过神经网络的泛化能力改进了表格化Q-routing在稀疏路由上下文中的信息共享和启动行为，在中等至大规模OHT车队场景中一致优于基线方法，尤其对大规模系统性能提升显著。

Abstract: Large-scale industrial robot fleets share constrained physical infrastructure, making vehicle travel times dependent on safety separation, intersection access, downstream blocking, and station contention. We study this problem in overhead hoist transport (OHT) systems, a representative ceiling-mounted material-handling system used in semiconductor fabs. Static shortest-path routing cannot account for these time-varying traffic costs, whereas tabular Q-routing adapts online but learns each destination--node--action value independently, limiting information sharing across sparsely visited routing contexts and making startup behavior sensitive to inaccurate value estimates. We propose Neural Double Q-routing, which replaces destination-indexed tables with a shared state--action value network. The network is warm-started through return-to-go regression on mixed simulator-generated routing trajectories and then refined online using Double-Q updates, local congestion correction, and event-stratified structured replay. Across nine matched fleet-size--arrival-rate settings with 100, 150, and 200 OHTs, the proposed framework reduces mean completion time relative to tabular Double Q-routing by $0.8\%$--$8.8\%$. It achieves the lowest mean completion time among all compared methods in the six 150- and 200-OHT settings, whereas Dijkstra remains best in the three 100-OHT settings. Completed-task counts remain within $1\%$ of tabular Double Q-routing in eight of nine settings, and 95th-percentile completion time decreases in eight settings. In two matched startup scenarios, offline initialization increases the number of completed tasks by up to $23\%$ and reduces tail completion time by up to $15\%$.

</details>


### [272] [Target-Aware State-Adaptive $p$-Dirichlet Graph Neural Regression for Non-Invasive Body-Composition Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29496)
*Nadejda Drenska,Matthew Lemoine,Gowri Priya Sunkara,Yu Wang,Sri Lakshmi Sravani Devarakonda,Steven B. Heymsfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了pSADE-GNR框架，一种目标感知、状态自适应的p-Dirichlet能量流图神经回归方法，利用非侵入性人体测量数据估计身体成分指标（BFP、BMD、ALM）。在Pennington生物医学研究中心的临床数据上，五折交叉验证中该模型在九个主要结局-队列组合中全部取得最低RMSE，并在八项对比中优于支持向量回归等参考方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估计身体成分（脂肪百分比、骨密度、肌肉量）对代谢、骨骼和肌肉健康的评估至关重要，但金标准DXA方法需要专用设备并涉及电离辐射。因此需要一种无需辐射、仅基于非侵入性人体测量的精确估计方法。

Method: 设计目标感知、状态自适应的p-Dirichlet能量流图神经网络回归框架。具体包括：1）神经编码器将参与者表征映射为隐状态；2）在结局特定的参与者相似性图上，通过图p-Dirichlet能量流的状态自适应前向欧拉离散化传播隐状态；3）图距离通过归一化绝对训练折相关系数加权原始或潜在坐标。同时比较了自编码器、变分自编码器和高斯混合变分自编码器三种潜在表征。

Result: 在Pennington生物医学研究中心临床数据上，使用原始标准化测量的相关系数加权模型在全部九个主要结局-队列组合中取得了最低RMSE，且在八项中优于已报道的SVR或LSSVR参考值。自编码器类表征通常未能提升预测精度或降低计算成本。在探索性年龄预测分析中，相关系数加权GMVAE模型在全部三个队列中取得最低平均误差。

Conclusion: 目标感知、状态自适应的p-Dirichlet图神经回归为非侵入性身体成分估计提供了有效的解决方案，验证了结合目标相关信息加权的图能量流传播机制在该任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of body-composition outcomes, including body fat percentage (BFP), bone mineral density (BMD), and appendicular lean mass (ALM), is important for evaluating metabolic, skeletal, and muscular health. Direct assessment using dual-energy X-ray absorptiometry (DXA), however, requires specialized equipment and involves ionizing radiation. We propose a target-aware, state-adaptive $p$-Dirichlet energy-flow graph neural regression ($p$SADE-GNR) framework for estimating these outcomes from non-invasive anthropometric measurements. A neural encoder maps participant representations to hidden states that are propagated over an outcome-specific participant-similarity graph by a state-adaptive forward-Euler discretization of the graph $p$-Dirichlet energy flow. Graph distances weight each original or latent coordinate by its normalized absolute training-fold correlation with the outcome. Using clinical data from the Pennington Biomedical Research Center and five-fold cross-validation, the correlation-weighted model using the original standardized measurements achieved the lowest root mean squared error in all nine primary outcome-cohort combinations and outperformed previously reported support vector regression or least-squares support vector regression reference values in eight of nine comparisons. Autoencoder, variational-autoencoder, and Gaussian-mixture variational-autoencoder representations generally did not improve primary-outcome prediction or reduce computational cost. In an exploratory age-prediction analysis including ALM, BMD, and BFP as predictors, the correlation-weighted GMVAE model achieved the lowest mean error in all three cohorts. These results support target-aware, state-adaptive $p$-Dirichlet graph neural regression for non-invasive body-composition estimation.

</details>


### [273] [Adversarial Online Classification with a Preview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29503)
*Roi Livni,Sahil Singla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线分类中的"预览模型"：对抗性对手固定整个长度为T的标注序列，预测开始前随机暴露pT个样本，剩余样本按原始对抗顺序呈现。作者刻画了最优超额损失对预览率p的依赖：二分类VC维为d的类别，最优超额损失为Θ(d/p+√(dT))；多分类类别对应的上界为Õ(d_DS/p+√(d_Nat T))，与标签数量无关。随机预览可以将最坏情况序贯复杂度（如Littlestone维）替换为经典统计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最坏情况在线分类由序贯复杂度（如Littlestone维）主导，即使是统计上简单的类别（如VC维为1的阈值函数）也可能是不可学习的。作者希望探究在一个更现实的预览模型下，随机预览能否将序贯复杂度降为经典的统计维度，从而突破在线学习的困难边界。

Method: 作者提出了ChainedPrediction算法，该算法采用在线版的chaining技术，实现为一种多尺度聚合算法（multiscale aggregation algorithm），而非仅作为分析论证工具。通过随机预览固定比例的样本，并结合经典统计维度（VC维、Dudley-Shatter维、Natarajan维）来刻画最优超额损失。

Result: 对于二分类VC维为d的类别，最优超额损失为Θ(d/p+√(dT))（截断至T）；对于多分类类别，得到Õ(d_DS/p+√(d_Nat T))的上界，且与标签数量无关。随机预览可以替代最坏情况序贯复杂度为经典统计维度，无需随机化在线顺序。

Conclusion: 随机预览模型提供了一种在保持对抗性在线顺序的同时，利用经典统计复杂度实现可学习性的途径。预览率p越大，序贯困难程度越低，最终超额损失由统计维度项√(dT)和预览相关项d/p共同决定。

Abstract: Worst-case online classification is governed by sequential complexity, such as Littlestone dimension, and can be impossible even for statistically simple classes, such as thresholds of VC dimension one. We study a preview model in which an oblivious adversary fixes an entire labeled sequence of length $T$, a uniformly random subset of size $pT$ is revealed before prediction begins, and the remaining $(1-p)T$ examples are then presented in their original adversarial order.
  Against the best full-sequence hypothesis evaluated on the unrevealed examples, we characterize the dependence on the preview rate $p$: for binary classes of VC dimension $d$, the optimal excess loss is $Θ(d/p+\sqrt{dT})$, up to the trivial cap at $T$; for multiclass classes we obtain the corresponding $\widetilde O(d_{\rm DS}/p+\sqrt{d_{\rm Nat}T})$ bound with no dependence on the number of labels. Thus a random preview can replace worst-case sequential complexity by classical statistical dimensions without randomizing the online order. To achieve the sharp binary bound, our ChainedPrediction algorithm uses an online analogue of chaining, implemented as a multiscale aggregation algorithm rather than only as an analytic argument.

</details>


### [274] [On the Plasticity Collapse in Continual Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29513)
*Yingdan Shi,Xiang Xu,Kaize Ding,Alfred O. Hero,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了连续机器遗忘（continual machine unlearning）中的一个根本性缺陷——可塑性崩溃（plasticity collapse），即模型在连续处理多个遗忘请求时，其遗忘能力逐渐退化的现象。通过理论分析和大量实验，作者证明参数空间中的几何约束累积会导致饱和子空间，从而引发前向失败（后续遗忘质量下降）和后向失败（已遗忘信息被自发重新记忆），这对机器遗忘系统的长期可靠性构成关键障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要聚焦于单次遗忘（single-shot unlearning），但现实系统必须应对连续遗忘场景——多个遗忘请求按时间顺序不断到达。作者识别出该场景下的一个根本性局限：可塑性崩溃，即模型逐渐丧失有效遗忘的能力。这一发现对机器学习系统的长期隐私保障和法规合规具有重要意义。

Method: 作者首先对连续遗忘动力学进行理论分析，证明连续遗忘操作会在参数空间中累积几何约束，导致饱和子空间（saturated subspaces）的产生，从而限制后续更新。这种结构性效应进一步被分类为两种失败模式：前向失败（forward failure）和后向失败（backward failure）。随后，作者在多种架构、数据集和遗忘方法上进行了大量图像分类实验来验证该现象。

Result: 跨多种架构、数据集和方法的广泛实验表明，可塑性崩溃并非特定实现造成的偶然现象，而是连续遗忘中固有的普遍性缺陷。实验证实了前向失败（遗忘质量随时间下降）和后向失败（先前遗忘的信息被再次记忆）两种失败模式的普遍存在。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了连续机器遗忘系统长期可靠性的关键障碍，强调了开发保持可塑性（plasticity-preserving）的遗忘算法的必要性，为未来机器遗忘研究指明了重要方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: Machine unlearning enables deep neural networks to selectively remove the influence of specific data in response to privacy and regulatory requirements. While prior work largely studies single-shot unlearning, real-world systems must accommodate continual unlearning, where multiple unlearning requests occur sequentially over time. In this work, we identify a fundamental limitation of this setting: plasticity collapse, a progressive breakdown in a model's ability to effectively forget. Through theoretical analysis of continual unlearning dynamics, we show that continual unlearning operations accumulate geometric constraints in parameter space, leading to saturated subspaces that restrict future updates. This structural effect induces two distinct failure modes: (1) Forward failure -- diminishing forgetting quality for subsequent tasks, and (2) Backward failure -- spontaneous re-memorization of previously forgotten information. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures, datasets, and methods in image classification confirm that plasticity collapse is not an artifact of specific implementations, but a pervasive phenomenon inherent to continual unlearning. Our findings reveal a critical barrier to the long-term reliability of machine unlearning systems and motivate the development of plasticity-preserving unlearning algorithms. Our code is available at https://github.com/TIML-Group/Continual-Machine-Unlearning-Plasticity-Collapse

</details>


### [275] [MedCache: Efficient and Temporally Valid Memory for Longitudinal Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29528)
*Hei Ting,Chan,Chenwei Wu,Xueshen Liu,Boyuan Zheng,Liyue Shen,Jiasi Chen,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MedCache框架，一种专门为纵向临床Agent设计的多轮随访、多专科患者记忆管理系统。通过构建时间有效的患者记忆、组织重叠专科视图、按查询路由到相关记忆，并自适应调用一个或多个专科Agent，显著提升推理准确率和记忆效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床Agent需要从跨就诊、跨时间点、跨专科的分散证据中维持不断演变的患者状态，但Agent记忆（memory）应如何设计这一核心问题尚未被系统研究。缺乏针对该场景的基准评测与设计指导。

Method: 1) 构建多就诊、多专科患者记录基准，评测长上下文证据检索、跨时间证据聚合和跨专科临床推理；2) 系统研究四个记忆设计选择：策展（curation）、组织（organization）、检索（retrieval）和记忆增强推理（memory-augmented reasoning）；3) 提出MedCache混合框架：构建时间有效的患者记忆，将证据组织为重叠的专科视图，将查询路由至相关记忆，自适应调用单个或多个专科Agent。

Result: 实验表明MedCache相比强单Agent和多Agent基线显著提升推理准确率与记忆效率，并在不同模型基座和外部数据集上表现出良好的泛化能力。关键发现包括：时间有效性比简单保留更多历史更重要；专科分解记忆能减少上下文但可能隐藏共享证据；多Agent仅在专科需要协同推理时有帮助。

Conclusion: 纵向临床记忆设计应优先考虑证据的时间有效性，专科视图需保留重叠部分以捕获共享证据，多Agent协作应根据推理需求自适应触发而非一律使用。MedCache验证了这些原则在提升临床Agent能力上的有效性。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical agents must maintain an evolving patient state from evidence distributed across visits, time points, and specialties.
