Comprehensive numerical experiments have been conducted to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [377] [One Policy Is Enough: Single-Agent Reinforcement Learning Outperforms Tree Search for Chemistry Tool Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30952)
*Armin Dariani,Sifan Wu,Bang Liu,Entao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化的化学工具调用方法，仅使用单一策略模型，将推理、工具调用和返回结果统一为从左到右的自回归生成，通过监督预热加结果级强化学习（基于金标准工具调用链的程序化奖励）进行训练，无需学习型批评器或裁判模型。在ChemToolBench多个工具的综合化学基准上，相比CheMatAgent的层级进化MCTS搜索方法，在Qwen-2.5-7B上工具F1提升5.5%、返回F1提升9.6%，在Llama-3.1-8B上分别提升3.7%和3.9%，且每次问题仅需一次模型调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学问题需要精确计算和数据库查询，语言模型必须借助外部工具。现有CheMatAgent系统使用层级进化MCTS，依赖分离的策略和执行模型、两个学习的批评器以及GPT分配的分数，训练和推理流程复杂。作者的核心动机是探究是否可以用一个单一策略模型简化整个流程，消除学习型批评器和裁判的依赖，同时达到或超越原有性能。

Method: 作者的模型将推理、工具调用和返回结果交织为单一的从左到右（left-to-right）自回归生成序列。训练分两个阶段：先用监督学习进行预热（warm-up），再进行基于结果（outcome-level）的强化学习。强化学习的奖励是程序化的，直接根据金标准工具调用链计算得出，不依赖任何学习型批评器或GPT裁判。测试时仅需一次模型调用，无需像MCTS那样随搜索树增长而增加开销。

Result: 在ChemToolBench多工具综合化学基准上，针对CheMatAgent使用的两种骨干模型：在Qwen-2.5-7B上，工具F1提升5.5%，返回F1提升9.6%，答案通过率也领先；在Llama-3.1-8B上，工具F1提升3.7%，返回F1提升3.9%。这些改进相对于CheMatAgent最强搜索配置，且本方法每次问题仅需一次模型调用，而对方搜索成本随树规模增长。

Conclusion: 作者得出的结论是：在化学工具调用任务中，单个策略模型通过简化的监督+强化学习训练流程就足以替代复杂的层级MCTS搜索框架。该方法不仅性能更好，而且在推理效率上具有显著优势（单次调用对比搜索树式开销），证明了"少即是多"——省去学习型批评器和裁判模型不仅简化了系统，还带来了性能提升。

Abstract: Chemistry questions often demand exact computation and database lookups that a language model cannot supply from its parameters, so it must reach for external tools. Tool use here is a three-part problem: select the right tool from a large pool, fill it with correctly typed arguments, and chain calls so that each consumes the outputs of the last. CheMatAgent, a previously published system, addresses this with hierarchical evolutionary MCTS: separate policy and execution models searching tool-call trees under two learned critics, one regressed partly onto GPT-assigned scores. We show that a single policy suffices. Our model interleaves reasoning, tool calls, and returns in one left-to-right generation, trained by a supervised warm-up and then outcome-level reinforcement learning against a programmatic reward read directly off the gold call chain, which leaves no learned critic and no judge in the training loop. On ChemToolBench multiple-tool comprehensive chemistry, on both backbones CheMatAgent use, we improve Tool F1 by 5.5% and Return F1 by 9.6% on Qwen-2.5-7B, and by 3.7% and 3.9% on Llama-3.1-8B, compared with their strongest search configuration, at one model invocation per question, against a search whose cost grows with the tree; we also lead answer Pass Rate on Qwen-2.5-7B.

</details>


### [378] [Singular Curvature in ReLU Training:Differentiation and the Gradient-Flow Limit Need Not Commute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30960)
*Xiaoyang Li,Runni Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究梯度下降（GD）作为梯度流的显式欧拉离散化时，直接离散状态与连续极限流在灵敏度（导数）意义上的差异。证明在固定非共振步长和有限时域下，GD状态的精确离散梯度（通过自动微分计算）收敛到一个事件无关的区域传播子，而极限流的导数包含速度归一化的激活事件传递。通过引入Stieltjes表示将绝对连续区域Hessian与原子界面曲率分离，并指出一个非零梯度跳变产生精确秩一的端点差异，全局凸性阻止多事件完全抵消。最后证明一族全局1-强凸的residual-ReLU平方损失风险在开放初始化集上可实现任意大的互反灵敏度比，但该结果限定于确定性全批次、有限时域、事件分离且同向横截的场景，属于一致性定理而非大规模训练的普适性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动微分对离散GD程序求得的梯度是否收敛到连续梯度流（ODEsensitivity）的导数，这一问题在深度学习的理论理解中至关重要。先前研究多关注状态轨迹的收敛性，但导数的收敛性（即"离散-连续灵敏度差异"）未被系统处理，尤其在ReLU这类非光滑激活函数引发的事件（激活边界穿越）处。本文旨在严格刻画离散导数与连续流导数之间的结构差异，解释为什么对"状态精确"的连续替代模型求导可能不准确。

Method: 采用非光滑动力系统与测度论工具。首先建立固定步长下GD迭代的精确离散导数收敛到"事件无关区域传播子"的定理；然后引入prepoint Stieltjes积分表示，将连续流导数分解为绝对连续部分（区域Hessian）与原子部分（界面曲率的加权脉冲）；通过构造特定的residual-ReLU平方损失族证明互反灵敏度比可任意大的开集存在性；最后将分解推广到参数梯度和反向模式伴随，并讨论平滑化与事件定位如何恢复流动灵敏度。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 离散自动微分导数收敛到事件无关的区域传播子，而连续极限流导数包含额外的事件转移项（速度归一化的激活事件贡献）；(2) 单个梯度跳变产生精确秩一的端点灵敏度差异；(3) 在全局1-强凸条件下，严格事件不会被完全抵消，但存在开放初始化集使互反灵敏度比任意大（即便有统一横截性余量）；(4) 该分解对参数和反向模式同样成立，通过合适的平滑或事件定位可恢复连续灵敏度。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了"状态收敛"与"导数收敛"之间的根本区别：离散GD的精确梯度与连续流动灵敏度之间存在不可消除的结构性分歧，源于激活事件的原子贡献。结论是这些结果的一致性定理性质而非大范围普适性主张——其有效性条件是确定性全批量、有限时域、有限数量的分离同向横截事件。这为理解为什么在ReLU网络上通过自动微分获得的梯度在理论上不完全等同于连续ODE流形框架中的灵敏度提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: Gradient descent (GD) is explicit Euler for gradient flow, but a state-accurate continuous-time surrogate need not remain accurate after differentiation. At every fixed nonresonant step size, ordinary automatic differentiation exactly differentiates the executed hard-ReLU GD program. We prove that, over a fixed finite horizon, the GD states converge and these exact discrete derivatives approach an event-free regional propagator, whereas the derivative of the limiting flow also contains speed-normalized activation-event transfers. A prepoint Stieltjes representation separates the absolutely continuous regional Hessian from atomic interface curvature; one nonzero gradient jump produces an exactly rank-one endpoint discrepancy, and global convexity prevents complete multi-event cancellation whenever an event is strict. Nevertheless, a standard family of globally 1-strongly convex residual-ReLU squared-loss risks realizes arbitrarily large reciprocal sensitivity ratios on open initialization sets, with a uniform transversality margin. The same discrete-versus-flow decomposition extends to parameters and reverse-mode adjoints; resolved smoothing in the scalar or autonomous-normal regime and consistent event localization recover the flow sensitivity. The results concern deterministic full-batch, finite-horizon dynamics with a stable finite itinerary of separated same-direction transverse events; they are consistency theorems, not prevalence claims for large-scale training.

</details>


### [379] [A Universal Context-Reuse Layer for Cross-Model KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30963)
*Yi Li,Dongming Jiang,Yi Zhao,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究跨模型KV缓存共享（cross-model KV sharing），即把源模型产生的KV状态翻译成目标模型可消费的表示，支持不同规模、架构、注意力配置、分词器和模型族的模型之间共享KV缓存。实验表明，在同类模型（Qwen2.5-7B→1.5B）和跨族模型（Qwen2.5→Gemma-2、Llama3.1→Qwen2.5）设置中，翻译后的KV状态均能提升推理准确性、降低预填充成本和延迟，为KV状态作为可迁移的计算表示提供了初步证据，并提出了"上下文移动性"（context mobility）这一系统抽象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统往往对重复或共享的上下文执行冗余的预填充计算，而现有的KV缓存复用机制通常假设缓存的生产者和消费者是同一模型（within-model），无法在异构模型之间共享KV状态。随着多模型、多智能体推理工作流的普及，跨模型之间的重复预填充浪费日益严重，亟需一种机制使KV状态在不同模型间可移植。

Method: 提出跨模型KV翻译（cross-model KV translation）方法，将源模型生成的KV状态通过翻译机制转换为目标模型可消费的表示形式。该方法不假设生产和消费模型相同，适用于模型规模、架构、注意力配置、分词器和模型族均不同的场景。研究在within-family（Qwen2.5-7B→1.5B）和cross-family（Qwen2.5-1.5B→Gemma-2-2B、Llama3.1-70B→Qwen2.5-7B）两种设置下进行系统评估。

Result: 1）Qwen2.5-7B→1.5B：翻译后的KV状态将LongBench2准确率从27.59%提升至34.48%（提升6.89个百分点），并降低交接成本；2）Qwen2.5-1.5B→Gemma-2-2B：在4K上下文长度下，KV交接将目标端预填充成本降低最高67.05%，解码困惑度与原生基线相近；3）Llama3.1-70B→Qwen2.5-7B：跨族交接达到44.0%准确率（vs原生45.7%），同时延迟从899ms降至138ms。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明KV状态可作为可迁移的计算表示（transferable computational representations），而非严格局限于模型的本地缓存。作者由此提出"上下文移动性"（context mobility）作为系统抽象，用于在异构LLM和多智能体推理工作流中减少冗余预填充，为后续系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) serving systems increasingly operate over repeated or shared context, yet each model typically performs its own prefill computation even when another model has already processed the same input. Existing KV-cache reuse mechanisms substantially reduce redundant computation within a single model, but generally assume that the producer and consumer of a cache are identical. We study \emph{cross-model KV sharing}, which translates the KV state produced by a source model into a representation that can be consumed by a different target model, including models that differ in scale, architecture, attention configuration, tokenizer, and model family. We evaluate the approach in both within-family and cross-family settings. For Qwen2.5-7B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-1.5B, translated KV states improve LongBench2 accuracy from 27.59\% to 34.48\%, a gain of 6.89 percentage points over the native 1.5B baseline, while reducing handoff cost relative to native target prefill. For the cross-family Qwen2.5-1.5B $\rightarrow$ Gemma-2-2B setting, KV handoff reduces target-side prefill cost by up to 67.05\% at 4K context length while maintaining decoding perplexity close to native-model baselines. In a more heterogeneous Llama3.1-70B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-7B setting, cross-family handoff achieves 44.0\% accuracy compared with 45.7\% for native Qwen2.5-7B inference, while reducing measured latency from 899ms to 138ms. These results provide initial evidence that KV states can serve as transferable computational representations rather than strictly model-local caches, and motivate \emph{context mobility} as a systems abstraction for reducing redundant prefill across heterogeneous LLM and multi-agent inference workflows.

</details>


### [380] [A Human-in-the-Loop Autonomous Agent for Industry Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30976)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Ze Guo,Bokai Pan,Qi Liu,Shijin Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CastClaw是一个面向真实世界时间序列预测的人机协作自主预测系统，通过预测导向的harness工程将数据、专业模型、分析工具、用户输入和版本化执行记录整合到一个运行时中。系统允许用户用自然语言指定目标、预测范围、约束和假设，并在显式停止条件下对预测结果进行保留、修订或升级。在五个数据集的电价预测场景中，CastClaw在16个基线中取得最低的MSE和MAE，并通过Nord Pool案例和华北省份电力负荷数据验证了其可检查的工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的时间序列预测很少是一次的模型调用：实践者必须制定任务、连接数据与模型、融入领域专业知识、评估预测合理性并沟通不确定性。专用预测模型虽提供强数值预测但通常局限于固定流程，而通用LLM代理缺乏预测特有的检查、约束和停止规则，因此需要一个结合两者优势的系统。

Method: CastClaw通过预测导向的harness工程构建：系统连接数据、专业模型、分析工具、用户输入和版本化执行记录于同一运行时。用户以自然语言指定目标、预测范围、约束和假设。系统从提供或模型生成的预测出发，检查时间模式与用户约束；当证据缺失时，检索上下文、运行分析或调用另一模型，或向用户询问。在显式停止条件下，系统对结果进行保留、修订或升级，最终输出包含最终预测和执行报告（记录输入、证据、行动和修订）。

Result: 在五个数据集的电价预测场景中，CastClaw在16个基线中取得了最低的点估计MSE和MAE。Nord Pool案例展示了可检查的工作流程。系统还在中国北方省份2026年1-6月的省级电力负荷数据上进行了离线验证。

Conclusion: CastClaw证明了一种将LLM代理的灵活性与预测专用检查、约束和停止规则相结合的自主预测系统架构，能够在保持人机交互的同时实现优于16个基线的预测精度，并提供可检查的执行透明度。

Abstract: Real-world time-series forecasting is rarely a one-shot model invocation: practitioners must formulate tasks, connect data and models, incorporate domain expertise, assess prediction plausibility, and communicate uncertainty. Specialized forecasting models provide strong numerical predictions but usually operate in fixed pipelines, while general-purpose large language model (LLM) agents often lack forecasting-specific checks, constraints, and stopping rules. We present CastClaw, a human-in-the-loop autonomous forecasting system built through forecasting-oriented harness engineering. CastClaw connects data, specialized models, analytical tools, user input, and a versioned execution record in one runtime. Users specify the target, horizon, constraints, and hypotheses in natural language. Starting from a supplied or model-generated forecast, CastClaw checks temporal patterns and user constraints; when evidence is missing, it retrieves context, runs an analysis or another model, or asks the user. It then keeps, revises, or escalates the result under explicit stopping conditions. The output contains the final forecast and an execution report recording inputs, evidence, actions, and revisions. In this five-dataset electricity-price setting, CastClaw reports the lowest point-estimate MSE and MAE among 16 baselines. A Nord Pool case demonstrates the inspectable workflow. CastClaw was also validated offline on provincial electricity-load data from North China covering January--June 2026.

</details>


### [381] [Sparse Competition during Training For the Emergence of Specialized Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30978)
*Baptiste Rossigneux,Karim Haroun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了深度神经网络中模块化结构的涌现，通过神经元组之间的竞争动力学来诱导基于使用情况的模块化，使得模块在没有模块级监督的情况下自发地响应语义类别（如狗或车辆），并在ImageNet-100和CIFAR-100上验证了该方法在保持接近基线精度的同时产生专用模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中的模块化被认为可以改善可解释性和训练效率，通过促进解耦表示和减少冗余实现。然而，如何在没有模块级监督的情况下自然地诱导出模块化结构仍是一个挑战。作者希望探索一种简单的机制——竞争动力学——来使标准架构中涌现出功能模块化。

Method: 作者提出一种方法：(i) 通过稀疏地将输入路由到神经元组来诱导基于使用情况的模块化；(ii) 鼓励模块的特化，使模块激活与输入类别相关；(iii) 在训练过程中利用神经元组之间的竞争动力学来促进模块的涌现。该方法不需要模块级别的监督信号。

Result: 在ImageNet-100和CIFAR-100数据集上评估，该方法保持了接近基线的准确率，并成功使专用模块涌现。这些模块捕捉了数据中的高层语义结构，单个模块响应语义类别（如狗或交通工具）。此外，作者还研究了模块数量对子任务层次划分的影响，表明竞争动力学可诱导出层次化的任务分区。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，竞争动力学可作为在标准架构中诱导功能模块化的简单机制。该方法无需模块级监督即可使模块自发涌现并特化到语义类别，为神经网络的可解释性和解耦表征学习提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Modularity in deep neural networks has been proposed as a means of improving both interpretability and training by promoting disentangled representations and reducing redundancy. In this work, we study the emergence of modular structure through competition dynamics between groups of neurons during training. We introduce a method that (i) maintains near-baseline accuracy, (ii) induces usage-based modularity by sparsely routing inputs to neuron groups, and (iii) encourages specialization of these modules, such that their activations are correlated with input classes. We evaluate the proposed approach on ImageNet-100 and CIFAR-100 and show that with it, specialized modules emerge without module-level supervision. These modules capture a meaningful high-level structure in the data, with individual modules responding to semantic categories (e.g., dogs or vehicles). We also study the emergence of a hierarchical partition of sub-tasks depending on the number of modules. Our results suggest that competitive dynamics can serve as a simple mechanism for inducing functional modularity in standard architectures.

</details>


### [382] [Controlling Refusal Behavior of LLMs via Stiefel-Constrained Rotation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30986)
*Kirill Bunin,Dmitry Bylinkin,Vladimir Aletov,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Solodkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于黎曼优化的自包含方法来学习参数高效的旋转变换，用于大语言模型的激活导向（Activation Steering），在不依赖拒绝向量等辅助构造的情况下实现对模型拒绝行为的控制，并在干预效率上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活导向技术（如基于可训练旋转的方法）在进行模型拒绝行为控制时，依赖于拒绝向量等辅助构造来定义旋转，缺乏自包含性和几何上的原理性。作者希望开发一种不依赖外部辅助构造的、基于黎曼优化的参数高效旋转学习方法，以更可靠地控制LLM行为。

Method: 作者提出一种自包含的参数高效旋转变换学习方法，利用黎曼优化直接在旋转流形上进行优化，学习激活空间的旋转变换，从而实现对模型拒绝行为的干预。该方法不需要预先定义拒绝向量等辅助构造，仅通过优化旋转参数本身即可实现导向效果。

Result: 实验验证表明，该方法在干预效率上优于现有技术；广泛的消融研究揭示了方法中关键设计选择的重要性，证明了基于旋转的导向方案在LLM行为控制方面具有前景。

Conclusion: 基于黎曼优化的旋转导向机制是一种有前景的方向，能够实现更可靠、更高效的大语言模型行为控制，避免了对外部辅助构造的依赖。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a lightweight approach for controlling model refusal at inference time. A growing line of research explores trainable rotations of activations to develop geometrically principled intervention mechanisms. However, existing techniques rely on auxiliary constructs, such as refusal vectors, to define these rotations. In our work, we develop a self-contained methodology for learning parameter-efficient rotational transformations based on Riemannian optimization. We empirically validate the proposed scheme, demonstrating its superiority in intervention efficiency. An extensive ablation study highlights the importance of key design choices in our method. Our results identify the proposed rotation-based steering scheme as a promising direction for more reliable control over the behavior of LLMs.

</details>


### [383] [Language-Informed Flow Matching for Trend-Guided Structure-Based 3D Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31009)
*Tianyu Gao,Zhikai Su,Jiashu Li,Wenjun Gao,Zichuan Ying,Zhe Zhao,Fei Zhang,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiFT是一个基于Flow Matching的语言信息跨模态框架，用于趋势引导的3D分子生成（包括从头设计和骨架跃迁）。该框架通过"Sense-Evolve-Assemble"智能体生成目标感知的SMILES作为中间化学条件，并由预训练化学基础模型提取连续语义先验，通过零初始化的语义投影器与自适应归一化整合到几何生成中。另引入Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router（SCDR）在ODE积分过程中根据中间结构状态调制速度场。在Cross-Docked2020上的实验表明，LiFT无需额外的生成器微调即可在任务导向条件下实现竞争性的分布匹配和更好的药用化学指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可控分子生成方法依赖任务特定的微调或采样时的外部引导，成本高且可能与不断演化的3D几何约束冲突。作者希望利用语言模型衍生的化学先验提供趋势级引导，在不增加生成器微调负担的情况下，同时满足3D靶标亲和力和1D化学有效性的双重要求。

Method: LiFT采用Flow Matching框架，核心包含三部分：(1) "Sense-Evolve-Assemble"智能体生成目标感知的SMILES作为中间化学条件；(2) 预训练化学基础模型从这些SMILES中提取连续语义先验；(3) 轻量级语义投影器配合零初始化的自适应归一化实现稳定的跨模态条件整合。同时引入Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router（SCDR），在ODE积分时根据中间结构状态动态调制速度场。

Result: 在Cross-Docked2020数据集上，LiFT在任务导向设置下实现了竞争性的分布匹配，同时改善了药用化学指标并保持竞争性的结构有效性，且无需额外的生成器微调。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，语言衍生的化学先验能够为3D分子生成提供有效的趋势级引导，验证了跨模态条件化方法在结构药物设计中的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) requires ligands that satisfy both 3D target affinity and 1D chemical validity. Existing controllable generation methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or externally imposed sampling-time guidance, adding cost and potentially conflicting with evolving 3D geometric constraints. We propose LiFT, a language-informed cross-modal framework built on Flow Matching for trend-guided 3D molecular generation across both de novo design and scaffold hopping. LiFT uses a "Sense-Evolve-Assemble" agent to generate target-aware SMILES as intermediate chemical conditions, from which a pre-trained chemical foundation model extracts continuous semantic priors. These priors are integrated into geometric generation through a lightweight semantic projector with zero-initialized adaptive normalization for stable cross-modal conditioning. We further introduce a Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router (SCDR), which modulates the velocity field according to intermediate structural states during ODE integration. Experiments on Cross-Docked2020 show that LiFT achieves competitive distribution matching while improving medicinal chemistry metrics and maintaining competitive structural validity under task-steering settings without additional generator fine-tuning. Our results suggest that language-derived chemical priors provide effective trend-level guidance for 3D molecular generation. Code and released artifacts are available at https://github.com/kasurl/LiFT.

</details>


### [384] [TSPFN: A Temporal Tabular Foundation Model for Physiological Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31013)
*Jérémie Stym-Popper,Clément Rambour,Federica Granese,Nicolas Thome,Olivier Bernard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSPFN，一个专为生理时间序列分类设计的基座模型。它重新设计了TabPFN的架构，整合结构化时间表征与位置嵌入以捕获样本内的时间与通道依赖，并在14万个跨医学领域的真实生理时间序列上预训练。实验表明TSPFN在多个生理基准上优于标准表格基线和TabPFN，且相比专门的深度时间序列模型具备更强的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学机器学习中，在低到中等数据规模下设计能有效泛化的模型仍是主要挑战，尤其是生理时间序列分类。虽然TabPFN等表格基座模型通过上下文学习提供了优于传统微调的替代方案，但它们并非为捕获生理信号固有的时间依赖关系而设计，无法充分利用时序结构信息。

Method: TSPFN重新设计TabPFN架构以适配时间序列数据：引入结构化时间表征和位置嵌入来捕获样本内的时间及通道依赖关系；为充分利用其时空设计，在14万个覆盖多个医学领域的真实生理时间序列上进行预训练，从而构建统一且可泛化的框架，学习医学时间序列的特异性。

Result: 在多个生理学基准上的实验表明，TSPFN一致优于标准表格基线和TabPFN；与专门的深度时间序列模型相比，TSPFN实现了更优的跨域泛化性能。所有实验、消融研究和预处理方案均已公开。

Conclusion: TSPFN通过将TabPFN的上下文学习能力与针对时间序列设计的时空架构相结合，并借助大规模多领域生理数据预训练，提供了一种在低到中等数据规模下兼具强大性能与跨域泛化能力的新范式，可作为医学时间序列分类的有力基座模型。

Abstract: Designing models that generalize effectively in low- to medium-data regimes remains a primary challenge in medical machine learning, particularly for physiological time-series classification. While tabular foundation models such as TabPFN offer an attractive alternative to conventional fine-tuning through in-context learning, they are not designed to capture the temporal dependencies inherent to physiological signals. ~In this paper, we introduce TSPFN, a foundation model that redesigns TabPFN's architecture for time series data. TSPFN integrates structured temporal representations and positional embeddings to capture intra-sample temporal and channel dependencies. To fully leverage its spatio-temporal design, the model is pretrained on 140,000 real-world physiological time series across multiple medical domains. This yields a unified, generalizable framework capable of learning the specificities of medical time series. Experiments across diverse physiological benchmarks demonstrate that TSPFN consistently outperforms standard tabular baselines and TabPFN, and achieves superior cross-domain generalization compared to specialized deep time-series models. All our experiments, ablation studies, and pre-processing scheme are publicly available at https://github.com/Jeremstym/TSPFN

</details>


### [385] [Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31036)
*Jiale Kang,Ziyin Yue,Zheng Zhan,Yangyi Huang,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NoRA通过在下投影矩阵上添加归一化来正则化LoRA的训练动力学，能够在不增加额外可训练参数或推理开销的情况下，在预训练、监督微调和强化学习中加速收敛、提升性能与稳定性，并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA被广泛用于参数高效模型适配，但其训练动力学的正则化方法尚未被充分探索。由于LoRA将上投影初始化为零，其早期优化动力学主要由下投影决定，因此作者希望利用这一特性设计简单有效的正则化方案来提升LoRA的训练稳定性与效果。

Method: 提出NoRA（Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation），即在训练过程中对下投影矩阵进行归一化；进一步证明仅在初始化时施加同样的归一化即可改进标准LoRA，无需在训练中反复归一化。

Result: 在预训练、监督微调和强化学习等场景中，NoRA一致地加速收敛、提升性能和训练稳定性，并缓解灾难性遗忘，且无需额外可训练参数或推理时计算。

Conclusion: NoRA是一种简单、广泛适用的LoRA增强方法，在保持参数效率和推理成本不变的前提下，能够显著改善LoRA的训练动力学与下游性能。

Abstract: While low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for parameter-efficient model adaptation, how to regularize its training dynamics for stable and effective optimization remains underexplored. Because LoRA initializes the up-projection to zero, its early optimization dynamics are largely governed by the down-projection. Building on this observation, we introduce Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation (NoRA), a simple yet effective method that normalizes the down-projection matrices during training. We further show that the same normalization can be applied only at initialization, improving standard LoRA without requiring repeated normalization throughout training. Across pretraining, supervised finetuning, and reinforcement learning, NoRA consistently accelerates convergence, improves performance and training stability, and mitigates catastrophic forgetting. These benefits require neither additional trainable parameters nor inference-time computation, making NoRA a simple and broadly applicable enhancement to LoRA.

</details>


### [386] [Rotational Equivariance in Machine Learning: A Comprehensive Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31045)
*Peter Lippmann,Fred A. Hamprecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于三维数据机器学习中旋转等变性（rotational equivariance）的综合性教程，从物理和几何直觉出发，系统介绍了群论、表示论、球谐函数、Wigner矩阵、张量积与Clebsch-Gordan分解等核心数学工具，并综述了群卷积、内部张量表示和基于规范化（canonicalization）的三大类等变网络实现策略及其权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D数据处理中（物理学、材料科学、3D计算机视觉等领域），预测结果不应依赖于输入坐标系的任意选择，旋转等变性是实现这一要求的数学保障。然而该领域涉及大量复杂数学概念且不同文献使用不同形式语言表达，入门门槛高，缺乏一份统一且面向实践者的系统教程。

Method: 以教程形式组织内容：首先从坐标独立的物理和几何直觉出发，逐步建立几何深度学习、群论和表示论所需的数学基础；随后系统介绍欧几里得图上的消息传递、群作用与表示、球谐函数、Wigner矩阵、张量积及Clebsch-Gordan分解等关键组件，说明它们如何组合成现代等变架构；最后综述三类主要实现策略——群卷积、内部张量表示和基于规范化的方法，并比较各自优缺点。

Result: 本文总结了三类将旋转等变性融入深度学习的主要策略（群卷积、内部张量表示、规范化方法），阐明了它们各自的实用优势与局限，并通过统一不同文献中的形式语言，为实践者在不同方法之间进行选择提供了清晰的权衡讨论。

Conclusion: 该教程通过将底层数学与实际模型设计相连接，统一了分散在不同形式语言中的相关思想，有效降低了旋转等变性领域的学习门槛，为研究人员和工程实践者提供了系统参考。

Abstract: Rotational symmetry is one of the most important structural principles in machine learning on 3D data. In applications ranging from physics and materials science to 3D computer vision, predictions should not depend on an arbitrary choice of coordinate frame. Rotational equivariance captures this requirement mathematically by enforcing that a rotation of the input induces a corresponding transformation of the model output. This tutorial provides a comprehensive introduction to rotational equivariance, starting from the physical and geometric intuition behind coordinate independence and building up the necessary machinery from geometric deep learning, group theory, and representation theory. We introduce message passing on Euclidean graphs, group actions and representations, spherical harmonics, Wigner matrices, tensor products, and Clebsch-Gordan decomposition, and explain how these ingredients give rise to modern equivariant architectures. We then survey the principal strategies for incorporating rotational equivariance in deep learning, including group convolutions, internal tensorial representations, and canonicalization-based methods, and discuss their practical strengths and limitations. The tutorial aims to lower the barrier to the subject by connecting the underlying mathematics to practical model design, by unifying ideas that are often expressed in different formal languages, and by helping practitioners choose among competing approaches through a clear discussion of their trade-offs.

</details>


### [387] [Does On-Policy Distillation Really Distill? From Noisy Teacher to Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31046)
*Yi Ding,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过定量分析发现在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中教师监督存在大量噪声，且学生策略对噪声不敏感；进一步发现OPD的增益主要来自抑制低对数概率token，而非真正的蒸馏。基于此提出无监督方法OPSA，使用熵自适应负优势信号，在多个基准上显著超越OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）提供密集的token级监督，作为RLVR稀疏outcome级优势的替代方案。然而教师评分的轨迹对学生而言是off-policy的，监督的可靠性以及学生改进的来源尚不清楚。作者旨在揭示OPD真正起作用的内在机制。

Method: 1) 定量分析OPD训练中教师监督的噪声水平及其与教师规模的关联；2) 通过消融实验（保留/移除噪声监督）检验学生策略对噪声的敏感度；3) 分析学习集中在低log-probability token上的现象，验证固定负优势是否等价于教师提供的优势；4) 提出OPSA（On-Policy Self-Adaptation），使用熵自适应负优势，对高熵位置施加更强学习信号以抑制尾部token，并将概率质量均匀重分配到头部token。

Result: 教师监督噪声随教师规模增大而增多，但学生策略对此不敏感。OPD的增益主要源于抑制低log-probability token（固定负优势可匹敌教师优势）。OPSA在AIME24上Avg@32提升35.41点（相对增益263%），Pass@32在三个基准上均翻倍以上，且相比OPD在AIME24上Avg@32高出16.77点。

Conclusion: OPD的有效性并非来自真正的蒸馏，而主要源于对低log-probability token的抑制。OPSA作为无监督的替代方法，通过熵自适应负优势实现了更优的性能，且跨模型族和任务具有广泛的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense token-level supervision as an alternative to the sparse outcome-level advantages of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, the teacher scores student-generated trajectories that are inherently off-policy for it, so the reliability of its supervision, and hence the source of the student's improvement, remains unclear. We quantitatively analyze teacher supervision during OPD training and find substantial noise whose prevalence increases with teacher scale. Surprisingly, the student policy is insensitive to such noise, converging to comparable performance regardless of whether noisy supervision is retained or removed. Does OPD distill at all? By analyzing what drives its gains, we find that learning concentrates on low log-probability tokens, and using a single fixed negative advantage matches the performance of teacher-provided ones. This suggests that OPD works largely by suppressing low log-probability tokens, which requires no teacher. These findings motivate On-Policy Self-Adaptation (OPSA), a supervision-free method using entropy-adaptive negative advantages. It assigns stronger learning signals to high-entropy positions, suppressing tail tokens, and evenly redistributing probability mass among head tokens. Compared with the base \texttt{Qwen3-1.7B}, OPSA improves Avg@32 by 35.41 points on AIME24, corresponding to a 263\% relative gain, and more than doubles Pass@32 across all three benchmarks. It also outperforms OPD by 16.77 points in Avg@32 on AIME24. Extensive experiments and analyses across model families and tasks further demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [388] [Universal Transformers for Circuit Computations: Perfect Length Generalization in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31067)
*Takuya Ito,Ruchir Puri,Murray Campbell,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可证明正确的Transformer参数化方法（仅需280个可学习参数），通过将算法任务建模为电路、引入深度追踪的位置编码和线性注意力机制，实现了对任意深度/长度的布尔代数等任务的完美泛化，并可在多轮迭代中逐步约简电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在学习通用算法计算时面临困难，特别是在组合泛化和长度泛化基准上持续失败。作者旨在解决这一问题，通过设计一种理论上可证明正确且参数极少的Transformer架构，实现精确的长度外推。

Method: 1) 将算法任务概念化为嵌入Transformer中的电路模型，实现单次前馈中深度为1的电路约简；2) 引入一种位置编码来追踪每个逻辑门在电路中的深度，使模型能通过掩码硬注意力在每次迭代中识别可求值的子表达式；3) 使用线性注意力实现每迭代O(n)复杂度；4) 结合自主停机准则，模型在深度为d的问题上迭代d次后终止，总复杂度为O(n·d)。

Result: 在深度1和深度2的浅层实例上训练，即可恢复出可解释的参数（参数"卡入"正确位置），实现精确的长度泛化。该构造可证明地完美求值任意长度的布尔表达式（通用符号计算），并在模块化算术和ListOps等其他常见长度泛化基准上达到100%准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过合理的设计（深度感知位置编码、线性注意力、层次化电路约简），Transformer可以实现可证明的精确长度泛化和深度泛化，为神经网络的算法学习能力提供了理论保障和实用验证。

Abstract: Learning generalizable algorithmic computations remains a challenge for neural networks, as reflected in persistent failures on compositional and length generalization benchmarks. We present a provably correct, transformer parameterization (with only 280 learnable parameters for Boolean algebra tasks) capable of learning and evaluating problems of any depth or length. We assume inputs are fully parenthesized, well-formed expressions. Our approach conceptualizes algorithmic tasks as circuit models embedded in transformers, enabling depth-1 circuit reduction in a single forward pass. To achieve depth generalization, we introduce a positional encoding that tracks each gate's depth within the circuit, enabling the model to identify evaluable subexpressions at each iteration via masked hard attention, with $O(n)$ per-iteration complexity via linear attention. Combined with an autonomous halting criterion, the model terminates after $d$ iterations for problems of depth $d$, yielding $O(n \cdot d)$ total complexity. We show that training on shallow problem instances (depth 1 and depth 2) effectively recovers interpretable parameters that {\em snap} into place, resulting in exact length generalization. Though we establish that our construction provably evaluates Boolean expressions -- a universal symbolic computation -- of arbitrary length perfectly, in other experiments we also demonstrate that our transformer variant can learn and generalize perfectly (100% accuracy) on other common length generalization benchmarks, including modular arithmetic and ListOps.

</details>


### [389] [A Model with No Head and Many Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31069)
*Nikita Koriagin,Yaroslav Aksenov,George Bredis,Gleb Gerasimov,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Soft Latent Thinking方法，在推理阶段用轻量级投影器替代大语言模型的词表头，使推理过程在嵌入空间中以连续方式自动回归展开，无需离散token生成，在多个模型上提升了pass@k指标并降低了每步计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在每一步推理时都需要通过巨大的词表头进行投影解码，这一操作计算开销大，且迫使所有推理过程都以离散token形式表达，限制了推理的灵活性和效率。作者希望探索在连续嵌入空间中进行推理的可能性以降低计算成本并提升推理效果。

Method: 将LM头替换为轻量级的投影器，在推理过程中于嵌入空间进行自回归展开，使推理步骤保持连续而非被token化，从而避免了每一步对大词汇表的投影计算。

Result: 在DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B和LLaMA-3.2-3B上进行实验，Soft Latent Thinking在所有k值上一致提升了pass@k指标，同时降低了思维链过程的每步计算量。在所有软思考方法中取得了最高的pass@32。

Conclusion: 有效推理可以在连续空间中完成而无需离散token生成，Soft Latent Thinking证明了一种更高效且效果更优的推理范式是可行的。

Abstract: Large language models decode by projecting hidden states through a large vocabulary head at every step. This operation is computationally costly and forces all reasoning to be expressed in discrete tokens. We introduce Soft Latent Thinking, a method that replaces the LM head during reasoning with a lightweight projector, enabling autoregressive rollout in embedding space where reasoning steps remain continuous rather than tokenized. Experiments on DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B and LLaMA-3.2-3B show that Soft Latent Thinking consistently improves pass@k across all k while reducing per-step compute during chain-of-thought. Our method achieves the highest pass@32 among all soft-thinking approaches, demonstrating that effective reasoning can be carried out in continuous space without discrete token generation.

</details>


### [390] [Sycophantic Agreement Transfers with Neutral Data via Contrastive Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31079)
*Camila Blank,Zhuofan Ying,Christopher Potts,Peter Hase,Jing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个重要发现：语言模型中的谄媚性认同（sycophantic agreement）行为不是通过显式的训练样本引入的，而是偏好优化目标（如DPO及其他6种目标函数）与偏好数据生成教师模型之间意外相互作用的结果。教师模型谄媚率与最终学生模型谄媚率之间存在强相关，且该信号扩散在整个数据集中而非集中在稀疏样本中，这使得基于数据筛选的缓解方法失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管谄媚性认同是语言模型对齐中广为人知的失败模式，但人们对它是如何从模型训练中涌现的机制理解极为有限。理解这一问题的成因对于改进模型对齐、避免有害行为向学生模型传递至关重要。

Method: 使用OLMo 3后训练管道，系统性地测试了三组教师模型家族的配对组合，测量教师模型谄媚性认同率的对数比值与学生模型谄媚率之间的相关性。在7种偏好优化目标（包括DPO）上验证该传递效应。通过探针式数据归因（probe-based data attribution）和logit-线性选择等过滤方法分析偏好数据，检查谄媚信号在数据集中的分布特征。

Result: 教师模型谄媚率对数比值与学生模型谄媚率之间存在强相关性；该效应在DPO及另外6种偏好优化目标中普遍存在。偏好数据集中的谄媚信号是扩散式的而非集中在稀疏的样例中——每个单独样例看起来是中性的，不存在显式的谄媚性认同实例，且过滤方法必须移除数据集的很大一部分才能缓解谄媚现象。

Conclusion: 偏好数据的生成教师模型与对齐训练目标之间存在意想不到的相互作用，这种相互作用会将谄媚性认同等不良且潜在有害的行为泛化并传递给最终的学生模型。这一发现提醒我们在偏好数据生成和模型对齐过程中需要更加审慎地考虑教师模型自身的行为特征。

Abstract: Sycophantic agreement refers to a behavior in which language models excessively affirm the user, often at the cost of factual accuracy. Although sycophantic agreement is a well-known failure of model alignment, there is limited understanding of how it emerges from model training. In this work, we demonstrate that sycophantic agreement can emerge as an unintended consequence of widely used contrastive preference optimization objectives. Using the OLMo 3 post-training pipeline, we show that, for various pairs of teacher models across three families, there is a strong correlation between the log-ratio of the teacher model sycophantic agreement rates and the resulting student model sycophantic agreement rate. We further demonstrate that this unintended transfer is not limited to DPO but also occurs across 6 other preference optimization objectives. To understand whether this effect can be attributed to particular training examples, we analyze the preference data and find that the sycophancy signal is diffused across the entire dataset rather than concentrated in a sparse set of examples: each example appears neutral, i.e., there are no explicit instances of sycophantic agreement, and filtering based on probe-based data attribution or logit-linear selection fails to mitigate sycophancy without removing a large portion of the dataset. Overall, our findings suggest that the teacher models used to generate preference data can interact with alignment training objectives in unexpected ways, generalizing to undesirable and potentially harmful behaviors like sycophantic agreement.

</details>


### [391] [Stress-Testing Efficient Responsible-AI Evaluation: When Compute Savings Change Benchmark Conclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31108)
*Ahmed El Kady,Aravind Narayanan,Rehana Noorani,Yani Ioannou,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地对"高效评估"这一测量干预手段进行压力测试，探究其在负责任AI基准测试中是否会影响结论的稳定性。作者在BBQ和BBQ-V基准上，对三种稠密和混合专家模型，在七种条件下（批处理、量化、基准缩减及其组合）评估其准确性、偏差严重度、推理质量、子群行为、子集稳定性、运行时间和GPU能耗，发现不同高效化手段对结论稳定性的影响差异显著，呼吁将高效评估视为需要验证其有效性的测量干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效评估手段已广泛用于支持模型行为声明，但很少被检验：当评估本身被加速或降成本后，原本基于该评估得出的结论（如偏差、质量等）是否仍然稳定成立。作者质疑仅凭聚合准确率的保持来判断高效评估有效性，认为需要跨多个结论维度进行系统性验证。

Method: 在BBQ和BBQ-V基准上，对三个稠密和混合专家模型，进行七种高效化条件的系统性对照实验（批处理、INT8/INT4量化、基准缩减及其组合），以完整基准的BF16结果作为基线，从准确率、偏差严重度与普遍性、推理质量、子群行为、子集成员稳定性、运行时和GPU能量七个维度全面比较结论的一致性。

Result: 较大的批处理能将准确率保持在基线0.35个百分点以内，子群变化较小，且在六个模型-数据集组合中的五个降低了能耗。INT8大体保持质量但能耗为基线的1.79-4.26倍。INT4导致较大且依赖模型与上下文的改变。基准缩减提供最一致的节省，但极小子集对保留哪些样本高度敏感。

Conclusion: 高效评估应被视为一种测量干预，其有效性必须根据基准预期支持的所有结论维度进行验证，而非仅依赖聚合准确率的保持。不同高效化策略的结论稳定性差异显著，研究者应根据所需结论选择合适的高效化手段。

Abstract: Efficient evaluation changes the protocol used to support claims about model behavior, yet it is rarely tested whether those claims remain stable after the evaluation itself is made cheaper. We stress-test conclusion robustness in responsible-AI benchmarking by evaluating three dense and mixture-of-experts models on BBQ and BBQ-V under seven conditions spanning batching, quantization, benchmark reduction, and their combinations. Rather than treating preserved aggregate accuracy as sufficient, we compare accuracy, bias severity and prevalence, reasoning quality, subgroup behavior, subset-membership stability, runtime, and measured GPU energy against a full-benchmark BF16 baseline. Larger batching keeps accuracy within 0.35 percentage points of baseline and produces comparatively small subgroup changes, while reducing energy in five of six model--dataset settings. INT8 largely preserves quality but uses 1.79--4.26$\times$ baseline energy. INT4 causes larger, model- and context-dependent changes. Reduced benchmarks provide the most consistent savings, but very small subsets are substantially more sensitive to which items are retained. Efficient evaluation should therefore be treated as a measurement intervention whose validity must be checked across the conclusions the benchmark is intended to support. Our project website is https://vectorinstitute.github.io/sustainable-rai-evaluation/ and the code is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/sustainable-rai-evaluation.

</details>


### [392] [On the Complexity of the Compatibility Problem for Succinctly Encoded Conditional Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31120)
*Guy Emerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究条件概率分布的兼容性问题（compatibility problem）在机器学习中的复杂度。当条件分布以算术电路（arithmetic circuits）这种简洁编码表示时，兼容性问题是不易处理的：在非零概率情形下为co-NP完全，在允许零概率情形下为PSPACE完全。文章进一步证明，除非多项式层级坍缩，否则存在兼容的简洁条件分布，其联合分布无法简洁表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中，模型常以条件概率p(x|y)和p(y|x)的形式进行预测，但一对条件分布未必与任何联合分布p(x,y)兼容。理解这种不兼容性及其计算代价，对于实际的高维概率建模（包括神经网络模型）具有重要实践意义。经典的兼容性检测方法假设条件分布以概率表编码，是计算上可行的，但在高维场景中需要更简洁的表示方式，因此需要研究简洁表示下的复杂度。

Method: 本文将兼容性问题形式化为一类以算术电路表示条件分布的新问题。通过对算术电路表示的条件分布进行复杂度归约，分别分析了非零概率情形（co-NP完全）和允许零概率情形（PSPACE完全）下的多个兼容性定义及其复杂度。同时通过构造性反例区分了不同兼容性概念，并利用多项式层级不坍缩的假设论证了存在兼容但联合分布无法简洁表示的实例。

Result: 在简洁电路表示下，兼容性问题整体上是不易处理的：非零概率情形为co-NP完全；允许零概率时，多种兼容性版本均为PSPACE完全。此外，在多项式层级不坍缩的假设下，存在兼容的简洁条件分布，其联合分布无法以多项式大小的电路简洁表达。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了在简洁表示下条件分布的兼容性检测在计算上本质困难，这为概率建模和机器学习实践提供了重要的理论边界：大规模场景中验证模型条件分布的一致性可能面临无法有效求解的困境，同时也暗示了简洁条件分布与简洁联合分布之间可能存在表达能力的鸿沟。

Abstract: The motivation for this paper is the investigation of the trade-offs implicit in probabilistic models used in machine learning. Models are often used to make predictions in the form of conditional probabilities. However, a pair of conditional distributions p(x|y) and p(y|x) may not be compatible with any joint distribution p(x,y). Given two such conditionals, determining if there exists a compatible joint is known as the compatibility problem. For discrete random variables, when the conditionals are encoded as probability tables, the compatibility problem has a known solution, which is computationally tractable. In this paper, we formalise and study a succinct version of the problem, encoding conditional distributions as arithmetic circuits. This is applicable to practical applications of probabilistic modelling in high-dimensional settings, including neural network models. We show that, for succinct circuit representations of conditionals, the compatibility problem is intractable. In the case that all probabilities are non-zero, the problem is co-NP-complete. In the case that probabilities can be zero, we give examples to demonstrate that several notions of compatibility can be distinguished, and we prove that multiple versions of the problem are PSPACE-complete. Furthermore, we show that, assuming the polynomial hierarchy does not collapse, there exist compatible succinct conditionals whose joint cannot be expressed succinctly. Implications of these results for probabilistic modelling and machine learning are discussed.

</details>


### [393] [Constant Individual Regret in General Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31166)
*Mingyang Liu,Gabriele Farina,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECHO-OFTRL算法，在完全信息反馈下，为任意有限N人正规型博弈提供与时间视野T无关的no-regret保证（每玩家遗憾为O(poly(N, log m_max))），彻底消除了之前算法中对视野的多对数依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无耦合no-regret动力学算法虽然能保证个体遗憾，但其上界包含对时间视野的多对数（polylogarithmic）依赖项。本文的目标是移除这一依赖，为任意有限N人正规型博弈在完全信息反馈下提供与视野T完全无关的遗憾保证。

Method: 提出ECHO-OFTRL算法，由两部分构成：(1) 基于乐观FTRL（OFTRL）框架；(2) 引入EMA级联（exponential moving average cascade）实现高阶乐观（optimism），即"ECHO"。该算法是确定性的且完全无耦合（fully uncoupled），并借鉴了现代滤波器设计中启发的新形式乐观机制。

Result: 对任意有限N人正规型博弈以及任意视野T≥1，算法同时保证每个玩家的遗憾上界为O(poly(N, log m_max))，其中m_max为最大的动作集大小。这是首次在该设定下实现遗憾与T完全无关（即T无关）的保证。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅从理论上突破了以往no-regret动力学中遗憾对视野的依赖瓶颈，还将滤波器设计思想引入优化算法，为无耦合动力学在均衡收敛方面的最优保证提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Uncoupled no-regret dynamics provide a decentralized route to equilibrium, but prior guarantees for individual regret retain a polylogarithmic dependence on the horizon. We remove this dependence for every finite $N$-player normal-form game under full-information feedback. We introduce \emph{ECHO-OFTRL}: optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OFTRL) equipped with an EMA cascade for high-order optimism (ECHO), where EMA denotes exponential moving average. The algorithm is deterministic and fully uncoupled. If $m_{\max}$ denotes the largest action-set size, then, simultaneously for every horizon $T\geq1$, it guarantees that each of the $N$ players in the game incurs regret upper bounded by $O(\textrm{poly}(N, \log m_{\max}))$. Our algorithm leverages a new form of optimism inspired by modern filter design.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [394] [Fluxes through a uniform double-diffusive staircase at low Prandtl number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28679)
*Arstanbek Tulekeyev,Pascale Garaud*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文通过三周期域直接数值模拟，系统研究了扩散型双扩散对流阶梯结构（双层状态）在低Prandtl数（Pr）和低扩散比（τ）下的性质。研究发现这些阶梯结构能在比以前报道更大的密度比下存在，并提出了一个新的长期存在判据。同时发现组成和温度的Nusselt数在此极限下按Ra^{1/3}标度，而非之前发现的(RaPr)^{1/3}标度，并提出基于密度异常上升和湍流混合的新模型来解释这些结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双扩散对流在扩散机制下常呈现长期存在的层状阶梯结构，这在北极海洋、火山湖及巨型行星内部均被观测到，但对其在低Prandtl数和低扩散比条件下的存在条件和输运性质了解有限。

Method: 在三周期域中进行直接数值模拟（DNS），系统扫描Prandtl数Pr、扩散比τ、密度比R_ρ和Rayleigh数Ra参数空间，测量密度、温度和组成的总通量以及通量比γ，并基于密度异常上升和湍流混合提出新的理论模型。

Result: 证明了双扩散阶梯可在比以往报道显著更大的密度比下存在，并提出了依赖Ra较弱的新存在判据；确认通量比γ在中等至大密度比下近似恒定但弱依赖Ra；揭示了组成和温度Nusselt数按Ra^{1/3}标度，与低R_ρ下的(RaPr)^{1/3}标度不同；新模型能合理准确解释现有的低Pr数据。

Conclusion: 双扩散对流阶梯结构在低Pr和低τ条件下比此前认为的更广泛存在，其输运标度律和存在判据需要新的理论框架来描述，所提出的模型为理解这类系统中层状结构的形成与维持提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Double-diffusive convection in the diffusive regime is often observed to be in a long-lived layered state, well-known in the Arctic ocean and in volcanic lakes, and thought to exist in the interiors of giant planets. The density profile resembles a `staircase', with stacks of convective layers of uniform density separated by strongly stratified interfaces. The governing parameters are the Prandtl number $\mathrm{Pr}$, diffusivity ratio $τ$, density ratio $R_ρ$, and Rayleigh number $\mathrm{Ra}$. In this work, we perform direct numerical simulations in triply periodic domains to systematically investigate the properties of statistically stationary double-diffusive staircases with $τ\le \mathrm{Pr} \ll 1$. We demonstrate that they can exist for significantly larger density ratios than previously reported, and propose a new criterion for the existence of long-lived layered states. Notably, this criterion depends weakly on $\mathrm{Ra}$. In all simulations, we measure the total fluxes of density $F^{\mathrm{tot}}_ρ$, temperature $F^{\mathrm{tot}}_T$ and composition $F^{\mathrm{tot}}_C$, and the flux ratio $γ=F^{\mathrm{tot}}_C/F^{\mathrm{tot}}_T$. We confirm that $γ$ is approximately constant for moderate to large $R_ρ$ but also depends weakly on $\mathrm{Ra}$. We show that the Nusselt numbers for composition and temperature scale as $\mathrm{Ra}^{1/3}$ in this limit, which notably differs from the $(\mathrm{RaPr})^{1/3}$ scaling previously found at lower $R_ρ$. We propose a new model for these findings that is based on the rise and turbulent mixing of density anomalies that form at the edges of the interfaces. The model explains the existing low $\mathrm{Pr}$ data with reasonable accuracy.

</details>


### [395] [Internal heating in rapidly rotating convection is not a shortcut to geostrophic turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28756)
*Justin A. Nicoski,Tobias G. Oliver,Stefano Maffei,Michael A. Calkins*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过渐近约简的准地转模型测试了内加热能否在中等强迫下快速进入扩散无关的地转湍流（GT）标度律。结果表明不能：Nusselt和Reynolds数并未比边界加热情形更接近扩散无关预测，在Pr=7时反而更远；预测的Pr^{-1/2}塌缩失效，prefactor依赖加热形式。虽然无滑移/无应力情形与HadJeRci等人的辐射驱动模拟逐例一致，但相同约化Rayleigh数和Prandtl数、不同Ekman数的扫描之间存在25%的Nu差异。数据仅覆盖补偿Nusselt数平稳的狭窄强迫范围，更宽范围内它先升后降。作者认为体积输运标度不是GT的完整诊断指标，而内部平均温度梯度饱和与垂直速度峰度仍是可靠指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在行星和恒星中，对流湍流常由内部加热驱动。此前有研究提出内部加热可作为在中等强迫下进入地转湍流（GT）扩散无关标度律的捷径。作者希望检验这一假说是否成立。

Method: 采用渐近约简的准地转（quasi-geostrophic）模型，该模型在Ekman数趋于零的极限下形式有效，并将Prandtl数保留为独立参数。通过系统扫描强迫参数，对比不同加热形式、不同Ekman数和Prandtl数下的输运标度与扩散无关预测。

Result: 内部加热并非进入GT标度的捷径：Nusselt和Reynolds数并未更接近扩散无关预测，Pr=7时反而更远；预测的Pr^{-1/2}塌缩失败；prefactor依赖加热形式。无滑移/无应力情形与HadjeRci等人（2024）的辐射驱动模拟逐例一致，但相同约化Rayleigh和Prandtl数、不同Ekman数的扫描间存在25%的Nu差异。数据仅覆盖补偿Nusselt数平稳的窄强迫范围，更宽范围内其先升至最大而后下降。

Conclusion: 体积输运标度（Nu和Re）不足以作为GT的唯一或完整诊断指标，而内部平均温度梯度的饱和与垂直速度峰度仍是GT的可靠指示器。

Abstract: Convective turbulence in planets and stars is often driven by internal heating. This forcing mechanism has also been proposed as a means of accessing the diffusion-free scalings of the geostrophic turbulence (GT) regime at modest forcing. We test this with the asymptotically reduced quasi-geostrophic model, which is formally valid in the limit of vanishing Ekman number, $Ek \rightarrow 0$, and retains the Prandtl number as an independent parameter. We find no such shortcut: the Nusselt and Reynolds numbers are no closer to their diffusion-free predictions than in the boundary-heated case, and at $Pr = 7$ they are further from them; the predicted $Pr^{-1/2}$ collapse fails; and the prefactor depends on the form of the heating. Our no-slip/stress-free cases track the radiatively driven simulations of Hadjerci et al. (2024) case by case, yet a $25\%$ variation in $Nu$ persists between sweeps that share the same reduced Rayleigh and Prandtl numbers but differ in Ekman number. Those data span only the narrow range of forcing over which the compensated Nusselt number is stationary; across the wider range accessible to the reduced model it rises to a maximum and then falls. We argue that bulk transport scalings are incomplete diagnostics of GT, whereas the saturation of the interior mean temperature gradient and of the vertical velocity kurtosis remain reliable indicators.

</details>


### [396] [Droplet sizes from impulsive capillary jets: an exponential distribution with no lower cut-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28761)
*Alfonso M. Ganan-Calvo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文推翻了一个经典观点：溃灭空泡抛出的韧带断裂产生的液滴尺寸并不存在以粘毛细长度ℓ_μ=μ²/ρσ为固定倍数下界的规律。核心论据是：脉冲射流自身不拥有任何固有长度。控制各次缩颈（pinch）的自相似解不含长度标度，拉伸-迭代卫星液滴的级联也不引入长度，ℓ_μ属于奇异性而非液滴。尺寸分布只能从一个地方继承尺度——产生射流的空泡。气泡破裂计算（受限与非受限）证实了该观点：所见的下界是网格强加的而非流体性质，当网格在ℓ_μ以下探查时能找到液滴。唯一发射液滴的普查呈指数分布，其唯一尺度由空泡半径决定，既与事件强度无关也和周围液体多少无关。指数分布是给定期望均值条件下结构最少的一种普查，因此碎片化只保留空泡尺寸而不保留其他母体记忆。持续拉伸则会保留这种记忆，印刻母体绳线的褶皱，产生韧带介导碎片化中报道的尖峰、类伽马分布。因此是加载方式而非流体本身，决定喷雾的形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论认为空泡溃灭韧带断裂产生的液滴尺寸存在以粘毛细长度ℓ_μ为固定倍数的下界，本文试图证明这一下界并非流体性质，而是数值网格的人为产物，从而纠正对空泡诱导碎片化尺寸标度规律的长期误解。

Method: 通过理论分析与数值计算相结合。理论上揭示脉冲射流缺乏固有长度标度，指出控制缩颈的自相似解、拉伸与迭代卫星级联均不引入长度；数值上对受限与非受限气泡破裂场景进行计算，通过改变网格分辨率探查ℓ_μ以下的液滴分布，并统计发射液滴的数量分布（指数分布）及其唯一尺度（空泡半径）。

Result: 数值计算表明：空泡破裂发射液滴的尺寸下界由网格分辨率决定，而非ℓ_μ；在低于ℓ_μ的尺度范围内网格可发现液滴。唯一发射液滴的普查呈指数分布，唯一尺度由空泡半径设定，且不依赖于事件剧烈程度与外围液体体积。对比之下，韧带介导的碎片化（持续拉伸）产生峰状、类伽马的分布，保留母体绳线褶皱记忆。

Conclusion: 空泡破裂产生的液滴尺寸不存在ℓ_μ量级的固有下界，其粒度分布呈指数型并仅记忆空泡尺寸；而持续拉伸的韧带碎片化则产生类伽马分布并保留母体结构记忆。喷雾形态由加载方式（而非仅流体性质）决定。

Abstract: A collapsing cavity throws up a ligament, which fragments into a train of droplets whose sizes are commonly given a lower bound at a fixed multiple of the viscocapillary length $\ell_μ=μ^2/ρσ$. We show that no such bound exists, for a reason more general than the fate of one parameter: an impulsive jet possesses no length of its own. The similarity solutions that govern each pinch contain none, the cascade of stretching and iterated satellites introduces none, and $\ell_μ$ belongs to the singularity rather than to the droplets. A size distribution built on such a process can inherit a scale from one place only, the cavity that launched the jet. Computations of bubble bursting, confined and unconfined, bear this out. The floor they display is the one the mesh imposes, not a property of the fluid, and wherever the mesh looks below $\ell_μ$ it finds droplets. The census of unique emitted droplets is exponential, with a single scale set by the cavity radius, indifferent both to how vigorous the event is and to how much liquid surrounds it. An exponential is the least structured census compatible with a prescribed mean, so the fragmentation keeps the size of the cavity and no other memory of its parent. Sustained stretching does keep such a memory, imprinting the corrugation of the parent thread and yielding the peaked, gamma-like distributions reported for ligament-mediated fragmentation. The manner of loading, and not the fluid alone, selects the shape of a spray.

</details>


### [397] [A priori Assessment of Tensor-Network Encoding for Isotropic Turbulent Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28869)
*Massen Esmaeili,Hirad Alipanah,Robert Pinkston,Peyman Givi,Daniel Livescu,Andrew J. Daley,Dieter Jaksch,Juan José Mendoza-Arenas*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用矩阵乘积态（MPS）作为张量网络降阶建模框架，对不可压缩三维湍流及守恒标量场进行截断表示与重建。通过奇异值分解并丢弃小奇异值的方式压缩数据，采用交错的空间张量索引排序以局部化主导关联。速度场仅用5%内存即可达99.8%保真度，标量场需15%内存。能量恢复误差在0.2%以内，耗散均值误差约10%，验证了MPS在复杂湍流数据降阶分析中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维湍流场数据的存储与计算成本极高，需要一种系统化的降阶建模框架来近似高维场，在保持关键统计量的同时大幅压缩数据，从而支持可扩展的湍流数据分析。张量网络方法源于量子多体系统模拟，具有天然的层次化压缩能力，为湍流降阶提供了新途径。

Method: 采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）作为张量网络降阶建模框架。具体流程包括：(1) 将湍流场编码为MPS，通过序列化奇异值分解并丢弃小奇异值实现截断；(2) 在分解前对输运变量的空间张量索引采用交错排序以局部化主导的跨张量相关性；(3) 将截断表示收缩回完整网格并与DNS数据对比；(4) 系统检验从低阶到高阶的统计量，包括速度梯度、耗散和结构函数。

Result: 速度场重建在仅使用5%的原始DNS内存时达到99.8%的保真度；标量场达到同等保真度需要15%的内存。在压缩水平下，总动能和标量能量相对误差均小于0.2%，平均耗散和平均标量耗散与DNS值偏差约在10%以内。研究者系统检验了广泛的高阶统计量。

Conclusion: MPS作为张量网络方法在湍流数据的降阶分析中表现出色，能够在极低压缩比下高保真恢复关键统计量，支持可扩展的降阶分析，并激励进一步探索基于张量网络的方法在计算湍流中的应用。

Abstract: Tensor networks (TNs), originally developed for simulating many-body quantum systems, provide a systematic framework for approximating high-dimensional fields. This is achieved by factorizing the field into interconnected tensors with small bond dimensions, thereby restricting the correlations captured across field bipartitions. Belonging to the family of TNs, the matrix product state (MPS) ansatz is utilized here as a reduced-order modeling framework to construct truncated representations of isotropic turbulent flow data. Two direct numerical simulation (DNS) datasets are considered: the hydrodynamic field of an incompressible three-dimensional flow, and a conserved Fickian scalar in a similar flow. Each field is encoded as an MPS through a sequence of singular value decompositions (SVDs) in which small singular values are discarded. The truncated representation is contracted back to the full grid, and the resulting reconstructed field is compared against DNS. An interleaved ordering of the spatial tensor indices of the transport variables is applied prior to decomposition in order to localize the dominant inter-tensor correlations. Velocity reconstructions achieve $99.8\%$ fidelity using only $5\%$ of the original DNS memory, while the scalar field reaches the same fidelity at $15\%$ memory usage. A wide range of lower- and higher-order statistics, including velocity gradients, dissipation, and structure functions, are systematically examined. At these compression levels, the total kinetic energy and the scalar energy are both recovered within $0.2\%$ relative error, while the mean dissipation and mean scalar dissipation remain within approximately $10\%$ of the DNS generated values. These findings support the suitability of MPS for scalable reduced-order analysis of complex turbulent datasets and motivate further exploration of TN-based methods in computational turbulence.

</details>


### [398] [NeuralFlowNet: Towards Data-Free Physics-Informed Neural Network Solutions of Navier-Stokes Equations Across Low and High Reynolds Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28935)
*Jayanga T. Samarasinghe,Luis De la Fuente,Laura Alvarez*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: NeuralFlowNet是一个数据无关、基于物理信息的证明概念框架，通过将Navier-Stokes方程嵌入学习过程，能够在无需外部流场数据集的情况下解决从低到高雷诺数的稳态流体动力学问题，并保持与参考数值解的良好一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI流场模型大多依赖大规模仿真或实验数据集，且往往针对特定问题，泛化能力和物理可靠性有限。传统PINN框架在高雷诺数条件下表现不佳，限制了其在CFD中的应用。因此需要一种数据无关且能在高雷诺数下保持物理一致性的求解框架。

Method: 本文提出了NeuralFlowNet，一个数据无关的物理信息神经网络框架，直接通过学习Navier-Stokes方程和预设边界条件来求解稳态流场，无需外部仿真数据。该框架通过逐步增加的物理和几何复杂度的基准问题来验证其适用性。

Result: 实验结果表明，NeuralFlowNet能够在广泛的雷诺数范围内准确恢复稳态流场，包括具有强压力梯度的情形，且无需外部流场数据训练，同时与参考数值解保持了良好的一致性。

Conclusion: NeuralFlowNet被确立为一个可靠的数据无关物理信息框架，为未来非稳态及更复杂仿真的测试奠定基础，并为开发高雷诺数流体动力学中可信赖且高效的AI求解器提供了依据。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a compelling pathway toward trustworthy artificial-intelligence-based computational fluid dynamics (CFD) by embedding governing equations directly into the learning process. Many existing AI flow models require large simulation or experimental datasets and often remain problem-specific, limiting their generalization and physical reliability. Data-free PINN frameworks offer an alternative by learning flow solutions from the Navier-Stokes equations and prescribed boundary conditions, potentially reducing dependence on expensive CFD datasets while retaining physical consistency. However, traditional PINN frameworks have struggled at high Reynolds numbers, limiting their application in CFD. In this work, we present NeuralFlowNet, a data-free, physics-informed proof-of-concept framework designed to solve steady Navier-Stokes problems across low- to high-Reynolds-number conditions. We describe the proposed methodological framework and demonstrate its applicability using benchmark problems with increasing physical and geometric complexity. The results demonstrate that NeuralFlowNet can accurately recover steady flow fields across a broad Reynolds-number range, including cases with strong pressure gradients, without training on external flow-field data and while maintaining good agreement with reference numerical solutions. These findings establish NeuralFlowNet as a reliable framework for future testing of unsteady and more complex simulations and could provide a basis for developing trustworthy and efficient AI solvers for high-Reynolds-number fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [399] [Physics-Guided Machine Learning for Predictive Turbulent Combustion Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29033)
*Shubhangi Bansude,Jay Patel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文综述了物理引导机器学习（PGML）在湍流燃烧中湍流-化学相互作用（TCI）建模中的应用，系统阐述了PGML如何通过将先验物理知识嵌入训练数据、输入特征、模型架构、损失函数、混合闭合结构和求解器感知验证协议等环节，来克服纯数据驱动模型可能违反守恒律、热化学一致性不足、外推能力差及破坏CFD求解器稳定性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流燃烧的预测模拟因刚性化学动力学、湍流输运、分子混合和热释放的跨尺度非线性相互作用而极具挑战性。传统闭合模型（如混合限制模型、火焰面/流形方法、条件矩闭合、传输PDF/FDF）依赖结构性假设，在标定范围外可能失效。机器学习虽能逼近复杂非线性函数、放松闭合假设并加速计算，但纯数据驱动的模型可能违反守恒律和热化学一致性，且在训练域外外推能力差，容易导致嵌入的CFD求解器失稳。因此需要一种融合物理先验与机器学习优势的系统性框架。

Method: 文章提出并梳理了物理引导机器学习（PGML）框架，其主要方法包括五个层面：1）训练数据与输入特征层面：使用物理约束的数据（如满足热化学一致性的组分空间）和物理启发的特征编码；2）模型架构层面：设计嵌入物理结构（如守恒律、对称性）的神经网络结构；3）损失函数层面：在训练目标中加入物理约束项（如质量守恒、能量守恒、热化学一致性惩罚）；4）混合闭合结构层面：将机器学习模型与经典闭合模型（如火焰面、CMC、PDF/FDF）以混合或校正方式结合；5）求解器感知验证协议层面：在真实CFD求解器环境中验证模型稳定性与泛化性能，而非仅做离线精度评估。

Result: 文章系统梳理了PGML在TCI建模中的最新进展，展示了PGML能有效放松传统闭合模型的限制性假设，同时保持物理一致性，改善纯数据驱动模型在保守性、热化学一致性和求解器稳定性方面的不足。通过多层级的物理知识嵌入策略，PGML框架能够提高模型在标定域外的可靠性，并降低详细化学计算的计算成本。

Conclusion: PGML为湍流燃烧中的TCI建模提供了一条兼具数据驱动灵活性与物理可解释性、可泛化性的有效路径。未来工作应继续深化物理知识在各建模环节的嵌入方式，发展更严格的求解器感知验证标准，并扩展PGML在更复杂燃烧工况（如多相燃烧、高压燃烧、大范围当量比变化等）中的应用。

Abstract: Predictive simulation of turbulent combustion remains challenging because stiff chemical kinetics, turbulent transport, molecular mixing, and heat release interact nonlinearly across unresolved scales. In the filtered or averaged formulations, reaction rate is unclosed, and the integration of detailed chemistry constitutes the dominant computational cost. Conventional closures such as mixing-limited models, flamelet and manifold methods, conditional moment closure, and transported probability/filtered density function (PDF/FDF) formulations, encode substantial physical insight, but rely on structural assumptions that may lose validity outside their calibrated regimes. Machine learning offers a complementary approach because of its ability to approximate complex nonlinear functions. In turbulent combustion, this capability can be used to relax restrictive closure assumptions, learn unresolved nonlinear mappings from data, and accelerate expensive computations such as detailed chemistry computation. However, purely data-driven models may violate conservation laws and thermochemical consistency. They may also extrapolate poorly outside the training domain and destabilize the CFD solvers in which they are embedded. Physics-guided machine learning (PGML) addresses these failure modes by incorporating prior knowledge throughout the modeling pipeline: in the training data and input features, the model architecture, the loss function, the hybrid closure structure, and the solver-aware validation protocol. This article reviews PGML for turbulence-chemistry interaction (TCI) modeling within this framework.

</details>


### [400] [Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks for viscoelastic fluid equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29895)
*Suryanshu Singh,Midhuna Suresh,Akanksha Gupta*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（PI-KAN）框架，用于求解粘弹性流体方程的正问题。该框架基于广义水动力学模型，并在Taylor-Green流动及其修改版本上进行了基准测试，系统研究了网络架构、超参数、配点分布和随机种子对精度与收敛性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性流体方程具有流体场之间的强非线性耦合，传统MLP在物理信息神经网络中面临可解释性差和训练困难等问题。KAN通过边缘可学习激活函数提供了更可解释的替代方案，但将其应用于粘弹性流体方程的研究尚未充分探索。

Method: 该研究构建了基于Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理的物理信息网络框架（PI-KAN），采用广义水动力学模型描述粘弹性流体（源自尘埃等离子体领域）。在Taylor-Green流动及修改版TG流动基准问题上，系统评估了不同网络架构、超参数、配点分布以及粘弹性参数（τ_m=1-20）对求解精度和收敛行为的影响，并考察了随机种子初始化对训练结果的影响。

Result: 研究系统地揭示了PI-KAN在不同网络架构、超参数和配点分布下的精度与收敛特性，并分析了粘弹性参数范围（τ_m=1-20）及随机种子初始化对训练结果的影响规律，为PI-KAN在粘弹性流体方程求解中的实际设计提供了定量指导。

Conclusion: 该工作为PI-KAN在粘弹性流体方程中的应用提供了系统的设计指南和实现参考，证明了KAN网络作为物理信息求解器在该类强非线性耦合问题上的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), inspired by the Kolmogorov Arnold representation theorem, provide an interpretable alternative to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) by using learnable activation functions on edges rather than fixed node activations. We propose a Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (PI-KAN) framework for solving forward problem of viscoelastic fluid equations, which arise in many complex fluid dynamics applications and are characterized by strong nonlinear coupling between fluid fields. For viscoelastic fluid equations, we adopt the generalized hydrodynamic model, which is well established in the field of dusty plasma. To evaluate the performance of the proposed framework for viscoelastic fluid, we consider benchmark problem based on the Taylor-Green (TG) flow and a modified Taylor-Green flow. We systematically investigate the effects of different network architectures, hyperparameters, and collocation point distributions on the accuracy and convergence behavior of PI-KANs for the range of viscoelastic parameter ($τ_m = 1$--$20$). We also study the impact of random seed initialization on training outcomes. The obtained results provide useful guidance for the design and implementation of physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (PI-KANs) in solving viscoelastic fluid equations

</details>


### [401] [Spectral-Embedded Operator Learning for Three-Phase Interfacial Flow: A Ternary Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29069)
*Muhammad Abid,Arth Sojitra,Omer San*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个三相互相流动基准测试，比较三种仅改变trunk坐标表示的DeepONet变体（原始坐标、随机傅里叶特征、张量积切比雪夫字典），发现切比雪夫表示（SEDONet）相比FEDONet降低16.8%测试相对L2误差，相比DeepONet降低24.0%，在所有五个输出通道上均有改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算子学习代理模型主要在单一场、单一界面问题上进行基准测试，不清楚在这些场景下验证的架构选择是否适用于受约束的多相流动问题。需要检验trunk坐标表示在多通道、界面主导的目标上的表现。

Method: 构建了一个三相界面流动基准：气泡在水中上升、穿过水-油界面、将水羽流卷入油中的壁面受限域配置。使用结构保持的三元Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes求解器生成参考数据（代数保持单纯形约束），从1,024个Sobol采样的九维参数空间仿真中学习从物理参数到五通道时空场的映射。比较三种仅改变trunk表示的参数匹配DeepONet变体：原始坐标（DeepONet）、随机傅里叶特征（FEDONet）和固定张量积切比雪夫字典（SEDONet）。

Result: SEDONet相比FEDONet将测试相对L2误差降低16.8%，相比DeepONet降低24.0%，且所有五个输出通道均得到改进。空间和时间误差分析表明主要增益集中在扩散界面附近和气泡突破后的时间段。切比雪夫表示对强非周期的壁法向和该三相流的时间结构特别有效。

Conclusion: trunk坐标表示对多相界面流动的算子学习至关重要，固定张量积切比雪夫字典（SEDONet）在处理强非周期壁法向和时间结构的复杂三相流动中优于原始坐标和随机傅里叶特征表示。

Abstract: Operator-learning surrogates have been benchmarked largely on single-field, single-interface problems, leaving unclear whether architectural choices validated in those settings transfer to constrained, multiphase flows. We introduce a three-phase interfacial-flow benchmark to examine whether the trunk coordinate representation matters for a multi-channel, interface-dominated target. The configuration consists of an air bubble rising through water, piercing a water-oil interface, and entraining a water plume into the oil within a bounded, wall-confined domain. Reference data are generated using a structure-preserving ternary Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes solver that algebraically preserves the simplex constraint. From 1,024 Sobol-sampled simulations spanning a nine-dimensional parameter space, we learn the mapping from physical parameters to five-channel space-time fields. We compare three parameter-matched DeepONet variants differing only in trunk representation: raw coordinates (DeepONet), random Fourier features (FEDONet), and a fixed tensor-product Chebyshev dictionary (SEDONet). SEDONet reduces the test relative L2 error by 16.8% compared with FEDONet and by 24.0% compared with DeepONet, while improving all five output channels. Spatial and temporal error analyses localize the principal gains near the diffuse interfaces and after bubble breakthrough. The results indicate that the Chebyshev representation is particularly effective for the strongly non-periodic wall-normal and temporal structure of this three-phase flow.

</details>


### [402] [From localized dryout to convective elongated vapor structures: Reynolds number effects on boiling transition in a rectangular mini-channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29240)
*Qi Wang,Xin Wang,Mingze Wang,Yifei Guan,Kang Luo,Jian Wu,Wei Wang,Alberto T. Perez*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过三维共轭模拟研究矩形微通道中饱和流动沸腾，重点考察入口雷诺数对沸腾模式选择与转变的影响。发现两种Re依赖的转变路径（低Re下局部干涸、高Re下蒸汽结构对流拉伸重组），其中Re=2000热性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解微通道内饱和流动沸腾中入口雷诺数如何影响沸腾模式选择与转变机制，并厘清沸腾模式转变与全局传热性能退化之间的关联，为微通道换热器设计与优化提供理论指导。

Method: 采用基于C++的开源数值框架进行三维共轭模拟；耦合物理信息多位点成核模型（成核点密度关联式与Halton序列空间分配策略相结合）；系统改变入口雷诺数并进行参数化对比分析。

Result: 识别出两种Re依赖的沸腾转变路径：低Re下由上游活跃沸腾与下游渐进缺液导致的局部干涸主导转变；高Re下由蒸汽结构的对流拉伸与重组（拉长汽栓演变为局部汽膜直至近似全覆盖）主导转变。Re=2000时整体热响应最佳，并阐明了壁面干涸与体积蒸汽份额在传热退化中的各自作用。

Conclusion: 在所研究的矩形微通道构型与运行范围内，雷诺数与蒸汽组织、沸腾转变、壁面干涸及全局传热性能密切相关，为通过调控Re来优化微通道沸腾传热提供了明确依据。

Abstract: Three-dimensional conjugate simulations were conducted to investigate saturated flow boiling in a rectangular mini-channel, with particular emphasis on the role of inlet Reynolds number on boiling mode selection and transition. A C++ based open-source numerical framework was employed, incorporating a physically informed multi-site nucleation model by coupling a nucleation site density correlation with a Halton-sequence based spatial allocation strategy. Two distinct Re-dependent transition pathways were identified. At low Re, boiling transition is mainly associated with localized dryout development associated with upstream active boiling and progressive downstream liquid starvation. At high Re, the transition is characterized by convective stretching and reorganization of vapor structures, through which elongated vapor slugs evolve into localized vapor films and eventually approach full surface vapor coverage. The global heat transfer characteristics and peak heat transfer capacity are further interpreted in conjunction with boiling mode transition, clarifying the respective roles of wall dryout and volumetric vapor fraction in heat transfer deterioration. Among all cases, Re=2000 provides the most favorable overall thermal response. Overall, within the rectangular mini-channel configuration and operating range considered in this study, Re is closely associated with vapor organization, boiling transition, wall dryout, and global heat transfer performance.

</details>


### [403] [Solute dispersion in magnetically influenced multiphase flow through a porous tube: axial transport and microrotational effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29946)
*Sohel Ahmed,Nanda Poddar,Jyotirmoy Rana,Kajal Kumar Mondal,Niall Madden*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文对磁流体动力学多相管流中通过多孔层的广义溶质扩散进行了理论研究，建立了一个包含微极流体核心（红细胞旋转行为）与嵌有Brinkman和Darcy多孔结构的牛顿血浆外围的两流体解析模型，并引入横向磁场研究了磁致载流变化对溶质局部化的影响。基于Sankarasubramanian & Gill的广义扩散框架推导了解析解，并通过布朗动力学模拟独立验证。结果揭示了微旋转动力学对溶质浓度、对流系数和有效扩散的先前未探索的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物流体和环境流体动力学中，溶质在磁流体动力学多相管流（含多孔层）中的扩散行为尚未被充分理论化理解，特别是微极流体旋转动力学、磁场与多孔动脉结构之间的耦合作用存在研究空白。

Method: 采用两流体解析模型：上层为微极（非牛顿）流体核心模拟红细胞旋转行为，外周为嵌入Brinkman和Darcy多孔结构（对应糖萼和内皮层）的牛顿血浆；引入横向磁场；基于Sankarasubramanian & Gill的广义扩散框架推导解析解，并通过布朗动力学模拟独立验证。

Result: 解析预测与布朗动力学模拟在零阶和一阶输运矩的时间演化上表现出极佳的一致性；揭示了微旋转动力学对溶质浓度、对流系数和有效扩散的先前未探索影响，阐明了平动与旋转流体运动在生物流体输运中的耦合作用。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了文献中的重要空白，建立了将磁场、微极流体与多孔动脉结构联系起来的广义理论框架，为生物流体输运、靶向药物递送和临床工程应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This study presents a theoretical investigation of generalized solute dispersion in magnetohydrodynamic multiphase tube flow with porous layers. A two-fluid analytical model is developed for applications in biofluid and environmental fluid dynamics. The model comprises a micropolar (non-Newtonian) fluid core representing the rotational behaviour of red blood cells and a Newtonian plasma periphery embedded with Brinkman and Darcy porous structures, corresponding to the glycocalyx and endothelial layers with distinct permeability characteristics. A transverse magnetic field is incorporated to investigate how magnetic-field-induced modifications of the carrier flow influence solute localisation, with potential relevance to magnetic nanoparticle-mediated drug delivery. Using the generalised dispersion framework of Sankarasubramanian & Gill, analytical solutions are derived to investigate how the coupled axial velocity field and associated microrotational dynamics influence solute transport. The analytical predictions are independently validated through Brownian dynamics simulations, demonstrating excellent agreement for the temporal evolution of the zeroth and first transport moments. The results reveal the previously unexplored influence of microrotational dynamics on solute concentration, convection coefficients and effective dispersion, providing new insights into the coupled roles of translational and rotational fluid motion in biofluid transport. This work bridges an important gap in the literature and establishes a generalized theoretical framework linking magnetic fields, micropolar fluids and porous arterial structures for biofluid transport, targeted drug delivery and clinical engineering applications.

</details>


### [404] [Droplet impact of emulsions on inclined microstructured surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29342)
*Ulises Rosales-Gallegos,Laura Oropeza-Ramos,Francisco Manuel Sánchez-Arévalo,Miguel A. Quetzeri-Santiago*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高速成像和光学显微镜实验，系统研究了20%硅油-水乳液液滴在倾斜55°的平滑玻璃、不同间距微柱阵列及超疏水涂层上的冲击动力学，识别出五种冲击模态，揭示表面倾斜、微结构和流体组成对乳液冲击行为的耦合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴冲击通常很少在水平光滑表面上发生，而表面倾斜、微观结构和流体组成对乳液冲击行为的综合影响仍鲜有研究。乳液在倾斜纹理表面上的冲击特性对农业喷洒和涂层技术具有重要意义，现有认知不足以预测其行为。

Method: 实验采用20%（v/v）硅油-水乳液液滴，在倾斜55°的平滑玻璃和两种间距（50和100 μm）的微柱阵列上进行冲击实验，并额外使用超疏水涂层考察润湿性的作用。使用高速成像表征冲击动力学（Weber数范围18-565），采用光学显微镜定量残余润湿面积，通过对比纯水的行为揭示乳液的特殊性。

Result: 识别出五种冲击模态：沉积、滑动、部分回弹、完全回弹和飞溅。表面倾斜引起铺展和回缩的不对称性；微结构基底通过减小液固接触面积降低粘附并促进回弹。与纯水相比，乳液在光滑表面表现出增强的沉积，在未涂覆微结构上增加滑动，在高Weber数下出现更早的液丝形成和二次破碎。分散油相局部改变润湿和润滑，产生液桥、不均匀回缩及更大的残余润湿面积。微结构表面的毛细约束限制了投影润湿面积，尽管微结构内部液体滞留增加。

Conclusion: 乳液冲击行为无法仅由传统无量纲数预测，局部相重新分布从根本上改变能量耗散、润湿和破碎过程，该发现为农业喷洒和涂层技术中倾斜纹理表面的喷雾沉积提供新见解。

Abstract: Droplet impacts rarely occur on horizontal smooth surfaces, yet the combined influence of surface inclination, microstructure, and fluid composition remains poorly understood for emulsions. Here, we experimentally investigate the impact of 20% (v/v) silicone oil-in-water emulsion droplets on smooth glass and micropillar arrays with two interpillar spacings (50 and 100 $μ$m) inclined at 55$^\circ$. The role of surface wettability is further examined using a superhydrophobic coating. High-speed imaging is employed to characterise the impact dynamics over a wide range of Weber numbers ($18 \leq We \leq 565$), while optical microscopy is used to quantify the residual wetted area after impact. Five impact regimes are identified: deposition, sliding, partial rebound, complete rebound, and splashing. Surface inclination introduces asymmetry in spreading and recoil, whereas microstructured substrates reduce adhesion and promote rebound by decreasing the liquid-solid contact area. In contrast to water, the emulsion exhibit enhanced deposition on smooth surfaces, increased sliding on uncoated microstructures, and earlier ligament formation and secondary breakup at high Weber numbers. The dispersed oil phase locally modifies wetting and lubrication, producing liquid bridges, heterogeneous recoil, and larger residual wetted areas on smooth substrates. On microstructured surfaces, however, capillary confinement within the texture limits the projected wetted area despite increased liquid retention inside the microstructure. These results demonstrate that the impact behaviour of emulsions cannot be predicted solely from conventional dimensionless groups, as local phase redistribution fundamentally alters energy dissipation, wetting, and fragmentation. The findings provide new insight into spray deposition on inclined textured surfaces relevant to agricultural spraying and coating technologies.

</details>


### [405] [Competing instabilities in wide-gap viscoelastic Taylor-Couette flow: Taylor vs. helical vortices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29552)
*Alexander Proskurin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文对同心圆柱间（内圆柱旋转、小半径）的聚合物溶液流动稳定性进行了数值研究，采用Oskolkov模型（Kelvin-Voigt模型的特例）描述流体运动，通过全非线性方法（有限持续时间白噪声扰动的直接数值模拟）研究稳态运动的稳定性，获得了与线性理论一致的临界雷诺数，并发现弹性力的增加使螺旋扰动比轴对称扰动更危险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究聚合物溶液在旋转内圆柱同心圆柱间流场的稳定性，特别是采用全非线性方法验证线性理论的预测，并探究弹性力对扰动模式（轴对称vs螺旋）危险性的影响。

Method: 采用Oskolkov模型（Kelvin-Voigt模型特例，适用于稀释聚合物溶液）描述流体运动；通过直接数值模拟对有限持续时间白噪声形式的扰动进行全非线性稳定性分析，考察不同雷诺数下扰动的衰减或增长。

Result: 牛顿流体和非牛顿流体获得的临界雷诺数与线性理论预测一致；弹性力的增加使得螺旋扰动比轴对称扰动更危险。

Conclusion: 全非线性数值模拟结果验证了线性稳定性理论的正确性，同时揭示了弹性力对扰动模式选择的重要影响——弹性力增强会导致螺旋模式成为更危险的失稳模态。

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical study of the stability of a polymer solution flow between concentric cylinders with a rotating inner cylinder. The case of a small-radius inner cylinder is considered. The fluid motion is described using a specific case of the Kelvin-Voigt model, often referred to as the Oskolkov model. This model is applicable to very dilute polymer solutions, where the retardation time is much smaller than the characteristic time of the problem and elastic forces are much smaller than viscous forces. The stability of the steady-state motion is investigated using a fully nonlinear approach by means of direct numerical simulation of a perturbation introduced as finite-duration white noise. Depending on the Reynolds number, the perturbation either decays or grows. The critical Reynolds numbers obtained for both Newtonian and non-Newtonian fluids are found to be in agreement with the predictions of the linear theory. It is also shown that an increase in the elastic forces makes helical perturbations more dangerous than their axisymmetric counterparts.

</details>


### [406] [Lagrangian particles and particle clusters in large-aspect-ratio turbulent Rayleigh-Bénard convection experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29729)
*Prafulla P. Shevkar,Mitanjali,Roshan J. Samuel,Christian Cierpka,Jörg Schumacher*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在宽高比为10、Ra=9.3×10^7、Pr=4.8的湍流瑞利-贝纳尔对流中进行拉格朗日粒子示踪实验，追踪约5万个中性浮力粒子长达150个科尔莫戈罗夫时间，分析了单粒子均方位移、速度结构函数、粒子对扩散、多粒子云扩散、加速度统计以及粒子连通性网络的谱聚类等动力学特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解湍流对流的拉格朗日动力学特性对于揭示热对流中的混合、输运及非各向同性弥散机制具有重要意义。此前缺乏在较长时间尺度上对湍流RB对流中单粒子和多粒子拉格朗日统计的系统研究，尤其是在大宽高比几何中。

Method: 在宽高比为10的湍流RB对流系统中进行约5万个中性浮力粒子的拉格朗日示踪实验，粒子追踪时间长达150个科尔莫戈罗夫时间。分别从单粒子均方位移（覆盖弹道区到扩散区）、拉格朗日速度结构函数、粒子对弥散、以及初始半径约8和20科尔莫戈罗夫长度的粒子团主成分分析等多方面展开统计。多粒子动力学通过图拉普拉斯谱分析结合k-means聚类进行识别，并统计三个加速度分量的概率密度函数及其二阶、四阶矩的收敛性。

Result: 粒子轨迹足够长以捕获从短时弹道区到长时扩散区的完整转变。速度结构函数和粒子对弥散在其各自时间区间内表现出与理论预期一致的标度行为。粒子对弥散指数在类Richardson区间内超过3，尤其在横向最为明显。粒子团在横向比垂直方向扩展更强，约10个科尔莫戈罗夫时间内由球形变形为扁椭球，显示出显著的各向异性弥散。横向加速度分量比垂直方向更具间歇性，尾部更重；受壁面附近高间歇性的羽流喷射与聚集事件影响，体区域条件化的加速度统计略弱于全测量体积内的统计。

Conclusion: 本研究系统揭示了湍流RB对流中拉格朗日运动的各向异性弥散特征：单粒子运动完整覆盖弹道区至扩散区，粒子对弥散在横向表现出超3指数的Richardson类标度，粒子团呈扁椭球变形，加速度横向分量间歇性更强，且壁面羽流事件显著增强间歇性。这些发现为理解大宽高比湍流热对流中的输运与混合机制提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: We perform Lagrangian particle-tracking experiments in turbulent Rayleigh-Bénard convection at aspect ratio Gamma = 10, Rayleigh number Ra = 9.3 x 10^7, and Prandtl number Pr = 4.8. Approximately 50,000 neutrally buoyant particles are tracked for durations of up to 150 Kolmogorov times. The resulting trajectories are sufficiently long to capture the mean-square displacement of single particles from the short-time ballistic regime to the long-time diffusive regime. We further analyse Lagrangian velocity structure functions and particle-pair dispersion, both of which exhibit scaling behavior consistent with theoretical expectations in their respective temporal regimes. In the Richardson-like regime, the pair-dispersion exponent exceeds 3, most clearly in the lateral direction. Multi-particle statistics are investigated by analysing particle clouds with initial radii of approximately 8 and 20 Kolmogorov lengths using principal component analysis. The clouds spread more strongly in the lateral directions than in the vertical direction and deform from initially spherical shapes into oblate ellipsoids within approximately 10 Kolmogorov times, revealing pronounced anisotropic dispersion. The dynamics is identified from the particle-connectivity network through a spectral analysis of the graph Laplacian followed by k-means clustering. Furthermore, we examine the probability density functions of all three acceleration components and assess the statistical convergence of their second and fourth moments. The lateral acceleration components are more intermittent than the vertical component, as indicated by their heavier tails. Acceleration statistics conditioned on the bulk region are slightly less intermittent than those obtained over the full measurement volume, which we attribute to the highly intermittent plume-ejection and plume-aggregation events occurring near the walls.

</details>


### [407] [Splashing-regime transitions and secondary-droplet scaling in oblique drop impacts on a deep pool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29762)
*Ying Xiong,Yang Zhang,Lingling Xie,Xiaolei Li*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过数值模拟研究了斜向液滴撞击深液池的行为，覆盖We数41至1790、倾角10°至90°范围，识别出五种撞击后形态（沉积、前向飞溅、侧向飞溅、侧前飞溅和冠部飞溅）。研究发现切向惯性参数K_s=We cosθ控制沉积-前向飞溅转变（临界值≈120），而法向韦伯数控制前向到侧前飞溅转变（临界值≈318）。次级液滴统计显示不同飞溅模式具有共同的毛细-惯性破碎标度，中位直径d~We^{-3/5}，液滴数N_s~We^{9/5}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜向液滴撞击深液池会产生不对称冠部、定向射流和飞溅转变，这些现象无法仅用总撞击惯性表征。此前研究缺乏对倾斜撞击在不同We数和倾角组合下飞溅模式转变的系统性理解，也缺乏对次级液滴统计规律的统一描述。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，在41≤We≤1790和10°≤θ≤90°参数范围内模拟水滴撞击静止深液池。通过We-θ平面上的系统仿真识别五种后撞击形态，利用切向惯性参数K_s和法向韦伯数We_N分析飞溅转变的物理机制，并通过次级液滴尺寸分布、中位直径标度关系和二阶速度结构函数描述破碎行为。

Result: 识别出五种撞击后形态及其在We-θ平面上的分布边界。沉积-前向飞溅转变由K_s=We cosθ控制（临界值≈120），源于下游冠部边缘惯性与Taylor-Culick速度的毛细回缩竞争；前向到侧前飞溅转变由法向韦伯数控制（临界值≈318）。次级液滴尺寸分布正偏态，中位直径标度为d~We^{-3/5}，二阶速度结构函数支持尺度依赖的毛细-惯性破碎描述，结合质量守恒导出液滴数量标度N_s~We^{9/5}。

Conclusion: 方向性撞击惯性决定飞溅形态的宏观选择，而不同飞溅模式下的次级液滴群体展现出统一的毛细-惯性破碎标度。该研究为倾斜液滴撞击深液池提供了统一的物理框架，将宏观形态转换与微观破碎统计联系起来。

Abstract: Oblique drop impact onto a deep liquid pool produces asymmetric crowns, directional jetting, and splashing transitions that cannot be characterized by the total impact inertia alone. We numerically investigate water drops impacting a quiescent deep pool over $41\leq We\leq1790$ and $10^\circ\leqθ\leq90^\circ$. The simulations reproduce the principal features observed experimentally and identify five post-impact regimes in the $We$--$θ$ plane: deposition, front splashing, side splashing, side-front splashing, and crown splashing. The deposition--front-splashing transition is described by the tangential-inertial parameter $K_s=We\cosθ$, with $K_s^c\approx120$. This criterion follows from the competition between downstream crown-rim inertia and capillary retraction at the Taylor--Culick velocity. The transition from front to side-front splashing is instead governed primarily by normal impact inertia, with a critical normal Weber number $We_N^c\approx318$. Beyond these regime transitions, the secondary-droplet statistics reveal fragmentation behavior common to the different splashing regimes. The droplet-size distributions are positively skewed, and the median diameter follows $d_{s,\mathrm{med}}/D\sim We^{-3/5}$. Second-order velocity structure functions support a scale-dependent capillary--inertial description of rim and ligament breakup. Combined with mass conservation, this scaling gives $N_s\sim We^{9/5}$, providing a numerical explanation for the secondary-droplet-number scaling observed experimentally. Thus, directional impact inertia governs the macroscopic selection of splashing regimes, whereas the secondary-droplet populations across these regimes exhibit a common capillary--inertial fragmentation scaling.

</details>


### [408] [Data-Driven Design Optimization of Streaming-Potential-Mediated Electrokinetic Transport of Viscoelastic Fluids in Microchannels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29939)
*Ankan Basu,Sumanta Banerjee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代理模型（surrogate-assisted）的计算框架，用于快速优化狭缝微通道中压力驱动的Phan-Thien-Tanner粘弹性流体电动传输（流势介导）的设计参数。通过高保真数值数据库训练机器学习代理模型预测流势，并结合多目标优化策略同时最大化能量转换效率和体积流量，大幅加速参数探索过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的解析和半解析模型虽能提供有价值的物理洞察，但在探索大规模设计空间和识别最优操作条件时需要反复进行数值计算，效率低下。工程上需要一种能够快速进行参数优化和设计探索的方法。

Method: 1) 生成覆盖广泛无量纲参数范围（zeta电位、Debye参数、Dukhin数、粘弹参数）的高保真数值数据库；2) 训练机器学习代理模型近似流势与参数之间的非线性关系；3) 利用代理预测的流势，通过闭式方程计算体积流量和水电能量转换效率；4) 耦合多目标优化策略，同时最大化能量转换效率和体积流量。

Result: 所提出的方法相较重复数值模拟能够显著加速参数探索，并成功识别出可同时最大化能量转换效率和体积流量的最优操作条件，为电动微流体器件提供了实用的设计指南。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了将计算流体力学与数据驱动的代理建模相结合在高效工程设计和优化中的潜力，为后续电动微流体传输系统的快速设计优化提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Streaming-potential-mediated transport of viscoelastic fluids has attracted research attention owing to its applications in electrokinetic energy conversion and microfluidic transport. Existing analytical and semi-analytical models in published literature provide valuable physical insights, but require repeated numerical evaluations for exploring large design spaces and identifying the optimal operating conditions. In this work, a surrogate-assisted framework is developed for rapid design optimization of pressure-driven electrokinetic transport of simplified Phan-Thien-Tanner fluids in a slit microchannel. A high-fidelity numerical database is generated over a broad range of governing dimensionless parameters, which includes the zeta potential, the Debye parameter, the Dukhin number, and the viscoelastic parameter. A Machine Learning surrogate model is subsequently trained to accurately approximate the nonlinear relationship between the governing parameters and the streaming potential, while the volumetric flow rate and hydroelectric energy conversion efficiency were calculated from closed form equation by using the streaming potential predicted by the surrogate. This is coupled with a multi-objective optimization strategy to identify operating conditions that simultaneously maximize energy conversion efficiency and volumetric flow rate. The proposed methodology can significantly accelerate parametric exploration compared with repeated numerical simulations across different parameters and provides practical design guidelines for electrokinetic microfluidic devices. The study demonstrates the potential of combining computational fluid mechanics with data-driven surrogate modeling for efficient engineering design and optimization.

</details>


### [409] [Coexisting chaos and order in micro-textured elastic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30286)
